`src/EagleEye/Core/Hashing.cs`:

```cs
using System.Security.Cryptography;

namespace EagleEye.Core;

public static class Hashing
{
    /// <summary>只读方式计算 SHA256，返回小写十六进制；失败返回 null 并给出原因。</summary>
    public static string? TrySha256(string path, out string? error)
    {
        error = null;
        try
        {
            using var fs = new FileStream(path, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.ReadWrite | FileShare.Delete, 1 << 20, FileOptions.SequentialScan);
            using var sha = SHA256.Create();
            byte[] hash = sha.ComputeHash(fs);
            return Convert.ToHexString(hash).ToLowerInvariant();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            error = ex.Message;
            return null;
        }
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Core/PatternMatcher.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace EagleEye.Core;

/// <summary>规则匹配：exact / glob / contains / regex，默认 glob，全部忽略大小写。</summary>
public static class PatternMatcher
{
    private static readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, Regex> Cache = new();

    public static bool IsMatch(string? input, string? pattern, string? matchType)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(pattern))
        {
            return false;
        }

        string mode = (matchType ?? "glob").Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
        switch (mode)
        {
            case "exact":
                return string.Equals(input, pattern, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            case "contains":
                return input.Contains(pattern, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            case "regex":
                return GetRegex("r|" + pattern, pattern).IsMatch(input);

            default:
                return GetRegex("g|" + pattern, GlobToRegex(pattern)).IsMatch(input);
        }
    }

    private static Regex GetRegex(string key, string expression)
    {
        return Cache.GetOrAdd(key, _ => new Regex(
            expression,
            RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | RegexOptions.CultureInvariant,
            TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(200)));
    }

    /// <summary>glob → 正则：* 匹配任意字符（含路径分隔符），? 匹配单个字符。</summary>
    public static string GlobToRegex(string glob)
    {
        var sb = new StringBuilder(glob.Length + 8);
        sb.Append('^');
        foreach (char c in glob)
        {
            switch (c)
            {
                case '*':
                    sb.Append(".*");
                    break;
                case '?':
                    sb.Append('.');
                    break;
                default:
                    sb.Append(Regex.Escape(c.ToString()));
                    break;
            }
        }
        sb.Append('$');
        return sb.ToString();
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Core/RiskAggregator.cs`:

```cs
using EagleEye.Models;

namespace EagleEye.Core;

/// <summary>证据链的一节：一条规则命中。</summary>
public sealed class EvidenceLink
{
    public string RuleId { get; set; } = "";
    public string RuleType { get; set; } = "";
    public string? Category { get; set; }
    public int Severity { get; set; }
    public double Confidence { get; set; }
    public double Score { get; set; }
    public string Description { get; set; } = "";
    public Dictionary<string, object?>? Evidence { get; set; }
}

/// <summary>针对单个目标（进程 / 文件 / 设备）的风险判定与证据链。</summary>
public sealed class TargetAssessment
{
    public string TargetKey { get; set; } = "";
    public string Target { get; set; } = "";
    public int? Pid { get; set; }
    public string? Path { get; set; }
    public string RiskLevel { get; set; } = RiskLevels.Info;
    public double Score { get; set; }
    public int StrongSignalCount { get; set; }
    public int MediumSignalCount { get; set; }
    public int WeakSignalCount { get; set; }
    /// <summary>等级是怎么升上去的（组合判定 / 强信号直判）。</summary>
    public List<string> EscalationReasons { get; set; } = new();
    public List<EvidenceLink> Evidence { get; set; } = new();
}

/// <summary>
/// 风险聚合：
///   1) 同一目标的多条规则命中累加（封顶 100）；
///   2) 强信号（severity>=4 且单条 >=70）直接判"高度可疑"；
///   3) 多个弱信号（severity<=2）叠加可升级为"可疑"——对应"组合信号"需求；
///   4) 最终等级取「分值等级」与「组合升级等级」中的较高者。
/// 被白名单抑制的命中不进入聚合。
/// </summary>
public static class RiskAggregator
{
    public static List<TargetAssessment> Assess(IEnumerable<Finding> findings)
    {
        var list = new List<TargetAssessment>();

        foreach (var group in findings.Where(f => !f.Suppressed).GroupBy(KeyOf))
        {
            var items = group.OrderByDescending(f => f.Score).ToList();
            var assessment = new TargetAssessment
            {
                TargetKey = group.Key,
                Target = items[0].Target,
                Pid = items[0].Pid,
                Path = items[0].TargetPath
            };

            foreach (var f in items)
            {
                assessment.Evidence.Add(new EvidenceLink
                {
                    RuleId = f.RuleId,
                    RuleType = f.RuleType,
                    Category = f.Category,
                    Severity = f.Severity,
                    Confidence = f.Confidence,
                    Score = f.Score,
                    Description = f.Description,
                    Evidence = f.Evidence
                });
            }

            assessment.Score = Math.Round(Math.Min(100.0, items.Sum(f => f.Score)), 2);
            assessment.StrongSignalCount = items.Count(f => f.Severity >= 4);
            assessment.MediumSignalCount = items.Count(f => f.Severity == 3);
            assessment.WeakSignalCount = items.Count(f => f.Severity <= 2);

            string byScore = Scoring.LevelOf(assessment.Score);
            string level = byScore;
            if (byScore != RiskLevels.Info)
            {
                assessment.EscalationReasons.Add($"累计得分 {assessment.Score} → {byScore}");
            }

            // 规则 2：强信号直判
            var strongest = items.FirstOrDefault(f => f.Severity >= 4 && f.Score >= Scoring.HighThreshold);
            if (strongest is not null && Scoring.Rank(RiskLevels.High) > Scoring.Rank(level))
            {
                level = RiskLevels.High;
                assessment.EscalationReasons.Add(
                    $"强信号直判：{strongest.RuleId}（severity {strongest.Severity}, 单条 {strongest.Score}）");
            }

            // 规则 3：弱信号组合升级
            if (Scoring.Rank(level) < Scoring.Rank(RiskLevels.Suspicious))
            {
                if (assessment.WeakSignalCount >= 3)
                {
                    level = RiskLevels.Suspicious;
                    assessment.EscalationReasons.Add(
                        $"组合升级：{assessment.WeakSignalCount} 个弱信号叠加（规则：弱信号≥3 视为可疑）");
                }
                else if (assessment.WeakSignalCount >= 2 && assessment.MediumSignalCount >= 1)
                {
                    level = RiskLevels.Suspicious;
                    assessment.EscalationReasons.Add(
                        $"组合升级：{assessment.WeakSignalCount} 弱 + {assessment.MediumSignalCount} 中（规则：弱≥2 且 中≥1 视为可疑）");
                }
                else if (assessment.MediumSignalCount >= 2)
                {
                    level = RiskLevels.Suspicious;
                    assessment.EscalationReasons.Add(
                        $"组合升级：{assessment.MediumSignalCount} 个中信号叠加");
                }
            }

            assessment.RiskLevel = level;
            list.Add(assessment);
        }

        return list
            .OrderByDescending(a => Scoring.Rank(a.RiskLevel))
            .ThenByDescending(a => a.Score)
            .ToList();
    }

    public static (double Score, string Level) Overall(IReadOnlyCollection<TargetAssessment> assessments)
    {
        if (assessments.Count == 0)
        {
            return (0, RiskLevels.Info);
        }

        double topScore = assessments.Max(a => a.Score);
        string level = RiskLevels.Info;
        foreach (var a in assessments)
        {
            level = Scoring.MaxLevel(level, a.RiskLevel);
        }

        return (Math.Round(topScore, 2), level);
    }

    private static string KeyOf(Finding f)
        => f.Pid.HasValue ? "pid:" + f.Pid.Value : "file:" + (f.TargetPath ?? f.Target);
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Core/Scoring.cs`:

```cs
using EagleEye.Models;

namespace EagleEye.Core;

public static class Scoring
{
    public const double SuspiciousThreshold = 40.0;
    public const double HighThreshold = 70.0;

    /// <summary>单条规则得分：severity(1-5) × 20 × confidence(0-1) × 分类权重。</summary>
    public static double ComputeScore(int severity, double confidence, double categoryWeight = 1.0)
    {
        double s = Math.Clamp(severity, 0, 5) * 20.0 * Math.Clamp(confidence, 0.0, 1.0) * Math.Clamp(categoryWeight, 0.0, 5.0);
        return Math.Round(s, 2);
    }

    public static string LevelOf(double score)
    {
        if (score >= HighThreshold) return RiskLevels.High;
        if (score >= SuspiciousThreshold) return RiskLevels.Suspicious;
        return RiskLevels.Info;
    }

    /// <summary>按分类 id 查权重；未定义分类返回 1.0。</summary>
    public static double WeightOf(RuleSet ruleSet, string? categoryId)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(categoryId))
        {
            return 1.0;
        }

        var category = ruleSet.Meta.Categories.FirstOrDefault(
            c => string.Equals(c.Id, categoryId, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

        return category is not null && category.Weight > 0 ? category.Weight : 1.0;
    }

    public static int Rank(string level) => level switch
    {
        RiskLevels.High => 2,
        RiskLevels.Suspicious => 1,
        _ => 0
    };

    public static string MaxLevel(params string[] levels)
        => levels.OrderByDescending(Rank).FirstOrDefault() ?? RiskLevels.Info;
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Core/WhitelistFilter.cs`:

```cs
using EagleEye.Models;
using EagleEye.Rules;

namespace EagleEye.Core;

/// <summary>
/// 统一的命中后置白名单过滤。
/// ProcessScanner / FileScanner 在生成命中时已经判过一次白名单；
/// 这里兜底处理句柄、驱动、overlay、特征码等"来自其它探测器"的命中，
/// 保证一条白名单对所有来源都生效（含 suppressRules 定向抑制）。
/// </summary>
public static class WhitelistFilter
{
    public static int Apply(RuleSet rules, IEnumerable<Finding> findings)
    {
        int suppressed = 0;

        foreach (var finding in findings)
        {
            if (finding.Suppressed) continue;
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(finding.Target)) continue;

            foreach (var entry in rules.Whitelist.Processes)
            {
                if (RuleLoader.IsExpired(entry)) continue;
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.Pattern)) continue;
                if (!PatternMatcher.IsMatch(finding.Target, entry.Pattern, entry.Match)) continue;

                bool applies = entry.SuppressRules is null || entry.SuppressRules.Length == 0 ||
                               entry.SuppressRules.Any(r => string.Equals(r, finding.RuleId, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

                if (!applies) continue;

                finding.Suppressed = true;
                finding.SuppressReason = entry.Reason ?? $"白名单: {entry.Pattern}";
                finding.SuppressedBy = "whitelist";
                suppressed++;
                break;
            }
        }

        return suppressed;
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Kernel/ConfigSpaceProvider.cs`:

```cs
using EagleEye.Models;

namespace EagleEye.Kernel;

/// <summary>
/// 一次性读取流程：装载驱动 → 逐设备 IOCTL → 立即停止并删除服务。
/// 卸载放在 finally，任何异常都不会留下驱动服务。
/// </summary>
public static class ConfigSpaceProvider
{
    public static ConfigSpaceReadResult ReadAll(
        List<DmaDeviceInfo> devices,
        string driverPath,
        string serviceName,
        string devicePath)
    {
        var result = new ConfigSpaceReadResult { Attempted = true, DriverPath = driverPath };
        var host = new PcieCfgDriverHost(serviceName, devicePath);
        Microsoft.Win32.SafeHandles.SafeFileHandle? device = null;

        try
        {
            if (!host.EnsureLoaded(driverPath, out string? loadError))
            {
                result.Error = loadError;
                return result;
            }

            result.Loaded = true;

            device = host.OpenDevice(out string? openError);
            if (device is null)
            {
                result.Error = openError;
                return result;
            }

            if (!PcieCfgIoctl.TryPing(device, out uint version, out string? pingError))
            {
                result.Error = pingError;
                return result;
            }

            if (version != 1)
            {
                result.Error = $"驱动协议版本不匹配（驱动 {version}，客户端 1）";
                return result;
            }

            foreach (var info in devices)
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(info.PdoName))
                {
                    result.Errors.Add($"{info.Name}: 缺少 PDO 名称，跳过");
                    continue;
                }

                result.DevicesAttempted++;

                if (PcieCfgIoctl.TryReadConfig(device, info.PdoName!, out var raw, out string? readError))
                {
                    if (raw.Status == 0)
                    {
                        info.ConfigSpace = ConfigSpaceInfo.From(raw);
                        result.DevicesRead++;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        result.Errors.Add($"{info.Name}: 驱动返回 0x{raw.Status:X8}");
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    result.Errors.Add($"{info.Name}: {readError}");
                }
            }

            return result;
        }
        finally
        {
            device?.Dispose();

            if (result.Loaded)
            {
                result.Unloaded = host.TryUnload(out string? unloadError);
                if (!result.Unloaded && result.Error is null)
                {
                    result.Error = unloadError;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Kernel/PcieCfgDriverHost.cs`:

```cs
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using Microsoft.Win32.SafeHandles;

namespace EagleEye.Kernel;

/// <summary>
/// 驱动装载/卸载：CreateService(DEMAND_START) → StartService → (调用方读数据) → ControlService(STOP) → DeleteService。
/// 驱动服务名固定，启动类型固定为按需启动，绝不会注册成自启动。
/// </summary>
public sealed class PcieCfgDriverHost
{
    private const uint ScManagerAllAccess = 0xF003F;
    private const uint ServiceAllAccess = 0xF01FF;
    private const uint ServiceKernelDriver = 0x00000001;
    private const uint ServiceDemandStart = 0x00000003;
    private const uint ServiceErrorNormal = 0x00000001;
    private const uint ServiceControlStop = 0x00000001;

    private const uint ServiceStopped = 0x00000001;
    private const uint ServiceRunning = 0x00000004;

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
    private struct SERVICE_STATUS
    {
        public uint dwServiceType;
        public uint dwCurrentState;
        public uint dwControlsAccepted;
        public uint dwWin32ExitCode;
        public uint dwServiceSpecificExitCode;
        public uint dwCheckPoint;
        public uint dwWaitHint;
    }

    [DllImport("advapi32.dll", SetLastError = true, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
    private static extern IntPtr OpenSCManager(string? machineName, string? databaseName, uint desiredAccess);

    [DllImport("advapi32.dll", SetLastError = true, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
    private static extern IntPtr CreateService(
        IntPtr hSCManager,
        string lpServiceName,
        string? lpDisplayName,
        uint dwDesiredAccess,
        uint dwServiceType,
        uint dwStartType,
        uint dwErrorControl,
        string lpBinaryPathName,
        string? lpLoadOrderGroup,
        IntPtr lpdwTagId,
        string? lpDependencies,
        string? lpServiceStartName,
        string? lpPassword);

    [DllImport("advapi32.dll", SetLastError = true, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
    private static extern IntPtr OpenService(IntPtr hSCManager, string lpServiceName, uint dwDesiredAccess);

    [DllImport("advapi32.dll", SetLastError = true, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool StartService(IntPtr hService, uint dwNumServiceArgs, IntPtr lpServiceArgVectors);

    [DllImport("advapi32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool ControlService(IntPtr hService, uint dwControl, ref SERVICE_STATUS lpServiceStatus);

    [DllImport("advapi32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool DeleteService(IntPtr hService);

    [DllImport("advapi32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool QueryServiceStatus(IntPtr hService, ref SERVICE_STATUS lpServiceStatus);

    [DllImport("advapi32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool CloseServiceHandle(IntPtr hSCObject);

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
    private static extern SafeFileHandle CreateFileW(
        string lpFileName,
        uint dwDesiredAccess,
        uint dwShareMode,
        IntPtr lpSecurityAttributes,
        uint dwCreationDisposition,
        uint dwFlagsAndAttributes,
        IntPtr hTemplateFile);

    public string ServiceName { get; }
    public string DevicePath { get; }

    public PcieCfgDriverHost(string serviceName = "PcieCfg", string devicePath = @"\\.\PcieCfg")
    {
        ServiceName = serviceName;
        DevicePath = devicePath;
    }

    /// <summary>安装并启动驱动。已存在同名服务时先删除（清理上次异常退出的残留）。</summary>
    public bool EnsureLoaded(string driverPath, out string? error)
    {
        error = null;

        if (!File.Exists(driverPath))
        {
            error = $"驱动文件不存在: {driverPath}";
            return false;
        }

        IntPtr scm = OpenSCManager(null, null, ScManagerAllAccess);
        if (scm == IntPtr.Zero)
        {
            error = Win32("OpenSCManager", out _);
            return false;
        }

        try
        {
            // 清理残留
            IntPtr stale = OpenService(scm, ServiceName, ServiceAllAccess);
            if (stale != IntPtr.Zero)
            {
                var staleStatus = default(SERVICE_STATUS);
                ControlService(stale, ServiceControlStop, ref staleStatus);
                DeleteService(stale);
                CloseServiceHandle(stale);
                Thread.Sleep(300);
            }

            IntPtr service = CreateService(
                scm,
                ServiceName,
                ServiceName,
                ServiceAllAccess,
                ServiceKernelDriver,
                ServiceDemandStart,          // 关键：按需启动，绝不设成自启动
                ServiceErrorNormal,
                driverPath,
                null,
                IntPtr.Zero,
                null,
                null,
                null);

            if (service == IntPtr.Zero)
            {
                error = Win32("CreateService", out int code);
                if (code == 5)
                {
                    error += "（需要以管理员身份运行）";
                }
                return false;
            }

            try
            {
                if (!StartService(service, 0, IntPtr.Zero))
                {
                    int startError = Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
                    // 1056 = 服务已在运行，视为成功
                    if (startError != 1056)
                    {
                        error = $"StartService 失败（Win32={startError} {new Win32Exception(startError).Message}）";
                        if (startError == 577)
                        {
                            error += " —— 驱动签名不被信任：正式使用需 EV 签名 + Microsoft attestation，测试机需 testsigning";
                        }
                        else if (startError == 1275)
                        {
                            error += " —— 驱动被系统策略/HVCI 阻止加载";
                        }

                        DeleteService(service);
                        return false;
                    }
                }

                for (int i = 0; i < 50; i++)
                {
                    var status = default(SERVICE_STATUS);
                    if (QueryServiceStatus(service, ref status) && status.dwCurrentState == ServiceRunning)
                    {
                        return true;
                    }

                    Thread.Sleep(100);
                }

                error = "驱动启动后未进入 RUNNING 状态（超时 5s）";
                return false;
            }
            finally
            {
                CloseServiceHandle(service);
            }
        }
        finally
        {
            CloseServiceHandle(scm);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>停止并删除服务。必须放在 finally 里，保证用完即卸。</summary>
    public bool TryUnload(out string? error)
    {
        error = null;

        IntPtr scm = OpenSCManager(null, null, ScManagerAllAccess);
        if (scm == IntPtr.Zero)
        {
            error = Win32("OpenSCManager", out _);
            return false;
        }

        try
        {
            IntPtr service = OpenService(scm, ServiceName, ServiceAllAccess);
            if (service == IntPtr.Zero)
            {
                int code = Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
                if (code == 1060) // 服务不存在，视为已卸载
                {
                    return true;
                }

                error = Win32("OpenService", out _);
                return false;
            }

            try
            {
                var stopStatus = default(SERVICE_STATUS);
                ControlService(service, ServiceControlStop, ref stopStatus);

                for (int i = 0; i < 50; i++)
                {
                    var status = default(SERVICE_STATUS);
                    if (QueryServiceStatus(service, ref status) && status.dwCurrentState == ServiceStopped)
                    {
                        break;
                    }

                    Thread.Sleep(100);
                }

                if (!DeleteService(service))
                {
                    int code = Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
                    if (code != 1072) // ERROR_SERVICE_MARKED_FOR_DELETE
                    {
                        error = Win32("DeleteService", out _);
                        return false;
                    }
                }

                return true;
            }
            finally
            {
                CloseServiceHandle(service);
            }
        }
        finally
        {
            CloseServiceHandle(scm);
        }
    }

    public SafeFileHandle? OpenDevice(out string? error)
    {
        const uint genericRead = 0x80000000;
        const uint shareReadWrite = 0x00000003;
        const uint openExisting = 3;
        const uint fileAttributeNormal = 0x80;

        SafeFileHandle handle = CreateFileW(DevicePath, genericRead, shareReadWrite, IntPtr.Zero, openExisting, fileAttributeNormal, IntPtr.Zero);
        if (handle.IsInvalid)
        {
            int code = Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
            handle.Dispose();
            error = $"打开设备 {DevicePath} 失败（Win32={code} {new Win32Exception(code).Message}）";
            return null;
        }

        error = null;
        return handle;
    }

    private static string Win32(string api, out int code)
    {
        code = Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
        return $"{api} 失败（Win32={code} {new Win32Exception(code).Message}）";
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Kernel/PcieCfgIoctl.cs`:

```cs
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using Microsoft.Win32.SafeHandles;

namespace EagleEye.Kernel;

/// <summary>
/// 与 PcieCfg.sys 的 IOCTL 契约。结构体布局必须与驱动里的 PCIECFG_REQUEST / PCIECFG_RESULT 完全一致。
/// </summary>
public static class PcieCfgIoctl
{
    /// <summary>CTL_CODE(0x8000, 0x800, METHOD_BUFFERED, FILE_READ_DATA)</summary>
    public const uint IoctlPing = 0x80002001;

    /// <summary>CTL_CODE(0x8000, 0x801, METHOD_BUFFERED, FILE_READ_DATA)</summary>
    public const uint IoctlReadConfig = 0x80002005;

    public const int MaxPath = 260;
    public const int ConfigLength = 256;
    public const int MaxCapabilities = 48;

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
    public struct PcieCfgRequest
    {
        [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValTStr, SizeConst = MaxPath)]
        public string PdoPath;

        public uint Offset;
        public uint Length;
    }

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
    public struct PcieCfgResult
    {
        public uint Status;
        public uint Ids;
        public uint Subsystem;
        public uint ClassInfo;
        public uint HeaderType;

        [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValArray, SizeConst = 6)]
        public uint[] Bar;

        [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValArray, SizeConst = 6)]
        public uint[] BarFlags;

        public uint CapCount;

        [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValArray, SizeConst = MaxCapabilities)]
        public byte[] CapIds;

        public byte HasMsi;
        public byte HasMsix;
        public byte CapabilityListOffset;
        public byte Reserved0;
        public uint MsiControl;

        [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValArray, SizeConst = ConfigLength)]
        public byte[] Raw;
    }

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool DeviceIoControl(
        SafeFileHandle hDevice,
        uint dwIoControlCode,
        IntPtr lpInBuffer,
        uint nInBufferSize,
        IntPtr lpOutBuffer,
        uint nOutBufferSize,
        out uint lpBytesReturned,
        IntPtr lpOverlapped);

    public static bool TryPing(SafeFileHandle device, out uint version, out string? error)
    {
        version = 0;
        error = null;

        IntPtr outBuffer = Marshal.AllocHGlobal(sizeof(uint));
        try
        {
            if (!DeviceIoControl(device, IoctlPing, IntPtr.Zero, 0, outBuffer, sizeof(uint), out uint returned, IntPtr.Zero) || returned < sizeof(uint))
            {
                error = $"IOCTL_PCIECFG_PING 失败（Win32={Marshal.GetLastWin32Error()}）";
                return false;
            }

            version = (uint)Marshal.ReadInt32(outBuffer);
            return true;
        }
        finally
        {
            Marshal.FreeHGlobal(outBuffer);
        }
    }

    public static bool TryReadConfig(SafeFileHandle device, string pdoPath, out PcieCfgResult result, out string? error)
    {
        result = default;
        error = null;

        var request = new PcieCfgRequest { PdoPath = pdoPath, Offset = 0, Length = ConfigLength };
        int inSize = Marshal.SizeOf<PcieCfgRequest>();
        int outSize = Marshal.SizeOf<PcieCfgResult>();

        IntPtr inBuffer = Marshal.AllocHGlobal(inSize);
        IntPtr outBuffer = Marshal.AllocHGlobal(outSize);

        try
        {
            Marshal.StructureToPtr(request, inBuffer, false);
            RtlZeroMemory(outBuffer, outSize);

            bool ok = DeviceIoControl(device, IoctlReadConfig, inBuffer, (uint)inSize, outBuffer, (uint)outSize, out uint returned, IntPtr.Zero);

            if (!ok)
            {
                error = $"IOCTL_PCIECFG_READ_CONFIG 失败（Win32={Marshal.GetLastWin32Error()}）";
                return false;
            }

            if (returned < outSize)
            {
                error = $"驱动返回长度不足（{returned}/{outSize}）";
                return false;
            }

            result = Marshal.PtrToStructure<PcieCfgResult>(outBuffer)!;
            return true;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            error = ex.Message;
            return false;
        }
        finally
        {
            Marshal.FreeHGlobal(inBuffer);
            Marshal.FreeHGlobal(outBuffer);
        }
    }

    private static void RtlZeroMemory(IntPtr buffer, int size)
    {
        byte[] zeros = new byte[size];
        Marshal.Copy(zeros, 0, buffer, size);
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Models/ConfigSpaceModels.cs`:

```cs
using EagleEye.Kernel;

namespace EagleEye.Models;

/// <summary>
/// 由内核 IOCTL 返回的真实 PCIe 配置空间解析结果。
/// 与用户态近似值不同：这里的 VID/DID/Revision/ClassCode/Subsystem/Capability 都是硬件真值，
/// 但 BAR 只有原始寄存器值 —— 确定 BAR 大小需要写 0xFFFFFFFF 回读，本工具禁止写配置空间。
/// </summary>
public sealed class ConfigSpaceInfo
{
    public ushort VendorId { get; set; }
    public ushort DeviceId { get; set; }
    public ushort SubsystemVendorId { get; set; }
    public ushort SubsystemId { get; set; }
    public byte RevisionId { get; set; }
    public byte BaseClass { get; set; }
    public byte SubClass { get; set; }
    public byte ProgIf { get; set; }
    public byte HeaderType { get; set; }
    public byte[] BarRaw { get; set; } = Array.Empty<uint>().Select(_ => (byte)0).ToArray();
    public uint[] Bars { get; set; } = new uint[6];
    public string[] BarKinds { get; set; } = new string[6];
    public byte[] CapabilityIds { get; set; } = Array.Empty<byte>();
    public bool HasMsi { get; set; }
    public bool HasMsiX { get; set; }
    public ushort MsiControl { get; set; }
    public byte CapabilityListOffset { get; set; }
    /// <summary>恒为 false：只读实现无法确定 BAR 大小。</summary>
    public bool BarSizeDeterminable { get; set; }
    /// <summary>原始配置空间前 64 字节的十六进制串，作为证据留痕。</summary>
    public string RawHex { get; set; } = "";

    public string ClassCodeHex => $"{BaseClass:X2}{SubClass:X2}";

    public static ConfigSpaceInfo From(PcieCfgIoctl.PcieCfgResult raw)
    {
        var info = new ConfigSpaceInfo
        {
            VendorId = (ushort)(raw.Ids & 0xFFFF),
            DeviceId = (ushort)(raw.Ids >> 16),
            SubsystemVendorId = (ushort)(raw.Subsystem & 0xFFFF),
            SubsystemId = (ushort)(raw.Subsystem >> 16),
            BaseClass = (byte)(raw.ClassInfo >> 24),
            SubClass = (byte)(raw.ClassInfo >> 16),
            ProgIf = (byte)(raw.ClassInfo >> 8),
            RevisionId = (byte)raw.ClassInfo,
            HeaderType = (byte)(raw.HeaderType & 0x7F),
            HasMsi = raw.HasMsi != 0,
            HasMsiX = raw.HasMsix != 0,
            MsiControl = (ushort)raw.MsiControl,
            CapabilityListOffset = raw.CapabilityListOffset,
            BarSizeDeterminable = false
        };

        info.Bars = raw.Bar ?? new uint[6];
        info.CapabilityIds = (raw.CapIds ?? Array.Empty<byte>()).Take((int)Math.Min(raw.CapCount, 48)).ToArray();

        for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++)
        {
            uint bar = info.Bars[i];
            uint flags = raw.BarFlags is null ? 0 : raw.BarFlags[i];

            if ((flags & 0x8) == 0)
            {
                info.BarKinds[i] = "none";
            }
            else if ((flags & 0x1) != 0)
            {
                info.BarKinds[i] = "io";
            }
            else if ((flags & 0x2) != 0)
            {
                info.BarKinds[i] = "mem64";
            }
            else
            {
                info.BarKinds[i] = "mem32";
            }
        }

        if (raw.Raw is not null)
        {
            int take = Math.Min(64, raw.Raw.Length);
            info.RawHex = Convert.ToHexString(raw.Raw, 0, take).ToLowerInvariant();
        }

        return info;
    }
}

/// <summary>内核配置空间读取的总体结果（含装载/卸载状态，便于审计）。</summary>
public sealed class ConfigSpaceReadResult
{
    public bool Attempted { get; set; }
    public bool Loaded { get; set; }
    public bool Unloaded { get; set; }
    public string? DriverPath { get; set; }
    public string? Error { get; set; }
    public int DevicesAttempted { get; set; }
    public int DevicesRead { get; set; }
    public List<string> Errors { get; set; } = new();
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Models/DeepModels.cs`:

```cs
namespace EagleEye.Models;

/// <summary>句柄线索规则：谁以高权限打开了目标进程。</summary>
public sealed class HandleRule
{
    public string Id { get; set; } = "";
    public string? Category { get; set; } = "handle";
    /// <summary>目标进程名模式（glob），如 "*game*"、"*"。</summary>
    public string? TargetProcessPattern { get; set; }
    /// <summary>命中哪些访问权限即算一条，十六进制字符串。</summary>
    public string[]? AccessMaskAny { get; set; }
    public int Severity { get; set; } = 3;
    public double Confidence { get; set; } = 0.5;
    public string? Description { get; set; }
    public string? Advice { get; set; }
    public bool Enabled { get; set; } = true;
}

public sealed class HandleScanOptions
{
    public bool Enabled { get; set; } = true;
    public List<HandleRule> Rules { get; set; } = new();
    /// <summary>持有者白名单（进程名 glob），如系统组件、杀软。</summary>
    public List<string> HolderWhitelist { get; set; } = new();
    /// <summary>目标白名单（进程名 glob）。</summary>
    public List<string> TargetWhitelist { get; set; } = new();
}

/// <summary>驱动线索规则。</summary>
public sealed class DriverRule
{
    public string Id { get; set; } = "";
    public string? Category { get; set; } = "driver";
    /// <summary>驱动文件名 glob，如 "dbk64.sys"。</summary>
    public string? Pattern { get; set; }
    public string? Sha256 { get; set; }
    public int Severity { get; set; } = 4;
    public double Confidence { get; set; } = 0.7;
    public string? Description { get; set; }
    public string? Advice { get; set; }
    public bool Enabled { get; set; } = true;
}

public sealed class DriverScanOptions
{
    public bool Enabled { get; set; } = true;
    public List<DriverRule> Rules { get; set; } = new();

    /// <summary>白名单模式：不在规则里、又不是受信任签名的驱动，单独上报（无样本也能查）。</summary>
    public bool WhitelistMode { get; set; } = true;
    public List<string> TrustedSigners { get; set; } = new();
    public int UntrustedSeverity { get; set; } = 3;
    public double UntrustedConfidence { get; set; } = 0.45;
    /// <summary>对这些路径前缀之外的驱动做验签（默认只验规则命中项）。</summary>
    public List<string> SignatureWhitelistPaths { get; set; } = new();
}

public sealed class OverlayScanOptions
{
    public bool Enabled { get; set; } = true;
    /// <summary>overlay 面积 / 被覆盖窗口面积 的最小比例。</summary>
    public double MinAreaRatio { get; set; } = 0.4;
    public int MinWidth { get; set; } = 320;
    public int MinHeight { get; set; } = 240;
    public int Severity { get; set; } = 2;
    public double Confidence { get; set; } = 0.45;
}

/// <summary>文件特征码规则（只读前 N 字节做字节/ASCII 匹配）。</summary>
public sealed class FileSignatureRule
{
    public string Id { get; set; } = "";
    /// <summary>ascii | hex</summary>
    public string PatternType { get; set; } = "ascii";
    public string Pattern { get; set; } = "";
    public int Severity { get; set; } = 4;
    public double Confidence { get; set; } = 0.6;
    public string? Description { get; set; }
    public bool Enabled { get; set; } = true;
}

public sealed class FileSignatureOptions

    public bool Enabled { get; set; }
    public int MaxFiles { get; set; } = 2000;
    public int MaxBytesPerFile { get; set; } = 2097152;
    public List<string> IncludeExtensions { get; set; } = new() { ".dll", ".exe", ".sys" };
    public List<FileSignatureRule> Rules { get; set; } = new();
```

`src/EagleEye/Models/DmaModels.cs`:

```cs
namespace EagleEye.Models;

/// <summary>
/// DMA 外设规则。
/// 注意：用户态部分只能与"由 SetupAPI 设备类近似推导的 PCI 基础类"比对 classCode；
/// 启用 --kernel 后才会用配置空间里的真实 Class Code 覆盖这一判断。
/// conditions 只在拿到配置空间数据时生效。
/// </summary>
public sealed class DmaRule
{
    public string Id { get; set; } = "";
    /// <summary>厂商 ID，4 位十六进制，可带 0x 前缀，如 "10EE"。</summary>
    public string? Vid { get; set; }
    /// <summary>设备 ID，可选；不填表示该厂商下所有设备。</summary>
    public string? Pid { get; set; }
    /// <summary>修订号，可选，如 "01"。</summary>
    public string? Revision { get; set; }
    /// <summary>PCI 基础类，可选，如 "06"。</summary>
    public string? ClassCode { get; set; }
    /// <summary>规则分类 id，对应 meta.categories[].id。</summary>
    public string? Category { get; set; }
    public string? Vendor { get; set; }
    public int Severity { get; set; } = 3;
    public double Confidence { get; set; } = 0.5;
    public string? Description { get; set; }
    public string? Advice { get; set; }
    public bool Enabled { get; set; } = true;

    /// <summary>
    /// 附加条件（需要内核配置空间数据才生效）：
    /// msi_missing / msix_missing / no_capability_list / bar0_zero / class_ff / revision_zero
    /// </summary>
    public string[]? Conditions { get; set; }
}

public sealed class DmaWhitelistEntry
{
    public string? Vid { get; set; }
    public string? Pid { get; set; }
    public string? Reason { get; set; }
    public string? Expires { get; set; }
}

public sealed class DmaKernelOptions
{
    /// <summary>默认 false：必须在命令行显式 --kernel 才会加载驱动。</summary>
    public bool Enabled { get; set; }
    public string ServiceName { get; set; } = "PcieCfg";
    public string DevicePath { get; set; } = "\\\\.\\PcieCfg";
    public string DriverFileName { get; set; } = "PcieCfg.sys";
    public int TimeoutMs { get; set; } = 5000;
}

public sealed class DmaSection
{
    public bool Enabled { get; set; } = true;
    /// <summary>是否把枚举到的全部 PCI 设备写进报告（排查用，默认 true）。</summary>
    public bool IncludeDeviceList { get; set; } = true;
    public List<DmaRule> Rules { get; set; } = new();
    public List<DmaWhitelistEntry> Whitelist { get; set; } = new();
    /// <summary>内核只读配置空间枚举（一次性装载，用完即卸）。</summary>
    public DmaKernelOptions Kernel { get; set; } = new();
}

public sealed class DmaDeviceInfo
{
    public string Name { get; set; } = "";
    public string InstanceId { get; set; } = "";
    public string? VendorId { get; set; }
    public string? DeviceId { get; set; }
    public string? SubsystemId { get; set; }
    public string? Revision { get; set; }
    /// <summary>SetupAPI 设备类名，如 Display / Net / System。</summary>
    public string? DeviceClass { get; set; }
    /// <summary>由设备类近似推导的 PCI 基础类（2 位十六进制），非配置空间真实值。</summary>
    public string? ClassCodeGuess { get; set; }
    public string? Service { get; set; }
    public string? Location { get; set; }
    public string? Manufacturer { get; set; }
    public uint? ProblemCode { get; set; }
    /// <summary>PDO 设备对象名（如 \Device\NTPNP_PCI0007），内核按它发 IRP_MN_READ_CONFIG。</summary>
    public string? PdoName { get; set; }
    /// <summary>内核读到的真实配置空间；未启用 --kernel 时为 null。</summary>
    public ConfigSpaceInfo? ConfigSpace { get; set; }
}

public sealed class DmaFinding
{
    public string RuleId { get; set; } = "";
    public string Target { get; set; } = "";
    public string InstanceId { get; set; } = "";
    public string? VendorId { get; set; }
    public string? DeviceId { get; set; }
    public string? Revision { get; set; }
    public string? ClassCodeGuess { get; set; }
    public int Severity { get; set; }
    public double Confidence { get; set; }
    public double Score { get; set; }
    public string RiskLevel { get; set; } = RiskLevels.Info;
    public string Description { get; set; } = "";
    public string? Advice { get; set; }
    public string? Category { get; set; }
    /// <summary>被白名单抑制：不参与聚合，但保留记录。</summary>
    public bool Suppressed { get; set; }
    public string? SuppressReason { get; set; }
    public string? SuppressedBy { get; set; }
    /// <summary>恒为 true：提醒这是用户态近似结果。</summary>
    public bool UserModeOnly { get; set; } = true;
    /// <summary>本条命中是否已经用真实配置空间数据校验过。</summary>
    public bool ConfigSpaceVerified { get; set; }
    public Dictionary<string, object?> Evidence { get; set; } = new();
}

public sealed class DmaReport
{
    public bool Enabled { get; set; }
    public bool UserModeOnly { get; set; } = true;
    public string Note { get; set; } =
        "用户态枚举无法读取 PCIe 配置空间（Vendor/Device/Revision/Class/Capability/BAR 均不可靠），" +
        "本段结果仅为线索，不能作为 DMA 检测的唯一依据；确认需要人工复核或内核辅助。";
    public int DevicesEnumerated { get; set; }
    public int PciDevices { get; set; }
    public int Findings { get; set; }
    public string RiskLevel { get; set; } = RiskLevels.Info;
    public double TopScore { get; set; }
    public List<DmaDeviceInfo> Devices { get; set; } = new();
    public DmaKernelReport Kernel { get; set; } = new();
}

public sealed class DmaKernelReport
{
    public bool Attempted { get; set; }
    public bool Loaded { get; set; }
    public bool Unloaded { get; set; }
    public string? DriverPath { get; set; }
    public string? Error { get; set; }
    public int DevicesAttempted { get; set; }
    public int DevicesRead { get; set; }
    public List<string> Errors { get; set; } = new();
    public string Note { get; set; } =
        "驱动只在扫描期间存在：加载 → 读配置空间 → 立即停止并删除服务；不做内存/进程/文件/网络/提权任何操作。" +
        "不要在 Vanguard / EAC / BattlEye 等反作弊活跃期间加载，建议先扫描后启动游戏。";
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Models/Finding.cs`:

```cs
namespace EagleEye.Models;

public sealed class Finding
{
    public string RuleId { get; set; } = "";
    public string RuleType { get; set; } = "";
    /// <summary>规则分类（对应 meta.categories[].id）。</summary>
    public string? Category { get; set; }
    public string Target { get; set; } = "";
    public string? TargetPath { get; set; }
    public int? Pid { get; set; }
    public int Severity { get; set; }
    public double Confidence { get; set; }
    public double Score { get; set; }
    /// <summary>信息 / 可疑 / 高度可疑</summary>
    public string RiskLevel { get; set; } = RiskLevels.Info;
    public string Description { get; set; } = "";
    public string? Advice { get; set; }
    public Dictionary<string, object?> Evidence { get; set; } = new();

    /// <summary>被白名单抑制：不参与风险聚合，但保留记录以便追溯。</summary>
    public bool Suppressed { get; set; }
    public string? SuppressReason { get; set; }
    /// <summary>抑制来源（白名单条目原文）。</summary>
    public string? SuppressedBy { get; set; }
}

public static class RiskLevels
{
    public const string Info = "信息";
    public const string Suspicious = "可疑";
    public const string High = "高度可疑";
}

public sealed class NotChecked
{
    public string Target { get; set; } = "";
    public string? Path { get; set; }
    public int? Pid { get; set; }
    public string Reason { get; set; } = "";
}
```

```cs
public sealed class SignatureInfo

    public string State { get; set; } = SignatureStates.Unavailable;

    public const string CatalogValid = "catalog_valid";
    public const string Unsigned = "unsigned";
    public const string Expired = "expired";
    public const string Revoked = "revoked";
    public const string UntrustedRoot = "untrusted_root";
    public const string InvalidChain = "invalid_chain";
    public const string BadDigest = "bad_digest";
    public const string Distrusted = "distrusted";
    public const string Error = "error";
    public const string Unavailable = "unavailable";
}
```

```cs
namespace EagleEye.Models;

public static class RuleTypes
{
    public const string ProcessName = "process_name";
    public const string ProcessPath = "process_path";
    public const string ProcessCommandLine = "process_command_line";
    public const string ModuleName = "module_name";
    public const string FileName = "file_name";
    public const string FileHash = "file_hash";
    public const string Signature = "signature";
}

public static class SignaturePatterns
{
    public const string Unsigned = "unsigned";
    public const string BadDigest = "bad_digest";
    public const string UntrustedRoot = "untrusted_root";
    public const string Expired = "expired";
    public const string Revoked = "revoked";
    public const string CatalogOnly = "catalog_only";
    public const string AnyInvalid = "any_invalid";
}

/// <summary>规则分类：用于报告分组、统计与权重。</summary>
public sealed class RuleCategory
{
    public string Id { get; set; } = "";
    public string Name { get; set; } = "";
    public string? Description { get; set; }
    /// <summary>分类权重，乘进单条得分；默认 1.0。</summary>
    public double Weight { get; set; } = 1.0;
}

public sealed class RuleMeta
{
    public string Name { get; set; } = "";
    /// <summary>规则内容版本，如 1.3.0。</summary>
    public string Version { get; set; } = "1.0.0";
    /// <summary>规则内容更新时间，ISO 日期或 ISO 8601。</summary>
    public string? Updated { get; set; }
    /// <summary>格式版本，用于兼容性判断。</summary>
    public string SchemaVersion { get; set; } = "2.0";
    public string? Author { get; set; }
    public string? Description { get; set; }
    public List<RuleCategory> Categories { get; set; } = new();
}

public sealed class Rule
{
    public string Id { get; set; } = "";
    public string Type { get; set; } = "";
    /// <summary>规则分类 id，对应 meta.categories[].id。</summary>
    public string? Category { get; set; }
    public string? Pattern { get; set; }
    public int Severity { get; set; } = 3;
    public double Confidence { get; set; } = 0.7;
    public string? Description { get; set; }
    /// <summary>exact | glob | contains | regex，默认 glob。</summary>
    public string? Match { get; set; }
    /// <summary>file_hash 专用：期望的 SHA256 十六进制。</summary>
    public string? Hash { get; set; }
    /// <summary>file_hash 可选预筛：文件大小（字节）。</summary>
    public long? Size { get; set; }
    public bool Enabled { get; set; } = true;
    public string? Advice { get; set; }
    public string[]? Tags { get; set; }
}

public sealed class WhitelistEntry
{
    public string? Pattern { get; set; }
    public string? Hash { get; set; }
    public string? SubjectContains { get; set; }
    public string? Match { get; set; }
    public string? Reason { get; set; }
    public string? Expires { get; set; }
    /// <summary>可选：只抑制这些规则 id；为空表示抑制该目标上全部规则命中。</summary>
    public string[]? SuppressRules { get; set; }
}

public sealed class Whitelist
{
    public List<WhitelistEntry> Processes { get; set; } = new();
    public List<WhitelistEntry> Paths { get; set; } = new();
    public List<WhitelistEntry> Files { get; set; } = new();
    public List<WhitelistEntry> Signers { get; set; } = new();
}

public sealed class ScanOptionsConfig
{
    public List<string> ScanRoots { get; set; } = new();
    public List<string> IncludeExtensions { get; set; } = new() { ".exe", ".dll", ".sys" };
    public int MaxFiles { get; set; } = 20000;
    public int MaxDepth { get; set; } = 6;
    public long MaxFileSizeBytes { get; set; } = 268435456;
    public int MaxHashFiles { get; set; } = 3000;
}

public sealed class RuleSet
{
    public RuleMeta Meta { get; set; } = new();
    public List<Rule> Rules { get; set; } = new();
    public Whitelist Whitelist { get; set; } = new();
    public ScanOptionsConfig Options { get; set; } = new();
    /// <summary>DMA 外设（用户态初筛）规则段。</summary>
    public DmaSection Dma { get; set; } = new();
    /// <summary>AI 视觉外挂线索规则段。</summary>
    public VisionSection Vision { get; set; } = new();
    /// <summary>深度扫描：句柄 / 驱动 / overlay / 文件特征码。</summary>
    public DeepSection Deep { get; set; } = new();
    /// <summary>游戏侧遥测对接（离线文件）。</summary>
    public TelemetrySection Telemetry { get; set; } = new();
}
```

```cs
using EagleEye.Core;

namespace EagleEye.Models;

public sealed class RuleSetSummary

    public List<string> Warnings { get; set; } = new();
}

public sealed class ScanSummary
{
    public int ProcessesScanned { get; set; }
    public int FilesScanned { get; set; }
    public int FilesHashed { get; set; }
    public int Findings { get; set; }
    public int SuppressedFindings { get; set; }
    public double TotalScore { get; set; }
    public double SumScore { get; set; }
    public string RiskLevel { get; set; } = RiskLevels.Info;
    public Dictionary<string, int> ByRiskLevel { get; set; } = new();
    public int NotCheckedCount { get; set; }
    public int AssessedTargets { get; set; }
    public bool FileScanEnabled { get; set; }
    public int DmaDevicesEnumerated { get; set; }
    public int DmaPciDevices { get; set; }
    public int DmaFindings { get; set; }
    public string DmaRiskLevel { get; set; } = RiskLevels.Info;
    public bool DmaKernelLoaded { get; set; }
    public int VisionSoftwareIndicators { get; set; }
    public int VisionHardwareIndicators { get; set; }
    public string VisionLevel { get; set; } = RiskLevels.Info;
    public int HandleFindings { get; set; }
    public int DriverFindings { get; set; }
    public int FileSignatureHits { get; set; }
    public int OverlayIndicators { get; set; }
    public bool TelemetryLoaded { get; set; }
    /// <summary>非管理员运行：受保护进程（含游戏本体）读不到，覆盖不完整。</summary>
    public bool PartialCoverage { get; set; }
    public int UnreadableTargets { get; set; }
    public string TelemetryJointLevel { get; set; } = RiskLevels.Info;
}

/// <summary>扫描器自身行为摘要（详细记录在同目录的 audit JSONL 里）。</summary>
public sealed class AuditSummary

    public string? LogPath { get; set; }
    public int ProcessOpenAttempts { get; set; }
    public int ProcessOpenDenied { get; set; }
    public int SignatureChecks { get; set; }
    public int DriverLoadAttempts { get; set; }
    public bool DriverLoaded { get; set; }
    public bool DriverUnloaded { get; set; }

    public int SignatureCacheNew { get; set; }
    /// <summary>验签缓存总条目数。</summary>
    public int SignatureCacheSize { get; set; }
    /// <summary>恒为 false：工具不发起任何网络请求。</summary>
    public bool NetworkUsed { get; set; }
}

public sealed class ScanReport
{
    public string Tool { get; set; } = "EagleEye";
    public string Version { get; set; } = "2.0.0";
    public string ScanStartedUtc { get; set; } = "";
    public string ScanFinishedUtc { get; set; } = "";
    public double DurationSeconds { get; set; }
    public string Machine { get; set; } = "";
    public string OsVersion { get; set; } = "";
    public string UserName { get; set; } = "";
    public bool IsElevated { get; set; }

    public RuleSetSummary Ruleset { get; set; } = new();
    public ScanSummary Summary { get; set; } = new();
    public AuditSummary Audit { get; set; } = new();

    /// <summary>参与风险聚合的命中（已排除白名单抑制项）。</summary>
    public List<Finding> Findings { get; set; } = new();
    /// <summary>被白名单抑制的命中，仅留痕，不参与评分。</summary>
    public List<Finding> SuppressedFindings { get; set; } = new();
    /// <summary>按目标聚合后的判定与证据链。</summary>
    public List<TargetAssessment> Assessments { get; set; } = new();

    public List<NotChecked> NotChecked { get; set; } = new();
    public List<DmaFinding> DmaUserModeFindings { get; set; } = new();
    /// <summary>视觉外挂线索（采集端/硬件/行为），独立成段，不进入主 risk_score。</summary>
    public VisionReport VisionCheatIndicators { get; set; } = new();
    /// <summary>句柄线索（谁以 VM_READ/WRITE 打开了目标进程）。</summary>
    public HandleScanReport Handles { get; set; } = new();
    /// <summary>已加载驱动线索。</summary>
    public DriverScanReport Drivers { get; set; } = new();
    /// <summary>文件特征码命中。</summary>
    public FileSignatureReport FileSignatures { get; set; } = new();
    /// <summary>注入痕迹：游戏进程里不在磁盘/路径异常/无签名的模块。</summary>

    /// <summary>游戏侧遥测与本机线索的联合评估。</summary>
```

`src/EagleEye/Native/DriverInterop.cs`:

```cs
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;

namespace EagleEye.Native;

/// <summary>已加载内核模块枚举（只读，SystemModuleInformation）。</summary>
internal static class DriverInterop
{
    private const int SystemModuleInformation = 11;

    [DllImport("ntdll.dll")]
    private static extern int NtQuerySystemInformation(int systemInformationClass, IntPtr systemInformation, int systemInformationLength, out int returnLength);

    public readonly record struct LoadedModule(string Name, string FullPath, long ImageBase, uint ImageSize);

    public static List<LoadedModule> Enumerate(out string? error)
    {
        error = null;
        var list = new List<LoadedModule>();

        IntPtr buffer = IntPtr.Zero;
        try
        {
            int length = 1 << 20;
            int status = -1;

            for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 5; attempt++)
            {
                buffer = Marshal.AllocHGlobal(length);
                status = NtQuerySystemInformation(SystemModuleInformation, buffer, length, out int needed);

                if (status == 0) break;

                Marshal.FreeHGlobal(buffer);
                buffer = IntPtr.Zero;

                if (status != unchecked((int)0xC0000004) || needed <= 0)
                {
                    error = $"NtQuerySystemInformation(SystemModuleInformation) 失败: 0x{status:X8}";
                    return list;
                }

                length = needed + (1 << 16);
            }

            if (buffer == IntPtr.Zero)
            {
                error = "无法分配模块信息缓冲区";
                return list;
            }

            int moduleCount = Marshal.ReadInt32(buffer);

            // RTL_PROCESS_MODULE_INFORMATION (x64) 布局：
            //   Section@0 MappedBase@8 ImageBase@16 ImageSize@24 Flags@28
            //   LoadOrderIndex@32 InitOrderIndex@34 LoadCount@36 OffsetToFileName@38
            //   FullPathName[256]@40  → 结构体步长 296（不是 152，早期写错导致路径被截断）
            const int ModuleStride = 296;
            const int PathOffset = 40;

            for (int i = 0; i < moduleCount; i++)
            {
                IntPtr entry = buffer + 8 + i * ModuleStride;

                long imageBase = Marshal.ReadIntPtr(entry, 16).ToInt64();
                uint imageSize = (uint)Marshal.ReadInt32(entry, 24);
                ushort offsetToFileName = (ushort)Marshal.ReadInt16(entry, 38);

                byte[] nameBuffer = new byte[256];
                Marshal.Copy(entry + PathOffset, nameBuffer, 0, 256);

                string fullPath = Encoding.ASCII.GetString(nameBuffer).TrimEnd('\0');
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(fullPath)) continue;

                string name = offsetToFileName < fullPath.Length ? fullPath[offsetToFileName..] : Path.GetFileName(fullPath);
                if (name.Contains('\0')) name = name[..name.IndexOf('\0')];

                list.Add(new LoadedModule(name, fullPath, imageBase, imageSize));
            }

            return list;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            error = ex.Message;
            return list;
        }
        finally
        {
            if (buffer != IntPtr.Zero) Marshal.FreeHGlobal(buffer);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>\SystemRoot\System32\drivers\xxx.sys → C:\Windows\System32\drivers\xxx.sys</summary>
    public static string ToWin32Path(string ntPath)
    {
        if (ntPath.StartsWith(@"\SystemRoot\", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            string win = Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.Windows);
            return Path.Combine(win, ntPath.Substring(@"\SystemRoot\".Length));
        }

        if (ntPath.StartsWith(@"\??\", StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            return ntPath[4..];
        }

        if (ntPath.StartsWith(@"\Device\", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            return ntPath;   // 无法映射到 Win32 路径
        }

        return ntPath;
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Native/HandleInterop.cs`:

```cs
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

namespace EagleEye.Native;

/// <summary>
/// 系统句柄表枚举（只读）。
/// 用途：找出"谁以 VM_READ / VM_WRITE / CREATE_THREAD 打开了某个进程"。
/// 做法：NtQuerySystemInformation(SystemExtendedHandleInformation) 拿句柄表 →
///       对高权限句柄 DuplicateHandle 到自己进程 → GetProcessId 得到目标 PID。
/// 不读取目标进程内存、不拦截、不修改任何对象。
/// </summary>
internal static class HandleInterop
{
    private const int SystemExtendedHandleInformation = 64;

    private const uint ProcessDuplicateHandle = 0x0040;
    private const uint DuplicateSameAccess = 0x00000002;

    private const uint AccessVmOperation = 0x0008;
    private const uint AccessVmRead = 0x0010;
    private const uint AccessVmWrite = 0x0020;
    private const uint AccessCreateThread = 0x0002;

    public const uint HighAccessMask = AccessVmOperation | AccessVmRead | AccessVmWrite | AccessCreateThread;

    [DllImport("ntdll.dll")]
    private static extern int NtQuerySystemInformation(int systemInformationClass, IntPtr systemInformation, int systemInformationLength, out int returnLength);

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern IntPtr OpenProcess(uint dwDesiredAccess, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] bool bInheritHandle, int dwProcessId);

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool DuplicateHandle(IntPtr hSourceProcessHandle, IntPtr hSourceHandle, IntPtr hTargetProcessHandle,
        out IntPtr lpTargetHandle, uint dwDesiredAccess, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] bool bInheritHandle, uint dwOptions);

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern uint GetProcessId(IntPtr Process);

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll")]
    private static extern IntPtr GetCurrentProcess();

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool CloseHandle(IntPtr hObject);

    public readonly record struct RawHandle(int HolderPid, IntPtr HandleValue, uint GrantedAccess, ushort TypeIndex);
    public readonly record struct TargetRef(int HolderPid, int TargetPid, uint GrantedAccess);

    public static List<TargetRef> FindProcessHandles(out int totalHandles, out int highAccessHandles, out string? error)
    {
        totalHandles = 0;
        highAccessHandles = 0;
        error = null;
        var results = new List<TargetRef>();

        IntPtr buffer = IntPtr.Zero;
        IntPtr probe = OpenProcess(ProcessDuplicateHandle, false, Environment.ProcessId);
        try
        {
            int length = 1 << 20;
            int status;
            for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 6; attempt++)
            {
                buffer = Marshal.AllocHGlobal(length);
                status = NtQuerySystemInformation(SystemExtendedHandleInformation, buffer, length, out int needed);

                if (status == 0)
                {
                    break;
                }

                Marshal.FreeHGlobal(buffer);
                buffer = IntPtr.Zero;

                // STATUS_INFO_LENGTH_MISMATCH
                if (status != unchecked((int)0xC0000004) || needed <= 0)
                {
                    error = $"NtQuerySystemInformation 失败: 0x{status:X8}";
                    return results;
                }

                length = needed + (1 << 20);
            }

            if (buffer == IntPtr.Zero)
            {
                error = "无法分配句柄表缓冲区";
                return results;
            }

            long count = Marshal.ReadIntPtr(buffer, 0).ToInt64();
            totalHandles = (int)Math.Min(count, int.MaxValue);

            ushort processTypeIndex = ResolveProcessTypeIndex(buffer, count, probe);
            if (processTypeIndex == ushort.MaxValue)
            {
                error = "无法解析 Process 对象的类型索引";
                return results;
            }

            var holderHandles = new Dictionary<int, IntPtr>();
            IntPtr self = GetCurrentProcess();

            for (long i = 0; i < count; i++)
            {
                IntPtr entry = buffer + 16 + (int)(i * 40);

                IntPtr handleValue = Marshal.ReadIntPtr(entry, 16);
                uint grantedAccess = (uint)Marshal.ReadInt32(entry, 24);
                ushort typeIndex = (ushort)Marshal.ReadInt16(entry, 30);
                int holderPid = (int)Marshal.ReadIntPtr(entry, 8).ToInt64();

                if (typeIndex != processTypeIndex) continue;
                if ((grantedAccess & HighAccessMask) == 0) continue;
                if (holderPid <= 4) continue;

                highAccessHandles++;

                if (!holderHandles.TryGetValue(holderPid, out IntPtr holder))
                {
                    holder = OpenProcess(ProcessDuplicateHandle, false, holderPid);
                    holderHandles[holderPid] = holder;
                    if (holder != IntPtr.Zero) { }
                }

                if (holder == IntPtr.Zero) continue;

                if (!DuplicateHandle(holder, handleValue, self, out IntPtr dup, 0, false, DuplicateSameAccess))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                try
                {
                    uint targetPid = GetProcessId(dup);
                    if (targetPid > 4 && targetPid != holderPid)
                    {
                        results.Add(new TargetRef(holderPid, (int)targetPid, grantedAccess));
                    }
                }
                finally
                {
                    CloseHandle(dup);
                }
            }

            foreach (var kv in holderHandles)
            {
                if (kv.Value != IntPtr.Zero) CloseHandle(kv.Value);
            }

            return results;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            error = ex.Message;
            return results;
        }
        finally
        {
            if (buffer != IntPtr.Zero) Marshal.FreeHGlobal(buffer);
            if (probe != IntPtr.Zero) CloseHandle(probe);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>用自己进程的一个已知 Process 句柄反查类型索引。</summary>
    private static ushort ResolveProcessTypeIndex(IntPtr buffer, long count, IntPtr probe)
    {
        int selfPid = Environment.ProcessId;

        if (probe == IntPtr.Zero)
        {
            return ushort.MaxValue;
        }

        try
        {
            for (long i = 0; i < count; i++)
            {
                IntPtr entry = buffer + 16 + (int)(i * 40);
                int holderPid = (int)Marshal.ReadIntPtr(entry, 8).ToInt64();
                if (holderPid != selfPid) continue;

                IntPtr handleValue = Marshal.ReadIntPtr(entry, 16);
                if (handleValue.ToInt64() == probe.ToInt64())
                {
                    return (ushort)Marshal.ReadInt16(entry, 30);
                }
            }

            return ushort.MaxValue;
        }
        catch
        {
            return ushort.MaxValue;
        }
    }

    public static string DescribeAccess(uint access)
    {
        var parts = new List<string>();
        if ((access & AccessVmOperation) != 0) parts.Add("VM_OPERATION");
        if ((access & AccessVmRead) != 0) parts.Add("VM_READ");
        if ((access & AccessVmWrite) != 0) parts.Add("VM_WRITE");
        if ((access & AccessCreateThread) != 0) parts.Add("CREATE_THREAD");
        return parts.Count == 0 ? $"0x{access:X}" : string.Join("|", parts) + $" (0x{access:X})";
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Native/NativeMethods.cs`:

```cs
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;

namespace EagleEye.Native;

/// <summary>
/// 只用到的 Win32 / NT 原生接口集合。
/// 设计约束：只用只读、最小权限接口；不包含任何内存写入、注入、Hook 相关 API。
/// </summary>
internal static class NativeMethods
{
    // ---- 进程访问权限（只请求查询权限，绝不请求 VM_READ / VM_WRITE / VM_OPERATION）----
    public const uint ProcessQueryLimitedInformation = 0x1000;
    public const uint ProcessQueryInformation = 0x0400;

    // ---- Toolhelp 快照 ----
    public const uint TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS = 0x00000002;
    public const uint TH32CS_SNAPMODULE = 0x00000008;
    public const uint TH32CS_SNAPMODULE32 = 0x00000010;

    public static readonly IntPtr InvalidHandleValue = new(-1);

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
    public struct PROCESSENTRY32W
    {
        public uint dwSize;
        public uint cntUsage;
        public uint th32ProcessID;
        public IntPtr th32DefaultHeapID;
        public uint th32ModuleID;
        public uint cntThreads;
        public uint th32ParentProcessID;
        public int pcPriClassBase;
        public uint dwFlags;
        [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValTStr, SizeConst = 260)]
        public string szExeFile;
    }

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
    public struct MODULEENTRY32W
    {
        public uint dwSize;
        public uint th32ModuleID;
        public uint th32ProcessID;
        public uint GlblcntUsage;
        public uint ProccntUsage;
        public IntPtr modBaseAddr;
        public uint modBaseSize;
        public IntPtr hModule;
        [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValTStr, SizeConst = 256)]
        public string szModule;
        [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValTStr, SizeConst = 260)]
        public string szExePath;
    }

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    public static extern IntPtr CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(uint dwFlags, uint th32ProcessID);

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, EntryPoint = "Process32FirstW")]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    public static extern bool Process32First(IntPtr hSnapshot, ref PROCESSENTRY32W lppe);

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, EntryPoint = "Process32NextW")]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    public static extern bool Process32Next(IntPtr hSnapshot, ref PROCESSENTRY32W lppe);

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, EntryPoint = "Module32FirstW")]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    public static extern bool Module32First(IntPtr hSnapshot, ref MODULEENTRY32W lpme);

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, EntryPoint = "Module32NextW")]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    public static extern bool Module32Next(IntPtr hSnapshot, ref MODULEENTRY32W lpme);

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    public static extern IntPtr OpenProcess(uint dwDesiredAccess, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] bool bInheritHandle, uint dwProcessId);

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    public static extern bool CloseHandle(IntPtr hObject);

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    public static extern bool QueryFullProcessImageName(IntPtr hProcess, uint dwFlags, StringBuilder lpExeName, ref int lpdwSize);

    // ---- 命令行：走内核信息类查询（ProcessCommandLineInformation），不做内存读取 ----
    private const int ProcessCommandLineInformation = 60;

    [DllImport("ntdll.dll")]
    private static extern int NtQueryInformationProcess(
        IntPtr processHandle,
        int processInformationClass,
        IntPtr processInformation,
        int processInformationLength,
        out int returnLength);

    public static string? TryGetImagePath(IntPtr hProcess)
    {
        var sb = new StringBuilder(1024);
        int size = sb.Capacity;
        if (QueryFullProcessImageName(hProcess, 0, sb, ref size))
        {
            return sb.ToString(0, size);
        }
        return null;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 通过 ProcessCommandLineInformation 信息类取命令行。
    /// 需要 PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION；不读取目标进程地址空间。
    /// </summary>
    public static string? TryGetCommandLine(IntPtr hProcess)
    {
        int needed = 0;
        // 第一次调用拿所需缓冲区大小，预期返回 STATUS_INFO_LENGTH_MISMATCH / STATUS_BUFFER_TOO_SMALL
        NtQueryInformationProcess(hProcess, ProcessCommandLineInformation, IntPtr.Zero, 0, out needed);
        if (needed <= 0 || needed > 64 * 1024)
        {
            return null;
        }

        IntPtr buffer = Marshal.AllocHGlobal(needed);
        try
        {
            int status = NtQueryInformationProcess(hProcess, ProcessCommandLineInformation, buffer, needed, out _);
            if (status < 0)
            {
                return null;
            }

            // 返回的是 UNICODE_STRING：Length(2) MaximumLength(2) padding Buffer(8)  —— x64 布局
            int length = Marshal.ReadInt16(buffer, 0);
            IntPtr stringPtr = Marshal.ReadIntPtr(buffer, 8);
            if (length <= 0 || stringPtr == IntPtr.Zero)
            {
                return string.Empty;
            }

            return Marshal.PtrToStringUni(stringPtr, length / 2);
        }
        catch
        {
            return null;
        }
        finally
        {
            Marshal.FreeHGlobal(buffer);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Native/SetupApiInterop.cs`:

```cs
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;

namespace EagleEye.Native;

/// <summary>
/// 用户态设备枚举：SetupAPI + CfgMgr32。
///
/// 只读约束（重要）：
/// 1. 只调用 SetupDiGetClassDevs / SetupDiEnumDeviceInfo / CM_Get_Device_ID / CM_Get_DevNode_Registry_Property /
///    CM_Get_DevNode_Status 这几个查询接口，不改设备状态、不改注册表、不打开设备句柄。
/// 2. 不访问 IOMMU、不读写 PCIe 配置空间（用户态本来也做不到）。
/// 3. 因此本模块拿到的 VID/PID/REV 来自设备实例 ID 字符串，Class 来自设备类名，
///    并不是配置空间真实值；ClassCode 只能"近似推导"。
/// </summary>
internal static class SetupApiInterop
{
    private const uint DIGCF_PRESENT = 0x00000002;
    private const uint DIGCF_ALLCLASSES = 0x00000004;

    private const int CR_SUCCESS = 0;
    private const int CR_BUFFER_SMALL = 0x1A;

    // CM_DRP_* —— cfg.h
    private const uint CM_DRP_DEVICEDESC = 0x01;
    private const uint CM_DRP_HARDWAREID = 0x02;
    private const uint CM_DRP_SERVICE = 0x05;   // cfg.h: 0x04 是 UNUSED4，SERVICE 是 0x05
    private const uint CM_DRP_CLASS = 0x08;
    private const uint CM_DRP_CLASSGUID = 0x09;
    private const uint CM_DRP_MFG = 0x0C;
    private const uint CM_DRP_FRIENDLYNAME = 0x0D;
    private const uint CM_DRP_LOCATION_INFORMATION = 0x0E;
    private const uint CM_DRP_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_OBJECT_NAME = 0x0F;

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
    private struct SP_DEVINFO_DATA
    {
        public uint cbSize;
        public Guid ClassGuid;
        public uint DevInst;
        public IntPtr Reserved;
    }

    [DllImport("setupapi.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern IntPtr SetupDiGetClassDevsW(IntPtr classGuid, string? enumerator, IntPtr hwndParent, uint flags);

    [DllImport("setupapi.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool SetupDiEnumDeviceInfo(IntPtr deviceInfoSet, uint memberIndex, ref SP_DEVINFO_DATA deviceInfoData);

    [DllImport("setupapi.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool SetupDiDestroyDeviceInfoList(IntPtr deviceInfoSet);

    [DllImport("cfgmgr32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
    private static extern int CM_Get_Device_IDW(uint devInst, StringBuilder buffer, int bufferLen, int flags);

    [DllImport("cfgmgr32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
    private static extern int CM_Get_DevNode_Registry_PropertyW(
        uint devInst, uint property, out uint regDataType, byte[]? buffer, ref uint length, uint flags);

    [DllImport("cfgmgr32.dll")]
    private static extern int CM_Get_DevNode_Status(out uint status, out uint problemNumber, uint devInst, uint flags);

    public sealed class RawDevice
    {
        public string InstanceId { get; set; } = "";
        public string? DeviceDesc { get; set; }
        public string? FriendlyName { get; set; }
        public string? ClassName { get; set; }
        public string? ClassGuid { get; set; }
        public string? Service { get; set; }
        public string? Location { get; set; }
        public string? Manufacturer { get; set; }
        public string? HardwareIds { get; set; }
        /// <summary>PDO 设备对象名，如 \Device\NTPNP_PCI0007</summary>
        public string? PdoName { get; set; }
        public uint? ProblemCode { get; set; }
    }

    /// <summary>枚举当前存在的所有设备，调用方自行按实例 ID 前缀过滤 PCI。</summary>
    public static List<RawDevice> EnumeratePresentDevices(out string? error)
    {
        error = null;
        var list = new List<RawDevice>();

        IntPtr deviceInfoSet = SetupDiGetClassDevsW(IntPtr.Zero, null, IntPtr.Zero, DIGCF_PRESENT | DIGCF_ALLCLASSES);
        if (deviceInfoSet == IntPtr.Zero || deviceInfoSet == new IntPtr(-1))
        {
            error = $"SetupDiGetClassDevs 失败（Win32={Marshal.GetLastWin32Error()}）";
            return list;
        }

        try
        {
            var info = new SP_DEVINFO_DATA { cbSize = (uint)Marshal.SizeOf<SP_DEVINFO_DATA>() };

            for (uint index = 0; ; index++)
            {
                info.cbSize = (uint)Marshal.SizeOf<SP_DEVINFO_DATA>();
                if (!SetupDiEnumDeviceInfo(deviceInfoSet, index, ref info))
                {
                    int err = Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
                    if (err != 259) // ERROR_NO_MORE_ITEMS
                    {
                        error = $"SetupDiEnumDeviceInfo 在第 {index} 项失败（Win32={err}）";
                    }
                    break;
                }

                string? instanceId = GetInstanceId(info.DevInst);
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(instanceId))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                var device = new RawDevice
                {
                    InstanceId = instanceId!,
                    DeviceDesc = ReadString(info.DevInst, CM_DRP_DEVICEDESC),
                    FriendlyName = ReadString(info.DevInst, CM_DRP_FRIENDLYNAME),
                    ClassName = ReadString(info.DevInst, CM_DRP_CLASS),
                    ClassGuid = ReadString(info.DevInst, CM_DRP_CLASSGUID),
                    Service = ReadString(info.DevInst, CM_DRP_SERVICE),
                    Location = ReadString(info.DevInst, CM_DRP_LOCATION_INFORMATION),
                    Manufacturer = ReadString(info.DevInst, CM_DRP_MFG),
                    HardwareIds = ReadMultiString(info.DevInst, CM_DRP_HARDWAREID),
                    PdoName = ReadString(info.DevInst, CM_DRP_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_OBJECT_NAME)
                };

                if (CM_Get_DevNode_Status(out _, out uint problem, info.DevInst, 0) == CR_SUCCESS)
                {
                    device.ProblemCode = problem;
                }

                list.Add(device);
            }
        }
        finally
        {
            SetupDiDestroyDeviceInfoList(deviceInfoSet);
        }

        return list;
    }

    private static string? GetInstanceId(uint devInst)
    {
        var sb = new StringBuilder(512);
        return CM_Get_Device_IDW(devInst, sb, sb.Capacity, 0) == CR_SUCCESS ? sb.ToString() : null;
    }

    private static string? ReadString(uint devInst, uint property)
    {
        uint type;
        uint length = 0;

        int cr = CM_Get_DevNode_Registry_PropertyW(devInst, property, out type, null, ref length, 0);
        if (cr != CR_BUFFER_SMALL || length == 0)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var buffer = new byte[length];
        cr = CM_Get_DevNode_Registry_PropertyW(devInst, property, out type, buffer, ref length, 0);
        if (cr != CR_SUCCESS)
        {
            return null;
        }

        string value = Encoding.Unicode.GetString(buffer, 0, (int)length).TrimEnd('\0');
        return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value) ? null : value;
    }

    private static string? ReadMultiString(uint devInst, uint property)
    {
        string? raw = ReadString(devInst, property);
        return raw;
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Native/SignatureChecker.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Security.Cryptography.X509Certificates;
using EagleEye.Models;

namespace EagleEye.Native;

/// <summary>
/// Authenticode 校验：内嵌签名（WinVerifyTrust）+ 目录签名（cat 文件）双路。
/// 结果按 路径+大小+修改时间 缓存，避免重复验签。
/// </summary>
public sealed class SignatureChecker
{
    private static readonly Guid WinTrustActionGenericVerifyV2 =
        new("00AAC56B-CD44-11d0-8CC2-00C04FC295EE");

    private const uint WTD_UI_NONE = 2;
    private const uint WTD_REVOKE_NONE = 0;
    private const uint WTD_REVOKE_WHOLECHAIN = 1;
    private const uint WTD_CHOICE_FILE = 1;
    private const uint WTD_STATEACTION_VERIFY = 1;
    private const uint WTD_STATEACTION_CLOSE = 2;
    private const uint WTD_CACHE_ONLY_URL_RETRIEVAL = 0x00001000;
    private const uint WTD_REVOCATION_CHECK_CHAIN_EXCLUDE_ROOT = 0x00000400;

    private const uint S_OK = 0;
    private const uint TRUST_E_NOSIGNATURE = 0x800B0100;
    private const uint CERT_E_EXPIRED = 0x800B0101;
    private const uint CERT_E_UNTRUSTEDROOT = 0x800B0109;
    private const uint CERT_E_CHAINING = 0x800B010A;
    private const uint CERT_E_REVOKED = 0x800B010C;
    private const uint TRUST_E_EXPLICIT_DISTRUST = 0x800B0111;
    private const uint TRUST_E_BAD_DIGEST = 0x80096010;

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
    private struct WINTRUST_FILE_INFO
    {
        public uint cbStruct;
        public IntPtr pcwszFilePath;
        public IntPtr hFile;
        public IntPtr pgKnownSubject;
    }

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
    private struct WINTRUST_DATA
    {
        public uint cbStruct;
        public IntPtr pPolicyCallbackData;
        public IntPtr pSIPClientData;
        public uint dwUIChoice;
        public uint fdwRevocationChecks;
        public uint dwUnionChoice;
        public IntPtr pFile;
        public uint dwStateAction;
        public IntPtr hWVTStateData;
        public IntPtr pwszURLReference;
        public uint dwProvFlags;
        public uint dwUIContext;
        public IntPtr pSignatureSettings;
    }

    [DllImport("wintrust.dll", ExactSpelling = true, SetLastError = true, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
    private static extern uint WinVerifyTrust(IntPtr hwnd, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPStruct)] Guid pgActionID, IntPtr pWVTData);

    [DllImport("wintrust.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern bool CryptCATAdminAcquireContext(out IntPtr phCatAdmin, IntPtr pgSubsystem, uint dwFlags);

    [DllImport("wintrust.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern bool CryptCATAdminCalcHashFromFileHandle(IntPtr hFile, ref uint pcbHash, byte[]? pbHash, uint dwFlags);

    [DllImport("wintrust.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern IntPtr CryptCATAdminEnumCatalogFromHash(IntPtr hCatAdmin, byte[] pbHash, uint cbHash, uint dwFlags, ref IntPtr phPrevCatInfo);

    [DllImport("wintrust.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern bool CryptCATAdminReleaseContext(IntPtr hCatAdmin, uint dwFlags);

    [DllImport("wintrust.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern bool CryptCATAdminReleaseCatalogContext(IntPtr hCatAdmin, IntPtr hCatInfo, uint dwFlags);

    private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, SignatureInfo> _cache = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    private readonly bool _checkRevocation;
    private readonly string? _cachePath;
    private int _newEntries;

    /// <summary>
    /// 验签结果缓存：内存 + 磁盘。
    /// 磁盘缓存键 = 路径|大小|修改时间，文件一变自动失效，因此不会误用旧结果。
    /// 273 个驱动逐一验签约 4-5s，命中缓存后接近 0。
    /// </summary>
    public SignatureChecker(bool checkRevocation, string? cachePath = null)
    {
        _checkRevocation = checkRevocation;
        _cachePath = cachePath;
        LoadCache();
    }

    public int CacheSize => _cache.Count;
    public int NewEntries => _newEntries;

    private void LoadCache()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_cachePath) || !File.Exists(_cachePath)) return;

        try
        {
            string json = File.ReadAllText(_cachePath!);
            var loaded = System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Deserialize<Dictionary<string, SignatureInfo>>(json);
            if (loaded is null) return;

            foreach (var kv in loaded.Take(20000))
            {
                _cache[kv.Key] = kv.Value;
            }
        }
        catch
        {
            // 缓存损坏就当没有，不影响扫描
        }
    }

    public void SaveCache()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_cachePath) || _newEntries == 0) return;

        try
        {
            string? dir = Path.GetDirectoryName(Path.GetFullPath(_cachePath!));
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dir)) Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);

            var snapshot = _cache.Take(20000).ToDictionary(kv => kv.Key, kv => kv.Value);
            string json = System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Serialize(snapshot, new System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions
            {
                WriteIndented = false,
                Encoder = System.Text.Encodings.Web.JavaScriptEncoder.UnsafeRelaxedJsonEscaping
            });
            File.WriteAllText(_cachePath!, json, new System.Text.UTF8Encoding(false));
```
