        }
        catch
        {
            // 写缓存失败不影响扫描
        }
    }

    public SignatureInfo Check(string? path)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path) || !File.Exists(path))
        {
            return new SignatureInfo { State = SignatureStates.Unavailable, Error = "文件不存在或路径不可读" };
        }

        string key = BuildCacheKey(path);

        if (_cache.TryGetValue(key, out SignatureInfo? cached))
        {
            return cached;
        }

        var fresh = CheckCore(path!);
        if (_cache.TryAdd(key, fresh))
        {
            Interlocked.Increment(ref _newEntries);
        }

        return fresh;
    }

    private static string BuildCacheKey(string path)
    {
        try
        {
            var fi = new FileInfo(path);
            return $"{fi.FullName}|{fi.Length}|{fi.LastWriteTimeUtc.Ticks}";
        }
        catch
        {
            return path;
        }
    }

    private SignatureInfo CheckCore(string path)
    {
        uint status = VerifyEmbedded(path);
        if (status == S_OK)
        {
            return new SignatureInfo
            {
                State = SignatureStates.Valid,
                HasEmbeddedSignature = true,
                WinTrustStatus = status,
                SignerSubject = TryGetSignerSubject(path)
            };
        }

        if (status == TRUST_E_NOSIGNATURE)
        {
            // 无内嵌签名 → 查目录签名（大量 Windows 组件只有 cat 签名）
            if (HasCatalogSignature(path))
            {
                return new SignatureInfo
                {
                    State = SignatureStates.CatalogValid,
                    CatalogSigned = true,
                    HasEmbeddedSignature = false,
                    WinTrustStatus = status,
                    SignerSubject = null
                };
            }

            return new SignatureInfo
            {
                State = SignatureStates.Unsigned,
                HasEmbeddedSignature = false,
                WinTrustStatus = status
            };
        }

        string state = status switch
        {
            TRUST_E_BAD_DIGEST => SignatureStates.BadDigest,
            CERT_E_EXPIRED => SignatureStates.Expired,
            CERT_E_REVOKED => SignatureStates.Revoked,
            CERT_E_UNTRUSTEDROOT => SignatureStates.UntrustedRoot,
            CERT_E_CHAINING => SignatureStates.InvalidChain,
            TRUST_E_EXPLICIT_DISTRUST => SignatureStates.Distrusted,
            _ => SignatureStates.Error
        };

        return new SignatureInfo
        {
            State = state,
            HasEmbeddedSignature = true,
            WinTrustStatus = status,
            Error = $"0x{status:X8}",
            SignerSubject = TryGetSignerSubject(path)
        };
    }

    private uint VerifyEmbedded(string path)
    {
        IntPtr pFilePath = IntPtr.Zero;
        IntPtr pFileInfo = IntPtr.Zero;
        IntPtr pData = IntPtr.Zero;

        try
        {
            pFilePath = Marshal.StringToHGlobalUni(path);

            var fileInfo = new WINTRUST_FILE_INFO
            {
                cbStruct = (uint)Marshal.SizeOf<WINTRUST_FILE_INFO>(),
                pcwszFilePath = pFilePath,
                hFile = IntPtr.Zero,
                pgKnownSubject = IntPtr.Zero
            };

            pFileInfo = Marshal.AllocHGlobal(Marshal.SizeOf<WINTRUST_FILE_INFO>());
            Marshal.StructureToPtr(fileInfo, pFileInfo, false);

            var data = new WINTRUST_DATA
            {
                cbStruct = (uint)Marshal.SizeOf<WINTRUST_DATA>(),
                pPolicyCallbackData = IntPtr.Zero,
                pSIPClientData = IntPtr.Zero,
                dwUIChoice = WTD_UI_NONE,
                fdwRevocationChecks = _checkRevocation ? WTD_REVOKE_WHOLECHAIN : WTD_REVOKE_NONE,
                dwUnionChoice = WTD_CHOICE_FILE,
                pFile = pFileInfo,
                dwStateAction = WTD_STATEACTION_VERIFY,
                hWVTStateData = IntPtr.Zero,
                pwszURLReference = IntPtr.Zero,
                dwProvFlags = _checkRevocation
                    ? WTD_CACHE_ONLY_URL_RETRIEVAL | WTD_REVOCATION_CHECK_CHAIN_EXCLUDE_ROOT
                    : WTD_CACHE_ONLY_URL_RETRIEVAL,
                dwUIContext = 0,
                pSignatureSettings = IntPtr.Zero
            };

            pData = Marshal.AllocHGlobal(Marshal.SizeOf<WINTRUST_DATA>());
            Marshal.StructureToPtr(data, pData, false);

            uint result = WinVerifyTrust(IntPtr.Zero, WinTrustActionGenericVerifyV2, pData);

            // 关闭状态句柄，避免内存泄漏
            var after = Marshal.PtrToStructure<WINTRUST_DATA>(pData);
            after.dwStateAction = WTD_STATEACTION_CLOSE;
            Marshal.StructureToPtr(after, pData, false);
            WinVerifyTrust(IntPtr.Zero, WinTrustActionGenericVerifyV2, pData);

            return result;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            return unchecked((uint)ex.HResult);
        }
        finally
        {
            if (pData != IntPtr.Zero) Marshal.FreeHGlobal(pData);
            if (pFileInfo != IntPtr.Zero) Marshal.FreeHGlobal(pFileInfo);
            if (pFilePath != IntPtr.Zero) Marshal.FreeHGlobal(pFilePath);
        }
    }

    private bool HasCatalogSignature(string path)
    {
        IntPtr hCatAdmin = IntPtr.Zero;
        FileStream? fs = null;
        try
        {
            if (!CryptCATAdminAcquireContext(out hCatAdmin, IntPtr.Zero, 0))
            {
                return false;
            }

            fs = new FileStream(path, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.ReadWrite | FileShare.Delete);
            uint hashSize = 0;
            if (!CryptCATAdminCalcHashFromFileHandle(fs.SafeFileHandle.DangerousGetHandle(), ref hashSize, null, 0) || hashSize == 0)
            {
                return false;
            }

            byte[] hash = new byte[hashSize];
            if (!CryptCATAdminCalcHashFromFileHandle(fs.SafeFileHandle.DangerousGetHandle(), ref hashSize, hash, 0))
            {
                return false;
            }

            IntPtr prev = IntPtr.Zero;
            IntPtr catInfo = CryptCATAdminEnumCatalogFromHash(hCatAdmin, hash, hashSize, 0, ref prev);
            if (catInfo == IntPtr.Zero)
            {
                return false;
            }

            CryptCATAdminReleaseCatalogContext(hCatAdmin, catInfo, 0);
            return true;
        }
        catch
        {
            return false;
        }
        finally
        {
            fs?.Dispose();
            if (hCatAdmin != IntPtr.Zero)
            {
                CryptCATAdminReleaseContext(hCatAdmin, 0);
            }
        }
    }

    private static string? TryGetSignerSubject(string path)
    {
        try
        {
#pragma warning disable SYSLIB0026
            using X509Certificate cert = X509Certificate.CreateFromSignedFile(path);
            return cert.Subject;
#pragma warning restore SYSLIB0026
        }
        catch
        {
            return null;
        }
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Native/WindowApi.cs`:

```cs
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;

namespace EagleEye.Native;

/// <summary>
/// 窗口/光标相关的只读查询。
/// 只做枚举与属性读取：不 SetWindowLong、不 SetForegroundWindow、不发消息、
/// 不截取窗口内容、不安装任何钩子。
/// </summary>
public static class WindowApi
{
    public const long WS_EX_TOPMOST = 0x00000008L;
    public const long WS_EX_TRANSPARENT = 0x00000020L;
    public const long WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW = 0x00000080L;
    public const long WS_EX_LAYERED = 0x00080000L;
    public const long WS_EX_NOACTIVATE = 0x08000000L;

    private const int GWL_EXSTYLE = -20;

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
    public struct RECT
    {
        public int Left;
        public int Top;
        public int Right;
        public int Bottom;
    }

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
    public struct POINT
    {
        public int X;
        public int Y;
    }

    public readonly record struct WindowInfo(
        IntPtr Handle, int ProcessId, string Title, bool Visible,
        int Left, int Top, int Width, int Height, long ExStyle)
    {
        public int Area => Width * Height;
    }

    private delegate bool EnumWindowsProc(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr lParam);

    [DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumWindowsProc lpEnumFunc, IntPtr lParam);

    [DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool IsWindowVisible(IntPtr hWnd);

    [DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr hWnd, out uint lpdwProcessId);

    [DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr hWnd, out RECT lpRect);

    [DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError = true, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
    private static extern int GetWindowTextW(IntPtr hWnd, StringBuilder lpString, int nMaxCount);

    [DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError = true, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
    private static extern int GetWindowTextLengthW(IntPtr hWnd);

    [DllImport("user32.dll", EntryPoint = "GetWindowLongPtrW", SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern IntPtr GetWindowLongPtr64(IntPtr hWnd, int nIndex);

    [DllImport("user32.dll", EntryPoint = "GetWindowLongW", SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern int GetWindowLong32(IntPtr hWnd, int nIndex);

    [DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    public static extern bool GetCursorPos(out POINT lpPoint);

    private static long GetExStyle(IntPtr hWnd)
    {
        return Environment.Is64BitProcess
            ? GetWindowLongPtr64(hWnd, GWL_EXSTYLE).ToInt64()
            : GetWindowLong32(hWnd, GWL_EXSTYLE);
    }

    public static List<WindowInfo> EnumerateVisibleWindows()
    {
        var list = new List<WindowInfo>();

        EnumWindows((hWnd, _) =>
        {
            try
            {
                if (!IsWindowVisible(hWnd)) return true;
                if (!GetWindowRect(hWnd, out RECT rect)) return true;

                int width = rect.Right - rect.Left;
                int height = rect.Bottom - rect.Top;
                if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return true;

                GetWindowThreadProcessId(hWnd, out uint pid);

                string title = "";
                int length = GetWindowTextLengthW(hWnd);
                if (length > 0 && length < 512)
                {
                    var sb = new StringBuilder(length + 1);
                    GetWindowTextW(hWnd, sb, sb.Capacity);
                    title = sb.ToString();
                }

                list.Add(new WindowInfo(hWnd, (int)pid, title, true,
                    rect.Left, rect.Top, width, height, GetExStyle(hWnd)));
            }
            catch
            {
                // 单个窗口失败不影响整体
            }

            return true;
        }, IntPtr.Zero);

        return list;
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Program.cs`:

```cs
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Security.Principal;
using EagleEye.Core;
using EagleEye.Kernel;
using EagleEye.Models;
using EagleEye.Native;
using EagleEye.Reporting;
using EagleEye.Rules;
using EagleEye.Scanning;

namespace EagleEye;

internal static class Program
{
    private const string ToolVersion = "2.0.0";

    private static int Main(string[] args)
    {
        try
        {
            Console.OutputEncoding = System.Text.Encoding.UTF8;
        }
        catch
        {
            // 控制台编码设置失败不影响主流程
        }

        try
        {
            return Run(args);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Console.Error.WriteLine($"[FATAL] {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}");
            Console.Error.WriteLine(ex.StackTrace);
            return 1;
        }
    }

    private static int Run(string[] args)
    {
        if (args.Length == 0)
        {
            return Wizard.Run();
        }

        if (args[0] is "-h" or "--help" or "help" or "/?")
        {
            PrintHelp();
            return 0;
        }

        string command = args[0].StartsWith("-", StringComparison.Ordinal) ? "scan" : args[0].ToLowerInvariant();
        var options = ParseOptions(args);
        ApplyProfile(options);

        if (command is not ("scan" or "validate" or "selftest" or "aggregate" or "tune"))
        {
            Console.Error.WriteLine($"未知命令: {args[0]}");
            PrintHelp();
            return 1;
        }

        if (command == "aggregate")
        {
            string aggregateDir = options.AggregateDir ?? Path.Combine(OutputRoot(), "reports");

            int aggregateCode = BatchAggregator.Run(aggregateDir, options.AggregateMd, options.AggregateJson, options.Quiet);
            if (aggregateCode != 0 || !options.WithTune)
            {
                return aggregateCode;
            }

            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine("-- 继续做调参 --");
            return RuleTuner.Run(new RuleTuner.Options
            {
                ReportsDir = aggregateDir,
                Label = options.TuneLabel,
                RulesDir = options.RulesDir,
                OutputPath = options.TuneOutputPath
            }, options.Quiet);
        }

        if (command == "tune")
        {
            return RuleTuner.Run(new RuleTuner.Options
            {
                ReportsDir = options.AggregateDir ?? Path.Combine(OutputRoot(), "reports"),
                Label = options.TuneLabel,
                RulesDir = options.RulesDir,
                OutputPath = options.TuneOutputPath,
                NoWhitelist = options.NoWhitelist
            }, options.Quiet);
        }

        RuleLoadResult load;

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(options.RulesDir))
        {
            load = RuleLoader.LoadDirectory(options.RulesDir!);
        }
        else if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(options.RulesPath))
        {
            load = RuleLoader.Load(options.RulesPath!);
        }
        else
        {
            // 默认：加载 exe 同目录的 rules 目录（把专项包/情报包一并合并），
            // 目录不存在再退回单文件
            string defaultDir = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "rules");
            bool hasJson = Directory.Exists(defaultDir) &&
                           Directory.GetFiles(defaultDir, "*.json").Length > 0;

            load = hasJson
                ? RuleLoader.LoadDirectory(defaultDir)
                : RuleLoader.Load(ResolveRulesPath(null));
        }
        PrintRuleInfo(load);

        if (!load.Success)
        {
            Console.Error.WriteLine();
            Console.Error.WriteLine("规则校验失败，已停止：");
            foreach (string err in load.Errors)
            {
                Console.Error.WriteLine("  - " + err);
            }
            return 1;
        }

        if (command == "validate")
        {
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine("规则校验通过。");
            return 0;
        }

        if (command == "selftest")
        {
            return SelfTest.Run(load, options.Quiet);
        }

        return ExecuteScan(load, options);
    }

    /// <summary>供向导调用的简化入口：等价于 scan（标准档位）。</summary>
    internal static int RunScanFromWizard(string rulesDir)
    {
        var load = RuleLoader.LoadDirectory(rulesDir);
        if (!load.Success)
        {
            return 1;
        }

        var options = new Options { RulesDir = rulesDir };
        ApplyProfile(options);
        return ExecuteScan(load, options);
    }

    private static int ExecuteScan(RuleLoadResult load, Options options)
    {
        var ruleSet = load.RuleSet;
        DateTime startedUtc = DateTime.UtcNow;
        var stopwatch = Stopwatch.StartNew();

        using var audit = new AuditLogger(options.LogPath ?? DefaultLogPath());
        audit.Write("scan.start", new
        {
            tool = "EagleEye",
            version = ToolVersion,
            rulesPath = load.SourcePath,
            rulesVersion = ruleSet.Meta.Version,
            rulesUpdated = ruleSet.Meta.Updated,
            options = new
            {
                fileScan = options.FileScanEnabled,
                dma = options.DmaEnabled,
                kernel = options.KernelEnabled,
                allProcesses = options.AllProcesses,
                timeoutSeconds = options.TimeoutSeconds
            },
            note = "本工具不发起任何网络请求，不上传任何数据"
        });

        using var cts = new CancellationTokenSource(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(Math.Max(10, options.TimeoutSeconds)));

        string sigCachePath = options.SigCachePath ?? Path.Combine(OutputRoot(), "cache", "sigcache.json");

        var signatureChecker = new SignatureChecker(
            options.CheckRevocation,
            string.IsNullOrEmpty(sigCachePath) ? null : sigCachePath);
        var processResult = new ProcessScanResult();
        FileScanResult? fileResult = null;
        DmaScanResult? dmaResult = null;
        VisionScanResult? visionResult = null;
        DeepScanResult? deepResult = null;
        bool timedOut = false;

        try
        {
            processResult = new ProcessScanner(signatureChecker).Scan(ruleSet, cts.Token, audit, ruleSet.Vision.Enabled && options.VisionEnabled);

            if (options.FileScanEnabled)
            {
                fileResult = new FileScanner().Scan(ruleSet, options.FileRoots, cts.Token, audit);
            }

            if (options.DmaEnabled && ruleSet.Dma.Enabled)
            {
                Func<List<DmaDeviceInfo>, ConfigSpaceReadResult>? configReader = null;

                if (options.KernelEnabled)
                {
                    var kernelOptions = ruleSet.Dma.Kernel;
                    string driverPath = options.DriverPath ?? Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, kernelOptions.DriverFileName);
                    configReader = devices => ConfigSpaceProvider.ReadAll(
                        devices, driverPath, kernelOptions.ServiceName, kernelOptions.DevicePath);
                }

                dmaResult = new DmaScanner().Scan(ruleSet.Dma, cts.Token, ruleSet, configReader);

                if (dmaResult.Kernel is { } kernel)
                {
                    audit.DriverEvent("load", ruleSet.Dma.Kernel.ServiceName, kernel.DriverPath,
                        kernel.Loaded, kernel.Error);
                    if (kernel.Loaded)
                    {
                        audit.DriverEvent("unload", ruleSet.Dma.Kernel.ServiceName, kernel.DriverPath,
                            kernel.Unloaded, kernel.Error);
                    }
                }
            }
            if (options.VisionEnabled && ruleSet.Vision.Enabled)
            {
                visionResult = new VisionScanner().Scan(
                    ruleSet, processResult, dmaResult?.Devices, cts.Token, audit, options.VisionBehavior);
            }

            if (options.DeepEnabled)
            {
                deepResult = ExtendedScans.Run(ruleSet, processResult, new DeepScanRequest
                {
                    Handles = options.HandleScan,
                    Drivers = options.DriverScan,
                    Overlay = options.OverlayScan,
                    FileSignatures = options.FileSignatureScan,
                    FileRoots = options.FileRoots.ToList()
                }, signatureChecker, cts.Token, audit);

                if (visionResult is not null && deepResult.Overlays.Count > 0)
                {
                    visionResult.SoftwareCapture.AddRange(deepResult.Overlays);
                    VisionScanner.Aggregate(ruleSet.Vision, visionResult);
                }
            }
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException)
        {
            timedOut = true;
        }

        signatureChecker.SaveCache();
        stopwatch.Stop();

        // ---- 命中分区：白名单抑制项不参与聚合，但保留记录 ----
        var allFindings = new List<Finding>();
        allFindings.AddRange(processResult.Findings);
        if (fileResult is not null)
        {
            allFindings.AddRange(fileResult.Findings);
        }
        if (deepResult is not null)
        {
            allFindings.AddRange(deepResult.Findings);
        }

        // 统一后置白名单：让句柄/驱动/overlay/特征码的命中也能被白名单抑制
        WhitelistFilter.Apply(ruleSet, allFindings);

        var activeFindings = allFindings.Where(f => !f.Suppressed).ToList();
        var suppressedFindings = allFindings.Where(f => f.Suppressed).ToList();
        activeFindings.Sort((a, b) => b.Score.CompareTo(a.Score));
        suppressedFindings.Sort((a, b) => b.Score.CompareTo(a.Score));

        var dmaFindings = dmaResult?.Findings ?? new List<DmaFinding>();
        var activeDma = dmaFindings.Where(f => !f.Suppressed).ToList();

        // ---- 风险聚合（进程/文件 + DMA 设备统一评估）----
        var aggregateInput = new List<Finding>(activeFindings);
        aggregateInput.AddRange(activeDma.Select(ToAggregateFinding));
        var assessments = RiskAggregator.Assess(aggregateInput);
        var (overallScore, overallLevel) = RiskAggregator.Overall(assessments);

        var (dmaScore, dmaLevel) = AggregateDma(activeDma);

        // 游戏侧遥测：离线文件导入，不联网
        TelemetryReport? telemetryReport = null;
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(options.TelemetryPath) && ruleSet.Telemetry.Enabled)
        {
            telemetryReport = TelemetryCorrelator.Load(options.TelemetryPath!, ruleSet.Telemetry, overallLevel);
            audit.Write("telemetry.load", new
            {
                path = telemetryReport.SourcePath,
                loaded = telemetryReport.Loaded,
                sessions = telemetryReport.Sessions,
                localLevel = telemetryReport.LocalLevel,
                jointLevel = telemetryReport.JointLevel,
                note = "离线文件导入，未发起任何网络请求"
            });
        }

        var notChecked = new List<NotChecked>();
        int unreadable = processResult.NotChecked.Count(n => n.Reason.Contains("拒绝访问", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
        notChecked.AddRange(processResult.NotChecked);
        if (fileResult is not null) notChecked.AddRange(fileResult.NotChecked);
        if (dmaResult is not null) notChecked.AddRange(dmaResult.NotChecked);
        if (deepResult is not null) notChecked.AddRange(deepResult.NotChecked);
        if (timedOut)
        {
            notChecked.Add(new NotChecked { Target = "scan", Reason = $"扫描超时（{options.TimeoutSeconds}s），结果不完整" });
        }

        var report = new ScanReport
        {
            Tool = "EagleEye",
            Version = ToolVersion,
            ScanStartedUtc = startedUtc.ToString("o"),
            ScanFinishedUtc = DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("o"),
            DurationSeconds = Math.Round(stopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, 2),
            Machine = Environment.MachineName,
            OsVersion = RuntimeInformation.OSDescription,
            UserName = Environment.UserName,
            IsElevated = IsElevated(),
            Ruleset = new RuleSetSummary
            {
                Name = ruleSet.Meta.Name,
                Version = ruleSet.Meta.Version,
                Updated = ruleSet.Meta.Updated,
                SchemaVersion = ruleSet.Meta.SchemaVersion,
                Path = load.SourcePath,
                RuleCount = ruleSet.Rules.Count + ruleSet.Dma.Rules.Count,
                EnabledRuleCount = ruleSet.Rules.Count(r => r.Enabled) + ruleSet.Dma.Rules.Count(r => r.Enabled),
                WhitelistCount = RuleLoader.CountWhitelistEntries(ruleSet.Whitelist) + ruleSet.Dma.Whitelist.Count,
                Categories = ruleSet.Meta.Categories.Select(c => $"{c.Id}:{c.Name}").ToList(),
                Warnings = load.Warnings.ToList()
            },
            Audit = new AuditSummary
            {
                LogPath = audit.LogPath,
                ProcessOpenAttempts = audit.ProcessOpenAttempts,
                ProcessOpenDenied = audit.ProcessOpenDenied,
                SignatureChecks = audit.SignatureChecks,
                SignatureCacheNew = signatureChecker.NewEntries,
                SignatureCacheSize = signatureChecker.CacheSize,
                DriverLoadAttempts = audit.DriverLoadAttempts,
                DriverLoaded = audit.DriverLoaded,
                DriverUnloaded = audit.DriverUnloaded,
                NetworkUsed = false
            },
            Findings = activeFindings,
            SuppressedFindings = suppressedFindings,
            Assessments = assessments,
            NotChecked = notChecked,
            DmaUserModeFindings = dmaFindings,
            Handles = deepResult?.Handles ?? new HandleScanReport(),
            Drivers = deepResult?.Drivers ?? new DriverScanReport(),
            FileSignatures = deepResult?.FileSignatures ?? new FileSignatureReport(),
            InjectionTraces = deepResult?.InjectionTraces ?? new List<InjectionFindingInfo>(),
            Telemetry = telemetryReport ?? new TelemetryReport { LocalLevel = overallLevel, JointLevel = overallLevel },

            VisionCheatIndicators = new VisionReport
            {
                Enabled = visionResult?.Enabled ?? false,
                SoftwareCapture = visionResult?.SoftwareCapture ?? new List<VisionIndicator>(),
                HardwareIndicators = visionResult?.HardwareIndicators ?? new List<VisionIndicator>(),
                BehavioralHint = visionResult?.BehavioralHint ?? new VisionBehavioralHint(),
                IndependentLayers = visionResult?.IndependentLayers ?? 0,
                HighestLevel = visionResult?.HighestLevel ?? RiskLevels.Info,
                Notes = visionResult?.Notes ?? new List<string>(),
                LayerSummary = new Dictionary<string, int>
                {
                    ["capture"] = visionResult?.SoftwareCapture.Count(i => !i.Suppressed) ?? 0,
                    ["hardware"] = visionResult?.HardwareIndicators.Count(i => !i.Suppressed) ?? 0,
                    ["behavior"] = (visionResult?.BehavioralHint.Collected ?? false) ? 1 : 0
                }
            },
            Dma = new DmaReport
            {
                Enabled = dmaResult?.Enabled ?? false,
                DevicesEnumerated = dmaResult?.DevicesEnumerated ?? 0,
                PciDevices = dmaResult?.Devices.Count ?? 0,
                Findings = activeDma.Count,
                TopScore = dmaScore,
                RiskLevel = dmaLevel,
                Kernel = ToKernelReport(dmaResult?.Kernel),
                Devices = options.IncludeDmaDeviceList
                    ? (dmaResult?.Devices ?? new List<DmaDeviceInfo>())
                    : new List<DmaDeviceInfo>()
            },
            Processes = options.AllProcesses ? processResult.Processes : null
        };

        report.Summary = new ScanSummary
        {
            ProcessesScanned = processResult.Processes.Count,
            FilesScanned = fileResult?.FilesScanned ?? 0,
            FilesHashed = fileResult?.FilesHashed ?? 0,
            Findings = activeFindings.Count,
            SuppressedFindings = suppressedFindings.Count,
            TotalScore = overallScore,
            SumScore = Math.Round(Math.Min(100.0, activeFindings.Sum(f => f.Score)), 2),
            RiskLevel = overallLevel,
            NotCheckedCount = notChecked.Count,
            AssessedTargets = assessments.Count,
            FileScanEnabled = options.FileScanEnabled,
            DmaDevicesEnumerated = dmaResult?.DevicesEnumerated ?? 0,
            DmaPciDevices = dmaResult?.Devices.Count ?? 0,
            DmaFindings = activeDma.Count,
            DmaRiskLevel = dmaLevel,
            DmaKernelLoaded = dmaResult?.Kernel?.Loaded ?? false,
            VisionSoftwareIndicators = visionResult?.SoftwareCapture.Count(i => !i.Suppressed) ?? 0,
            VisionHardwareIndicators = visionResult?.HardwareIndicators.Count(i => !i.Suppressed) ?? 0,
            VisionLevel = visionResult?.HighestLevel ?? RiskLevels.Info,
            HandleFindings = deepResult?.Findings.Count(f => f.RuleType == "handle_access") ?? 0,
            DriverFindings = deepResult?.Findings.Count(f => f.RuleType == "kernel_driver") ?? 0,
            FileSignatureHits = deepResult?.FileSignatures.Hits.Count ?? 0,
            OverlayIndicators = deepResult?.Overlays.Count ?? 0,
            TelemetryLoaded = telemetryReport?.Loaded ?? false,
            PartialCoverage = !IsElevated(),
            UnreadableTargets = unreadable,
            TelemetryJointLevel = telemetryReport?.JointLevel ?? overallLevel,
            ByRiskLevel = assessments
                .GroupBy(a => a.RiskLevel)
                .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.Count())
        };

        foreach (string level in new[] { RiskLevels.Info, RiskLevels.Suspicious, RiskLevels.High })
        {
            report.Summary.ByRiskLevel.TryAdd(level, 0);
        }

        // ---- 输出 ----
        var written = new List<string>();
        string stamp = DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd-HHmmss");
        string jsonPath = options.OutputPath ?? Path.Combine(OutputRoot(), "reports", $"scan-{stamp}.json");
        string htmlPath = options.HtmlPath ?? Path.Combine(OutputRoot(), "reports", $"scan-{stamp}.html");

        if (options.Format is "json" or "both")
        {
            written.Add(ReportWriter.WriteJson(report, jsonPath));
        }

        if (options.Format is "html" or "both")
        {
            written.Add(ReportWriter.WriteHtml(report, htmlPath));
        }

        audit.Write("scan.end", new
        {
            durationSeconds = report.DurationSeconds,
            riskLevel = overallLevel,
            score = overallScore,
            activeFindings = activeFindings.Count,
            suppressed = suppressedFindings.Count,
            assessments = assessments.Count,
            outputs = written
        });

        PrintResults(report, options, written);

        return Scoring.Rank(overallLevel) switch
        {
            >= 2 => 3,
            1 => 2,
            _ => 0
        };
    }

    /// <summary>把 DMA 命中转成统一 Finding，参与同一套风险聚合与证据链。</summary>
    private static Finding ToAggregateFinding(DmaFinding d) => new()
    {
        RuleId = d.RuleId,
        RuleType = "dma",
        Category = d.Category,
        Target = d.Target,
        TargetPath = d.InstanceId,
        Pid = null,
        Severity = d.Severity,
        Confidence = d.Confidence,
        Score = d.Score,
        RiskLevel = d.RiskLevel,
        Description = d.Description,
        Advice = d.Advice,
        Evidence = d.Evidence
    };

    private static void PrintResults(ScanReport report, Options options, List<string> written)
    {
        if (options.Quiet)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"[{report.Summary.RiskLevel}] targets={report.Summary.AssessedTargets} " +
                              $"findings={report.Summary.Findings} suppressed={report.Summary.SuppressedFindings} " +
                              $"score={report.Summary.TotalScore} dma={report.Summary.DmaFindings} " +
                              $"outputs={string.Join(";", written)}");
            return;
        }

        Console.WriteLine();
        Console.WriteLine("================ 扫描结果 ================");
        Console.WriteLine($"主机      : {report.Machine}  ({report.OsVersion})");
        Console.WriteLine($"管理员    : {(report.IsElevated ? "是" : "否（部分进程不可查，已在未检查项注明）")}");
        if (!report.IsElevated)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("            ⚠ 未提权：游戏进程（受 ACE 保护）的模块/句柄读不到，本次覆盖不完整");
            Console.WriteLine("            ⚠ 现场扫描请用「以管理员身份运行」，否则注入痕迹与句柄线索会全部落空");
        }
        Console.WriteLine($"耗时      : {report.DurationSeconds}s");
        Console.WriteLine($"规则集    : {report.Ruleset.Name} v{report.Ruleset.Version}（更新 {report.Ruleset.Updated ?? "-"}）");
        Console.WriteLine($"进程/文件 : {report.Summary.ProcessesScanned} 个进程 / {report.Summary.FilesScanned} 个文件（哈希 {report.Summary.FilesHashed}）");
        Console.WriteLine($"DMA 设备  : {report.Summary.DmaDevicesEnumerated} 个，其中 PCI/PCIe {report.Summary.DmaPciDevices} 个");
        Console.WriteLine($"总体风险  : {report.Summary.RiskLevel}（最高目标分 {report.Summary.TotalScore} / 100，阈值 40 可疑 / 70 高度可疑）");
        Console.WriteLine($"目标分布  : " + string.Join("  ", report.Summary.ByRiskLevel.Select(kv => $"{kv.Key} {kv.Value}")));
        Console.WriteLine($"命中/抑制 : {report.Summary.Findings} 条活跃 / {report.Summary.SuppressedFindings} 条被白名单抑制");
        Console.WriteLine($"未检查项  : {report.Summary.NotCheckedCount}");

        var visible = report.Assessments
            .Where(a => Scoring.Rank(a.RiskLevel) >= Scoring.Rank(options.MinLevel))
            .Take(10)
            .ToList();

        if (visible.Count > 0)
        {
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine("---- 风险评定（按等级/分值排序，最多 10 个目标）----");
            foreach (var a in visible)
            {
                string who = a.Pid.HasValue ? $"PID {a.Pid}" : "文件/设备";
                Console.WriteLine($"[{a.RiskLevel}] {a.Score,6:F1}  {a.Target} ({who})");
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(a.Path))
                {
                    Console.WriteLine($"           {a.Path}");
                }

                Console.WriteLine($"           信号: 强 {a.StrongSignalCount} / 中 {a.MediumSignalCount} / 弱 {a.WeakSignalCount}");

                foreach (string reason in a.EscalationReasons)
                {
                    Console.WriteLine($"           ↑ {reason}");
                }

                int index = 1;
                foreach (var e in a.Evidence.Take(6))
                {
                    Console.WriteLine($"           {index++}. {e.RuleId} (sev {e.Severity}, conf {e.Confidence:F2}) → {e.Score:F1}  {e.Description}");
                }

                if (a.Evidence.Count > 6)
                {
                    Console.WriteLine($"           ... 另 {a.Evidence.Count - 6} 条证据见报告");
                }
            }

            if (report.Assessments.Count > visible.Count)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"... 另有 {report.Assessments.Count - visible.Count} 个目标，详见报告");
            }
        }
        else
        {
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine("没有达到当前显示等级（--min-level）的目标。");
        }

        if (report.SuppressedFindings.Count > 0)
        {
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine($"---- 白名单抑制记录（{report.SuppressedFindings.Count} 条，不计分）----");
            foreach (var f in report.SuppressedFindings.Take(5))
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"  {f.RuleId}  {f.Target}  原因: {f.SuppressReason}");
            }
            if (report.SuppressedFindings.Count > 5)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"  ... 另 {report.SuppressedFindings.Count - 5} 条见报告");
            }
        }

        if (report.Dma.Enabled)
        {
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine($"---- DMA 外设（用户态初筛，等级 {report.Dma.RiskLevel}）----");
            Console.WriteLine($"枚举设备 {report.Dma.DevicesEnumerated} 个 / PCI {report.Dma.PciDevices} 个 / 命中 {report.Dma.Findings} 条");

            if (report.Dma.Kernel.Attempted)
            {
                string state = report.Dma.Kernel.Loaded
                    ? (report.Dma.Kernel.Unloaded ? "已加载并已卸载" : "已加载，卸载失败！")
                    : $"加载失败：{report.Dma.Kernel.Error}";
                Console.WriteLine($"内核配置空间: {state}，读取 {report.Dma.Kernel.DevicesRead}/{report.Dma.Kernel.DevicesAttempted} 个设备");
            }
            else
            {
                Console.WriteLine("内核配置空间: 未启用（加 --kernel 才会装载驱动）");
            }

            Console.WriteLine("注意: 用户态枚举读不到 PCIe 配置空间，DMA 段仅为线索，不能作为唯一依据。");
        }

        Console.WriteLine();
        if (report.VisionCheatIndicators.Enabled)
        {
            var vision = report.VisionCheatIndicators;
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine($"---- 视觉外挂线索（等级 {vision.HighestLevel}，{vision.IndependentLayers} 层独立线索）----");
            Console.WriteLine($"软件采集 {report.Summary.VisionSoftwareIndicators} 条 / 硬件 {report.Summary.VisionHardwareIndicators} 条 / " +
                              $"行为提示 {(vision.BehavioralHint.Collected ? "已采集（信息级）" : "未启用")}");

            foreach (var item in vision.SoftwareCapture.Take(5))
            {
                string wl = item.Suppressed ? $"  [白名单: {item.SuppressReason}]" : "";
                Console.WriteLine($"[{item.RiskLevel}] {item.Score,6:F1}  {item.Id}  {item.Target} (PID {item.Pid}){wl}");
                Console.WriteLine($"           模块: {string.Join(", ", item.MatchedModules.Take(4))}");
                Console.WriteLine($"           签名: {item.SignatureState}   窗口: {item.Evidence.GetValueOrDefault("largestWindow")}");
            }

            foreach (var item in vision.HardwareIndicators.Take(5))
            {
                string wl = item.Suppressed ? $"  [白名单: {item.SuppressReason}]" : "";
                Console.WriteLine($"[{item.RiskLevel}] {item.Score,6:F1}  {item.Id}  {item.Target}{wl}");
                Console.WriteLine($"           VID:PID {item.Evidence.GetValueOrDefault("vendorId")}:{item.Evidence.GetValueOrDefault("deviceId")}" +
                                  $"  Class {item.Evidence.GetValueOrDefault("deviceClass")}  Service {item.Evidence.GetValueOrDefault("service")}");
            }

            if (vision.BehavioralHint.Collected)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"行为提示: {vision.BehavioralHint.Description}");
            }

            foreach (string note in vision.Notes.Take(3))
            {
                Console.WriteLine("  · " + note);
            }

            Console.WriteLine("声明: 双机+外部采集+硬件注入链路在本机用户态无法独立确证，以上为可观测线索，不代表最终判定。");
        }

        if (report.Telemetry.Loaded)
        {
            var tel = report.Telemetry;
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine("---- 游戏侧遥测（联合评估）----");
            Console.WriteLine($"本机等级 {tel.LocalLevel} / 遥测等级 {(tel.Assessments.Count > 0 ? tel.Assessments[0].TelemetryLevel : "信息")} → 联合建议 {tel.JointLevel}");

            foreach (var s in tel.Assessments.Take(2))
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"会话 {s.SessionId}（玩家 {s.AccountName ?? s.PlayerId ?? "-"}，{s.DurationSeconds}s，信号 {s.Signals.Count} 条）");
                foreach (var signal in s.Signals.Take(4))
                {
                    string baseText = signal.BaselineKnown ? $"，基线 {signal.Baseline}" : "";
                    Console.WriteLine($"  [{signal.RiskLevel}] {signal.RuleId}  {signal.Metric}={signal.Value}（阈值 {signal.Threshold}{baseText}）");
                }
            }

            foreach (string reason in tel.JointReasons.Take(4))
            {
                Console.WriteLine("  · " + reason);
            }

            Console.WriteLine("说明: 遥测只反映行为异常，本地只反映痕迹，联合结论用于排序复核优先级，不构成判定。");
        }
        else if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(report.Telemetry.Error))
        {
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine("---- 游戏侧遥测 ----");
            Console.WriteLine("导入失败: " + report.Telemetry.Error);
        }

        if (report.Handles.Enabled || report.Drivers.Enabled)
        {
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine("---- 深度线索（句柄 / 驱动 / 特征码）----");

            if (report.Handles.Enabled)
            {
                Console.WriteLine(report.Handles.Error is not null
                    ? $"句柄枚举: 失败（{report.Handles.Error}）"
                    : $"句柄枚举: 共 {report.Handles.HandlesEnumerated} 个，高权限 {report.Handles.HighAccessHandles} 个，命中 {report.Summary.HandleFindings} 条");
            }

            if (report.Drivers.Enabled)
            {
                Console.WriteLine(report.Drivers.Error is not null
                    ? $"驱动枚举: 失败（{report.Drivers.Error}）"
                    : $"已加载驱动: {report.Drivers.DriversEnumerated} 个，命中规则 {report.Summary.DriverFindings} 条");
            }

            if (report.FileSignatures.Enabled)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"文件特征码: 扫描 {report.FileSignatures.FilesScanned} 个文件，命中 {report.Summary.FileSignatureHits} 条");
            }

            foreach (var f in report.Findings.Where(x => x.RuleType is "handle_access" or "kernel_driver").Take(6))
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"[{f.RiskLevel}] {f.Score,6:F1}  {f.RuleId}  {f.Target}");
                Console.WriteLine($"           {f.Description}");
            }
        }

        Console.WriteLine("---- 审计 ----");
        Console.WriteLine($"进程打开 {report.Audit.ProcessOpenAttempts} 次（拒绝 {report.Audit.ProcessOpenDenied}），权限仅 0x1000 QUERY_LIMITED");
        Console.WriteLine($"验签 {report.Audit.SignatureChecks} 次（缓存新增 {report.Audit.SignatureCacheNew}，总量 {report.Audit.SignatureCacheSize}），驱动 {(report.Audit.DriverLoadAttempts == 0 ? "未加载" : (report.Audit.DriverLoaded ? "已加载并卸载" : "加载失败"))}，网络访问：无");
        Console.WriteLine($"审计日志: {report.Audit.LogPath ?? "-"}");

        Console.WriteLine();
        foreach (string path in written)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"报告: {path}");
        }
        Console.WriteLine("退出码: 0=未发现可疑  2=存在可疑  3=存在高度可疑  1=错误");
    }

    private static (double Score, string Level) AggregateDma(List<DmaFinding> findings)
    {
        double best = 0;
        foreach (var group in findings.GroupBy(f => f.InstanceId))
        {
            double sum = Math.Min(100.0, group.Sum(f => f.Score));
            if (sum > best) best = sum;
        }

        best = Math.Round(best, 2);
        return (best, Scoring.LevelOf(best));
    }

    private static DmaKernelReport ToKernelReport(ConfigSpaceReadResult? read)
    {
        if (read is null)
        {
            return new DmaKernelReport();
        }

        return new DmaKernelReport
        {
            Attempted = read.Attempted,
            Loaded = read.Loaded,
            Unloaded = read.Unloaded,
            DriverPath = read.DriverPath,
            Error = read.Error,
            DevicesAttempted = read.DevicesAttempted,
            DevicesRead = read.DevicesRead,
            Errors = read.Errors
        };
    }

    private static void ApplyProfile(Options options)
    {
        switch (options.Profile)
        {
            case "quick":
                options.HandleScan = false;
                options.DriverScan = true;
                options.OverlayScan = false;
                options.FileSignatureScan = false;
                break;
            case "deep":
                options.HandleScan = true;
                options.DriverScan = true;
                options.OverlayScan = true;
                options.FileSignatureScan = true;
                break;
            default:
                break;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 输出根目录：优先 exe 同目录下的 out\（便携分发时结果跟着程序走），
    /// 不可写时回退到当前工作目录。
    /// </summary>
    private static string OutputRoot()
    {
        try
        {
            string baseDir = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "out");
            Directory.CreateDirectory(baseDir);
            return baseDir;
        }
        catch
        {
            return Path.Combine(Directory.GetCurrentDirectory(), "out");
        }
    }

    private static string DefaultLogPath()
    {
        string stamp = DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd-HHmmss");
        return Path.Combine(OutputRoot(), "logs", $"audit-{stamp}.jsonl");
    }

    private static bool IsElevated()
    {
        try
        {
            using WindowsIdentity identity = WindowsIdentity.GetCurrent();
            return new WindowsPrincipal(identity).IsInRole(WindowsBuiltInRole.Administrator);
        }
        catch
        {
            return false;
        }
    }

    private static void PrintRuleInfo(RuleLoadResult load)
    {
        Console.WriteLine($"规则文件 : {load.SourcePath}");
        if (load.Success)
        {
            var rs = load.RuleSet;
            Console.WriteLine($"规则集   : {rs.Meta.Name} v{rs.Meta.Version}（更新 {rs.Meta.Updated ?? "-"}，schema {rs.Meta.SchemaVersion}）");
            Console.WriteLine($"规则数量 : 通用 {rs.Rules.Count}（启用 {rs.Rules.Count(r => r.Enabled)}），DMA {rs.Dma.Rules.Count}，" +
                              $"分类 {rs.Meta.Categories.Count}，白名单 {RuleLoader.CountWhitelistEntries(rs.Whitelist) + rs.Dma.Whitelist.Count} 条");
        }

        foreach (string w in load.Warnings)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("  [警告] " + w);
        }
    }

    internal sealed class Options
    {
        public string? RulesPath { get; set; }
        public string? RulesDir { get; set; }
        public string? OutputPath { get; set; }
        public string? HtmlPath { get; set; }
        public string? LogPath { get; set; }
        public string Format { get; set; } = "both";
        public bool FileScanEnabled { get; set; } = true;
        public bool DmaEnabled { get; set; } = true;
        public bool KernelEnabled { get; set; }
        public string? DriverPath { get; set; }
        public bool IncludeDmaDeviceList { get; set; } = true;
        public bool VisionEnabled { get; set; } = true;
        public bool VisionBehavior { get; set; }
        public bool DeepEnabled { get; set; } = true;
        public bool HandleScan { get; set; } = true;
        public bool DriverScan { get; set; } = true;
        public bool OverlayScan { get; set; } = true;
        public bool FileSignatureScan { get; set; }
        public string Profile { get; set; } = "standard";
        public string? TelemetryPath { get; set; }
        public string? SigCachePath { get; set; }
        public string? AggregateDir { get; set; }
        public string? AggregateMd { get; set; }
        public string? AggregateJson { get; set; }
        public bool WithTune { get; set; }
        public string TuneLabel { get; set; } = "clean";
        public string? TuneOutputPath { get; set; }
        public bool NoWhitelist { get; set; }
        public bool AllProcesses { get; set; }
        public bool CheckRevocation { get; set; }
        public bool Quiet { get; set; }
        public List<string> FileRoots { get; } = new();
        public int TimeoutSeconds { get; set; } = 180;
        public string MinLevel { get; set; } = RiskLevels.Info;
    }

    private static Options ParseOptions(string[] args)
    {
        var options = new Options();
        int start = args.Length > 0 && !args[0].StartsWith("-", StringComparison.Ordinal) ? 1 : 0;

        for (int i = start; i < args.Length; i++)
        {
            string arg = args[i];
            string? inline = null;

            int eq = arg.IndexOf('=');
            if (arg.StartsWith("--", StringComparison.Ordinal) && eq > 0)
            {
                inline = arg[(eq + 1)..];
                arg = arg[..eq];
            }

            string? TakeValue()
            {
                if (inline is not null) return inline;
                return i + 1 < args.Length ? args[++i] : null;
            }

            switch (arg.ToLowerInvariant())
            {
                case "--rules": options.RulesPath = TakeValue(); break;
                case "--rules-dir": options.RulesDir = TakeValue(); break;
                case "--out": options.OutputPath = TakeValue(); break;
                case "--html": options.HtmlPath = TakeValue(); break;
                case "--log": options.LogPath = TakeValue(); break;
                case "--format":
                    options.Format = (TakeValue() ?? "both").ToLowerInvariant() switch
                    {
                        "json" => "json",
                        "html" => "html",
                        _ => "both"
                    };
                    break;
                case "--file-root":
                    string? root = TakeValue();
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(root)) options.FileRoots.Add(root!);
                    break;
                case "--no-files": options.FileScanEnabled = false; break;
                case "--no-vision": options.VisionEnabled = false; break;
                case "--vision-behavior": options.VisionBehavior = true; break;
                case "--no-handles": options.HandleScan = false; break;
                case "--no-drivers": options.DriverScan = false; break;
                case "--no-overlay": options.OverlayScan = false; break;
                case "--file-signatures": options.FileSignatureScan = true; break;
                case "--profile": options.Profile = (TakeValue() ?? "standard").ToLowerInvariant(); break;
                case "--telemetry": options.TelemetryPath = TakeValue(); break;
                case "--dir": options.AggregateDir = TakeValue(); break;
                case "--label": options.TuneLabel = TakeValue() ?? "clean"; break;
                case "--tune": options.WithTune = true; break;
                case "--no-whitelist": options.NoWhitelist = true; break;
                case "--tune-out": options.TuneOutputPath = TakeValue(); break;
                case "--json": options.AggregateJson = TakeValue(); break;
                case "--sig-cache": options.SigCachePath = TakeValue(); break;
                case "--no-sig-cache": options.SigCachePath = ""; break;
                case "--no-dma": options.DmaEnabled = false; break;
                case "--kernel": options.KernelEnabled = true; break;
                case "--driver-path": options.DriverPath = TakeValue(); break;
                case "--no-device-list": options.IncludeDmaDeviceList = false; break;
                case "--all-processes": options.AllProcesses = true; break;
                case "--check-revocation": options.CheckRevocation = true; break;
                case "--quiet": options.Quiet = true; break;
                case "--timeout":
                    if (int.TryParse(TakeValue(), out int timeout)) options.TimeoutSeconds = timeout;
                    break;
                case "--min-level":
                    options.MinLevel = TakeValue()?.ToLowerInvariant() switch
                    {
                        "suspicious" => RiskLevels.Suspicious,
                        "high" => RiskLevels.High,
                        _ => RiskLevels.Info
                    };
                    break;
                default:
                    if (arg.StartsWith("-", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                    {
                        Console.Error.WriteLine($"[警告] 未识别的参数: {arg}");
                    }
                    break;
            }
        }

        return options;
    }

    private static string ResolveRulesPath(string? explicitPath)
    {
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(explicitPath))
        {
            return explicitPath!;
        }

        string baseDir = AppContext.BaseDirectory;
        string cwd = Directory.GetCurrentDirectory();

        string[] candidates =
        {
            Path.Combine(baseDir, "rules", "rules.json"),
            Path.Combine(cwd, "rules", "rules.json")
        };

        foreach (string candidate in candidates)
        {
            if (File.Exists(candidate))
            {
                return candidate;
            }
        }

        return candidates[0];
    }

    private static void PrintHelp()
    {
        Console.WriteLine($"""
        EagleEye v{ToolVersion} —— 本地反外挂只读查询工具（进程 / 文件 / 签名 / DMA）

        用法:
          EagleEye scan     [选项]   # 默认命令
          EagleEye validate [选项]   # 只校验规则文件
          EagleEye selftest [选项]   # 环境自检（权限/规则/句柄/驱动/目标进程）
          EagleEye aggregate --dir <报告目录> [--tune]  # 批量汇总；加 --tune 顺带生成调参包
          EagleEye tune --dir <报告目录>                # 只做调参分析（clean-*/cheat-* 标签）
          EagleEye --help

        报告输出:
          --format <json|html|both>  输出格式，默认 both
          --out  <路径>              JSON 报告路径
          --html <路径>              HTML 报告路径
          --log  <路径>              审计日志路径（JSONL）

        扫描范围:
          --no-files                 跳过文件扫描
          --no-dma                   跳过 DMA 外设枚举
          --no-vision                跳过 AI 视觉外挂线索扫描
          --vision-behavior          采集输入行为统计（信息级，默认关闭）
          --no-handles               跳过句柄线索（谁以 VM_READ 打开目标进程）
          --no-drivers               跳过已加载驱动线索
          --no-overlay               跳过 overlay 窗口检测
          --file-signatures          启用文件特征码扫描（默认关闭，需规则）
          --profile <quick|standard|deep>  扫描档位预设
          --telemetry <文件>        导入游戏侧遥测 JSON，做本地×遥测联合评估（离线文件，不联网）
          --kernel                   加载一次性只读驱动读取 PCIe 配置空间（用完立即卸载）
          --driver-path <路径>       指定 PcieCfg.sys
          --no-device-list           报告不附全部 PCI 设备清单
          --file-root <目录>         追加文件扫描目录，可重复
          --all-processes            报告附全部进程清单
          --check-revocation         验签时检查吊销（需联网，默认关闭）

        其他:
          --timeout <秒>             整体超时，默认 180
          --min-level <等级>         控制台只显示 >= 该等级：info | suspicious | high
          --quiet                    只输出一行摘要

        退出码: 0=未发现可疑  2=存在可疑  3=存在高度可疑  1=错误

        安全约束: 只读枚举；不读进程内存、不注入、不 Hook、不联网、不上传数据。
        """ );
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/README.md`:

```md
# EagleEye

本地反外挂**只读**查询工具（Windows x64 CLI）。扫描本机进程、文件、数字签名，按外置 JSON 规则匹配，输出带证据链和风险等级的 JSON 报告。

- 不注入、不 Hook、不读目标进程内存、不加载驱动、不改系统配置
- 输出的不是"作弊判定"，而是"检测到某特征 + 证据 + 置信度"，风险分三级：`信息` / `可疑` / `高度可疑`

---

## 1. 环境要求

| 项目 | 要求 |
|---|---|
| 系统 | Windows 10 / 11 x64 |
| 运行时 | .NET 8（框架依赖模式）；或直接用 self-contained 发布包 |
| 构建 | .NET 8 SDK |

本机已安装用户级 SDK：`E:\dotnet8\dotnet.exe`（8.0.427）。

## 2. 构建

```powershell
# 常规构建
cd E:\查挂小程序\src\EagleEye
E:\dotnet8\dotnet.exe build -c Release

# 单文件发布（依赖框架，体积小，目标机需装 .NET 8 运行时）
E:\dotnet8\dotnet.exe publish -c Release -r win-x64 --self-contained false -p:PublishSingleFile=true -o E:\查挂小程序\out\publish

# 单文件发布（自带运行时，目标机无需装 .NET）
E:\dotnet8\dotnet.exe publish -c Release -r win-x64 --self-contained true -p:PublishSingleFile=true -o E:\查挂小程序\out\publish-standalone
```

产物：`bin\Release\net8.0-windows\EagleEye.exe`（`rules\` 目录会一起复制到输出目录）。

## 3. 运行

```powershell
# 校验规则文件（不扫描）
EagleEye.exe validate --rules rules\rules.json

# 进程 + 文件全量扫描，报告写到默认位置 out\reports\scan-<时间>.json
EagleEye.exe scan

# 只扫进程（快）
EagleEye.exe scan --no-files

# 指定规则与输出，附加扫描目录，只看高危
EagleEye.exe scan --rules rules\rules.json --out D:\r.json --file-root D:\tools --min-level suspicious

# 报告里附带全部进程清单（便于排查"为什么没命中"）
EagleEye.exe scan --all-processes --out D:\r.json
```

| 参数 | 说明 |
|---|---|
| `--rules <路径>` | 规则文件，默认 exe 目录下 `rules\rules.json` |
| `--out <路径>` | JSON 报告路径，默认 `out\reports\scan-<时间>.json` |
| `--no-files` | 跳过文件扫描 |
| `--no-dma` | 跳过 DMA 外设用户态枚举 |
| `--no-device-list` | 报告不附全部 PCI 设备清单（默认附） |
| `--file-root <目录>` | 追加文件扫描目录，可重复 |
| `--all-processes` | 报告附全部进程明细（默认不附，控制体积） |
| `--check-revocation` | 验签时检查证书吊销（需联网，较慢） |
| `--timeout <秒>` | 整体超时，默认 180 |
| `--min-level info\|suspicious\|high` | 控制台只显示 ≥ 该等级的命中 |
| `--quiet` | 只输出一行摘要 |

**退出码**：`0` 未发现可疑 / `2` 存在可疑 / `3` 存在高度可疑 / `1` 错误。便于接 CI 或批处理。

## 4. 规则文件

JSON 顶层两段：`rules` + `whitelist`（另有 `meta`、`options`）。

```json
{
  "meta": { "name": "默认规则集", "version": "1.0.0" },
  "options": {
    "scanRoots": ["%TEMP%", "%USERPROFILE%\\Downloads"],
    "includeExtensions": [".exe", ".dll", ".sys"],
    "maxFiles": 20000, "maxDepth": 5,
    "maxFileSizeBytes": 268435456, "maxHashFiles": 3000
  },
  "rules": [
    {
      "id": "proc.cheatengine",
      "type": "process_name",
      "pattern": "cheatengine*",
      "severity": 4,
      "confidence": 0.9,
      "description": "Cheat Engine 主程序在运行（内存扫描/调试工具）",
      "advice": "单独存在不构成作弊，需结合其他证据人工复核",
      "match": "glob",
      "enabled": true
    }
  ],
  "whitelist": {
    "processes": [{ "pattern": "explorer.exe", "match": "exact", "reason": "Windows 外壳" }],
    "paths":     [{ "pattern": "C:\\Windows\\System32\\*", "reason": "系统目录" }],
    "files":     [{ "pattern": "*.tmp", "reason": "临时文件" }],
    "signers":   [{ "subjectContains": "Microsoft Corporation", "reason": "微软签名" }]
  }
}
```

### 4.1 规则字段

| 字段 | 必填 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | ✅ | 唯一标识，重复会报错 |
| `type` | ✅ | 见下表 |
| `pattern` | ✅（`file_hash` 除外） | 匹配模式 |
| `severity` | ✅ | 1–5：1 提示 / 2 低 / 3 中 / 4 高 / 5 确定 |
| `confidence` | ✅ | 0–1，与该信号的可靠度相乘，避免"名字像就判死" |
| `description` | ✅ | 报告里显示的一句话说明 |
| `match` | 否 | `glob`（默认）/ `exact` / `contains` / `regex`，均忽略大小写 |
| `hash` | `file_hash` 必填 | 64 位 SHA256（小写十六进制） |
| `size` | 否 | `file_hash` 预筛：只有大小匹配才计算哈希，省 IO |
| `advice` | 否 | 给复核人的建议，写入报告 |
| `enabled` | 否 | 默认 `true`，写 `false` 可灰度下线单条规则 |

### 4.2 规则类型

| type | 作用对象 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `process_name` | 进程名 | 例：`cheatengine*` |
| `process_path` | 进程映像路径 | 例：`*\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\*` |
| `process_command_line` | 命令行 | 例：`--inject`（`match: contains`） |
| `module_name` | 进程已加载模块名 | 例：`aimbot*.dll`，命中后证据里列出全部命中模块 |
| `file_name` | 扫描到的文件名 | 例：`*speedhack*` |
| `file_hash` | 文件 SHA256 | 与 `hash` 字段比对 |
| `signature` | Authenticode 状态 | `pattern` 取 `unsigned` / `bad_digest` / `untrusted_root` / `expired` / `revoked` / `catalog_only` / `any_invalid` |

## 5. 如何新增白名单

白名单的目的是"确认无害后降噪"，命中白名单**不会删除命中项**，只是把该条分值衰减到 20% 并标记 `whitelisted: true` + 原因，保证可追溯。

### 5.1 按进程名/路径放行

```json
"whitelist": {
  "processes": [
    { "pattern": "OBS*.exe", "match": "glob", "reason": "直播软件，覆盖层合法" }
  ],
  "paths": [
    { "pattern": "D:\\Games\\AntiCheatPro\\\\*", "reason": "自家反作弊目录" }
  ]
}
```

- `pattern` 里含 `\` 或 `/` 时按**映像路径**匹配，否则按**进程名**匹配
- 可选 `"hash": "<sha256>"`：要求文件哈希也一致才放行（防止同名伪造，推荐用于高风险放行）
- 可选 `"expires": "2027-01-01"`：到期自动失效，过期条目启动时会打印告警

### 5.2 按签名主体放行

```json
"signers": [
  { "subjectContains": "Microsoft Corporation", "reason": "微软签名" }
]
```

只对**验签通过**的文件生效（`subjectContains` 与证书 Subject 做包含匹配）。

### 5.3 按文件放行

```json
"files": [
  { "pattern": "*.tmp", "reason": "临时文件" },
  { "hash": "<sha256>", "reason": "已确认无害的已知文件" }
]
```

### 5.4 新增白名单的标准流程

1. 先跑一次，看报告里被误报的 `target` / `targetPath`
2. 确认无害（人工核对签名、来源、哈希）
3. 在白名单对应段加条目，**写清 `reason`**，高风险条目务必带 `hash` 和 `expires`
4. `EagleEye.exe validate --rules rules\rules.json` 校验通过后再上线

## 6. 评分与风险等级

```
单条得分 = severity × 20 × confidence            （1–100）
白名单命中 = 单条得分 × 0.2
目标累计 = 该进程/该文件所有命中之和，封顶 100
整体风险 = 各目标累计分中的最高者
```

| 分值 | 等级 | 含义 |
|---|---|---|
| 0–39 | 信息 | 低置信提示，多半是签名/路径类弱信号 |
| 40–69 | 可疑 | 需要人工看一眼 |
| 70–100 | 高度可疑 | 多条强证据叠加，优先复核 |

按目标聚合而不是全局累加，避免"一堆 18 分的弱提示"被累加成高危。

## 7. 报告结构

```jsonc
{
  "tool": "EagleEye", "version": "1.0.0",
  "scanStartedUtc": "...", "durationSeconds": 2.43,
  "machine": "...", "osVersion": "...", "isElevated": false,
  "ruleset": { "name": "...", "version": "...", "ruleCount": 18, "whitelistCount": 11, "warnings": [] },
  "summary": {
    "processesScanned": 293, "filesScanned": 773, "filesHashed": 0,
    "findings": 4, "totalScore": 18.0, "sumScore": 72.0,
    "riskLevel": "信息", "byRiskLevel": { "信息": 4, "可疑": 0, "高度可疑": 0 },
    "notCheckedCount": 191
  },
  "findings": [
    {
      "ruleId": "sig.unsigned", "ruleType": "signature",
      "target": "xxx.exe", "targetPath": "C:\\...\\xxx.exe", "pid": 1234,
      "severity": 2, "confidence": 0.45, "score": 18.0, "riskLevel": "信息",
      "description": "...", "advice": "...",
      "evidence": { "matchedField": "authenticode", "matchedValue": "unsigned", "winTrustStatus": "0x800B0100", "catalogSigned": false },
      "whitelisted": false
    }
  ],
  "notChecked": [
    { "target": "svchost.exe", "pid": 984, "reason": "受保护进程或权限不足：仅完成名称级检查..." }
  ],
  "processes": null
}
```

`notChecked` 是关键字段：**没查到 ≠ 干净**，受保护进程、权限不足、目录不可读都会记在这里。

## 8. 安全边界（代码层面可审计）

只使用以下 API：

- 进程枚举：`CreateToolhelp32Snapshot` + `Process32First/Next`、`Module32First/Next`
- 进程信息：`OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION)`、`QueryFullProcessImageName`、`NtQueryInformationProcess(ProcessCommandLineInformation)`
- 签名：`WinVerifyTrust`、`CryptCATAdmin*`（目录签名）
- 文件：`FileStream` 只读 + `SHA256`

代码中**不存在**：`WriteProcessMemory`、`VirtualAllocEx`、`CreateRemoteThread`、`SetWindowsHookEx`、`ReadProcessMemory`、任何驱动加载/服务创建/注册表写入。

自检命令：

```powershell
Select-String -Path .\*.cs,.\**\*.cs -Pattern "WriteProcessMemory|CreateRemoteThread|SetWindowsHookEx|ReadProcessMemory|VirtualAllocEx|NtWriteVirtualMemory" -SimpleMatch:$false
# 期望：无输出
```

## 9. 已知限制

1. **受保护进程**（PPL / 反作弊进程）：`OpenProcess` 会被拒绝，只能拿到名称；报告 `notChecked` 里注明，绝不降级为强开。
2. **命令行**：走 `ProcessCommandLineInformation` 信息类查询，需要 `PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION`；仍拿不到就留空，不做内存读取兜底。
3. **目录签名**：只校验"存在 cat 签名"，不深入验证 cat 内容；如需严格校验可对 cat 路径单独跑 `WinVerifyTrust`。
4. **吊销检查**：默认关闭（离线快）；`--check-revocation` 打开后需联网，且可能明显变慢。
5. **文件扫描**：只覆盖 `options.scanRoots` + `--file-root`；达到 `maxFiles` 会截断并在 `notChecked` 说明。
6. 不是反作弊，不做实时监控；一次扫描是一份快照。

## 10. 目录

```
src\EagleEye\
├─ EagleEye.csproj
├─ Program.cs              CLI 入口、参数、汇总输出
├─ Models\                 Rule / RuleSet / Finding / ProcessRecord / ScanReport
├─ Native\                 Win32 + NT 互操作、Authenticode 验签
├─ Core\                   PatternMatcher / Hashing / Scoring
├─ Rules\                  规则加载与 JSON 校验
├─ Scanning\               ProcessScanner / FileScanner
├─ Reporting\              JSON 报告输出
└─ rules\rules.json        默认规则库
```

# 附：DMA 外设用户态初筛（阶段 2 新增）

## A. 做了什么 / 没做什么

| | 内容 |
|---|---|
| ✅ 做 | SetupAPI + CfgMgr32 只读枚举当前存在的设备；筛出 `PCI\` 实例；解析 VID/PID/SUBSYS/REV；读取设备名、设备类、驱动服务名、位置、厂商、问题码；与 DMA 规则比对并输出证据 |
| ❌ 不做 | 不加载驱动、不打开设备写句柄、不改设备状态、不访问 IOMMU 寄存器、不读写 PCIe 配置空间 |
| ⚠️ 关键限制 | **用户态读不到 PCIe 配置空间**：Capability 链表、BAR 大小、MSI/MSI-X、真实 Class Code、Subsystem 都拿不到；`classCode` 只是由"设备类名"近似推导的**猜测值**。因此 DMA 段结果 **不能作为 DMA 检测的唯一依据** |

代码里这段结论写死在两处：`Scanning\DmaScanner.cs` 的类注释、`DmaReport.Note` 字段（每次报告都会带上）。

## B. 输出字段

报告里新增两段：

```jsonc
"dmaUserModeFindings": [
  {
    "ruleId": "dma.xilinx.pid7024",
    "target": "PCI 设备 7024",
    "instanceId": "PCI\\VEN_10EE&DEV_7024&SUBSYS_00000000&REV_01\\4&1b2c3d&0&00E0",
    "vendorId": "10EE", "deviceId": "7024", "revision": "01", "classCodeGuess": "06",
    "severity": 5, "confidence": 0.75, "score": 75.0, "riskLevel": "高度可疑",
    "description": "Xilinx 7024 设备 ID，常见于市售 DMA 板卡",
    "advice": "与设备类、驱动服务名、位置信息一起看，勿单独定性",
    "whitelisted": false,
    "userModeOnly": true,
    "evidence": {
      "instanceId": "...", "hardwareIds": "...", "deviceClass": "System",
      "classCodeSource": "setupapi-derived-guess",
      "service": "xdma", "location": "PCI 总线 1、设备 0、功能 0",
      "manufacturer": "Xilinx", "problemCode": 0, "configSpaceAvailable": false
    }
  }
],
"dma": {
  "enabled": true, "userModeOnly": true,
  "note": "用户态枚举无法读取 PCIe 配置空间……本段结果仅为线索，不能作为 DMA 检测的唯一依据",
  "devicesEnumerated": 218, "pciDevices": 38,
  "findings": 1, "riskLevel": "高度可疑", "topScore": 75.0,
  "devices": [
    { "name": "AMD Radeon(TM) Graphics", "instanceId": "PCI\\VEN_1002&DEV_1681...",
      "vendorId": "1002", "deviceId": "1681", "subsystemId": "..." , "revision": "C7",
      "deviceClass": "Display", "classCodeGuess": "03", "service": "amdwddmg",
      "location": "PCI 总线 6、设备 0、功能 0", "manufacturer": "Advanced Micro Devices, Inc.",
      "problemCode": 0 }
  ]
}
```

`summary` 里同步新增：`dmaDevicesEnumerated`、`dmaPciDevices`、`dmaFindings`、`dmaRiskLevel`。

## C. 规则字段（新增 `dma` 段）

```json
"dma": {
  "enabled": true,
  "includeDeviceList": true,
  "rules": [
    {
      "id": "dma.xilinx.vendor",
      "vid": "10EE",                 // 必填之一：4 位十六进制，可带 0x
      "vendor": "Xilinx",            // 可选，仅用于报告可读性
      "severity": 4,                 // 1-5
      "confidence": 0.55,            // 0-1，用户态建议 ≤0.8
      "description": "Xilinx FPGA 厂商设备，是常见 DMA 采集卡的载板芯片",
      "advice": "单靠 VID 不足以定性……"
    },
    {
      "id": "dma.xilinx.pid7024",
      "vid": "10EE",
      "pid": "7024",                 // 可选，不填=该厂商全部设备
      "severity": 5,
      "confidence": 0.75,
      "description": "Xilinx 7024 设备 ID，常见于市售 DMA 板卡"
    },
    {
      "id": "dma.xilinx.rev01",
      "vid": "10EE", "pid": "7024", "revision": "01",   // 可选，精确匹配 REV
      "severity": 4, "confidence": 0.6,
      "description": "指定修订号的板卡批次"
    },
    {
      "id": "dma.class.system",
      "classCode": "06",             // 可选，近似 PCI 基础类（见下表），慎用
      "severity": 1, "confidence": 0.15, "enabled": false,
      "description": "按近似 Class Code 匹配（06=桥/系统类），默认关闭"
    }
  ],
  "whitelist": [
    { "vid": "8086", "reason": "Intel 芯片组 / 核显 / 网卡" },
    { "vid": "10DE", "pid": "2520", "reason": "已知型号显卡", "expires": "2027-01-01" }
  ]
}
```

**匹配语义**：一条规则里出现的字段（`vid` / `pid` / `revision` / `classCode`）必须**全部**与设备一致才命中；没写的字段不参与比较。校验器会拦下：severity 越界、confidence 越界、重复 id、vid/pid 非 4 位十六进制；并在 `severity>=4 && confidence>0.8` 时告警（用户态证据不该给这么高的置信度）。

**设备类 → 近似 Class Code（启发式，不是配置空间真实值）**

| 设备类 | 近似基础类 | 设备类 | 近似基础类 |
|---|---|---|---|
| Display / HIDClass | 03 | HDC / SCSIAdapter / DiskDrive | 01 |
| Net | 02 | Mouse / Keyboard | 09 |
| Media | 04 | Ports / Modem | 07 |
| System | 06 | USB / SmartCardReader | 0C |
| Memory | 05 | Bluetooth | 0D |
| Processor | 0B | SecurityDevices | 10 |
| 其它 | FF | | |

## D. 测试方法

### D1. 对照设备管理器做正确性验证

```powershell
# 枚举数量与名称对照
EagleEye.exe scan --no-files --out D:\dma.json
# 打开 设备管理器 → 查看 → 按连接排序，显卡/网卡/USB 控制器逐项核对 VID:PID 与位置
```

看 `dma.pciDevices` 是否与设备管理器里"PCI 总线上的设备数"接近（差几个通常是隐藏/未启动设备）。

### D2. 用本机真实设备造规则，验证命中链路

```json
{ "id": "dma.test.vga", "vid": "1002", "pid": "1681", "revision": "C7",
  "severity": 5, "confidence": 0.8, "description": "测试：VID/PID/REV 三段匹配" }
```

期望：`dmaUserModeFindings` 出现 1 条，score = 5×20×0.8 = 80 → `高度可疑`，退出码 3。
（这条规则就是本项目实测用的用例，见 `samples\verify\rules.dma.verify.json`）

### D3. 白名单衰减验证

把 `"whitelist": [{ "vid": "1002", "pid": "1681", "reason": "确认是显卡" }]` 加进去，重跑：

```
dma.test.vga | score=16 | 信息 | wl=true | 确认是显卡
```

分数变成原来的 20%，等级降为 `信息`，退出码回到 0 —— 与进程/文件白名单行为一致（衰减而不是删除，保证可审计）。

### D4. 负向测试（防误报回归）

把规则 `vid` 改成 `FFFF` 这种不存在的值，或把 `classCode` 改成 `FF`：

```powershell
EagleEye.exe scan --rules rules\invalid-vid.json --no-files --quiet
# 期望：dma=0(信息)，退出码 0
```

### D5. 想伪造一张 DMA 卡来测？

需要真板卡，或在虚拟机里挂一个 PCI 直通设备。**注意**：虚拟机里厂商 ID 往往被改写，测试价值有限；真实判定只能上物理机。别指望用软件"假装"出 Xilinx 设备，SetupAPI 读的是总线枚举结果。

### D6. classCode 规则的正确用法

只在"某厂商 + 某近似类"两条一起用时才给中等权重，例如：

```json
{ "id": "dma.fpga.system", "vid": "10EE", "classCode": "06", "severity": 3, "confidence": 0.5 }
```

单靠 `classCode` 会命中一大堆桥片/USB 控制器（本机实测就命中了显卡和网卡），必须配白名单。

## E. 接口清单（可审计）

只用到这些只读接口：`SetupDiGetClassDevsW`、`SetupDiEnumDeviceInfo`、`SetupDiDestroyDeviceInfoList`、`CM_Get_Device_IDW`、`CM_Get_DevNode_Registry_PropertyW`、`CM_Get_DevNode_Status`。

不存在的调用：`CreateFile(\\.\PCI...)`、`DeviceIoControl`、`SetupDiSetDeviceRegistryProperty`、`SetupDiSetClassInstallParams`、`CM_Reenumerate_DevNode`、任何 IOMMU/PCI 配置空间访问。
---

# 附：一次性只读 PCIe 配置空间驱动（阶段 3）

## A. 这是什么 / 风险在哪

一个 **DEMAND_START 的 WDM 驱动**，只在扫描期间存在：`CreateService → StartService → IOCTL → Stop → DeleteService`，读完立刻卸载。

**加载前必读**（详见 `docs\15_内核驱动风险说明.md`）：

1. 这是本工具**风险最高**的部分，默认关闭，不加 `--kernel` 完全不碰驱动。
2. **不要在 Vanguard / EAC / BattlEye / ACE / GameGuard 活跃期间加载**。推荐姿势：**先扫描、后启动游戏**。
3. 未签名驱动在 Secure Boot + HVCI 下无法加载（`StartService` 返回 577）；正式使用需 EV 签名 + Microsoft attestation。
4. 只在授权诊断机上使用，保留加载/卸载审计日志。

## B. 能力范围

| 输出项 | 是否可靠 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| VendorId / DeviceId / Revision | ✅ 真值 | 配置空间 0x00–0x0B |
| Class Code（Base/Sub/ProgIf） | ✅ 真值 | 0x09–0x0B，替代用户态的"近似猜测" |
| Subsystem Vendor / Device ID | ✅ 真值 | 0x2C–0x2F |
| Capability 链表 | ✅ 真值 | 从 0x34 起逐级遍历，最多 48 项 |
| MSI / MSI-X 存在性 + MSI Control | ✅ 真值 | Capability ID 0x05 / 0x11 |
| BAR 原始值 + 类型（IO/MEM/64bit/预取） | ✅ 真值 | 0x10–0x27 |
| **BAR 大小** | ❌ 不提供 | 确定大小必须写 `0xFFFFFFFF` 回读，**本驱动禁止写配置空间**，因此 `barSizeDeterminable` 恒为 `false` |

## C. 驱动与用户态的契约

```
设备名   \Device\PcieCfg      符号链接 \DosDevices\PcieCfg
IOCTL    IOCTL_PCIECFG_PING        0x80002001  返回协议版本 ULONG
         IOCTL_PCIECFG_READ_CONFIG 0x80002005  输入 PcieCfgRequest，输出 PcieCfgResult
权限     只允许 Token 已提权的调用方（SeQueryInformationToken），否则 STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED
```

请求结构（528 字节）：

```c
typedef struct _PCIECFG_REQUEST {
    WCHAR PdoPath[260];   // 如 \Device\NTPNP_PCI0007，由用户态 DEVPKEY_Device_PDOName 取得
    ULONG Offset;         // 只允许 0
    ULONG Length;         // 只允许 4..256，且 4 字节对齐
} PCIECFG_REQUEST;
```

响应结构（384 字节，全 4 字节对齐，与 C# `PcieCfgResult` 一一对应）：

```c
typedef struct _PCIECFG_RESULT {
    ULONG Status;            // NTSTATUS 原值
    ULONG Ids;               // VendorId | DeviceId<<16
    ULONG Subsystem;         // SubVendorId | SubsystemId<<16
    ULONG ClassInfo;         // Base<<24 | Sub<<16 | ProgIf<<8 | Revision
    ULONG HeaderType;
    ULONG Bar[6];
    ULONG BarFlags[6];       // bit0 IO / bit1 64bit / bit2 prefetch / bit3 非零
    ULONG CapCount;
    UCHAR CapIds[48];
    UCHAR HasMsi, HasMsix, CapabilityListOffset, Reserved0;
    ULONG MsiControl;
    UCHAR Raw[256];          // 原始配置空间，证据留痕
} PCIECFG_RESULT;
```

## D. 用法

```powershell
# 1) 先编译驱动（需要 WDK，见 native\pciecfg\build.md）
# 2) 把 PcieCfg.sys 放到 exe 同目录，或显式指定
EagleEye.exe scan --no-files --kernel --driver-path E:\path\PcieCfg.sys --out D:\r.json
```

报告新增：

```jsonc
"dma": {
  "kernel": {
    "attempted": true, "loaded": true, "unloaded": true,
    "driverPath": "...\\PcieCfg.sys", "error": null,
    "devicesAttempted": 38, "devicesRead": 37,
    "errors": ["PCI 设备 xxx: 驱动返回 0xC0000001"],
    "note": "驱动只在扫描期间存在……不要在 Vanguard / EAC / BattlEye 活跃期间加载"
  }
}
```

命中的 `dmaUserModeFindings[]` 里会多两个字段：

- `userModeOnly: false` —— 表示这条证据已经过真实配置空间校验
- `evidence.configSpace.{vendorId,classCode,capabilityIds,hasMsi,bars,barKinds,raw64Bytes}` —— 硬件真值

## E. 规则里怎么用配置空间条件

```json
{
  "id": "dma.fpga.no_msi",
  "vid": "10EE",
  "severity": 3,
  "confidence": 0.6,
  "conditions": ["msi_missing"],
  "description": "Xilinx FPGA 设备没有 MSI 能力"
}
```

支持的条件：`msi_missing`、`msix_missing`、`no_capability_list`、`bar0_zero`、`class_ff`、`revision_zero`。

**注意**：带 `conditions` 的规则在没启用 `--kernel` 时**不会触发**（没有配置空间数据就不做猜测），这正是"不拿近似值当证据"的体现。

## F. 卸载保证

```csharp
try {
    host.EnsureLoaded(driver, out err);
    device = host.OpenDevice(out err);
    foreach (var d in devices) PcieCfgIoctl.TryReadConfig(device, d.PdoName, out raw, out err);
} finally {
    device?.Dispose();
    host.TryUnload(out err);   // ControlService(STOP) + DeleteService
}
```

- 启动前先清理同名残留服务
- `finally` 里强制 stop + delete
- 卸载后 `sc query PcieCfg` 应为 1060（服务不存在）

## G. 实测（无 WDK 环境下的失败路径）

本机没有 WDK，驱动未编译，因此只验证了用户态的失败处理：

```
内核配置空间: 加载失败：驱动文件不存在: ...\PcieCfg.sys，读取 0/0 个设备
内核配置空间: 加载失败：OpenSCManager 失败（Win32=5 拒绝访问。），读取 0/0 个设备
sc query PcieCfg → FAILED 1060（未创建任何服务）
```

即：**驱动缺失 / 权限不足都不会影响其余扫描，也不会留下服务残留**。
---

# 附：规则引擎与报告（阶段 4）

## A. 规则文件的元信息与分类

```json
"meta": {
  "name": "EagleEye 默认规则集",
  "version": "1.3.0",          // 规则内容版本
  "updated": "2026-11-10",     // 规则内容更新时间
  "schemaVersion": "2.0",      // 格式版本
  "categories": [
    { "id": "tool.cheat",     "name": "作弊工具", "weight": 1.2 },
    { "id": "tool.injector",  "name": "注入器",   "weight": 1.3 },
    { "id": "path.anomaly",   "name": "路径异常", "weight": 0.8 }
  ]
}
```

每条规则通过 `category` 归属分类，分类的 `weight` 会乘进单条得分：

```
单条得分 = severity × 20 × confidence × categoryWeight
```

报告里会按分类统计，HTML 报告的证据条目也会带上分类标签。

## B. 白名单抑制（不是"降权"，是"抑制 + 留痕"）

```json
"whitelist": {
  "processes": [
    {
      "pattern": "ArmouryCrate.exe",
      "match": "exact",
      "reason": "已知外设厂商软件",
      "suppressRules": ["sig.unsigned"],   // 可选：只压制指定规则
      "expires": "2027-01-01"              // 可选：到期自动失效
    }
  ]
}
```

语义：

| 配置 | 行为 |
|---|---|
| `suppressRules` 省略或空数组 | 抑制该目标上**全部**规则命中 |
| `suppressRules: ["sig.unsigned"]` | 只抑制这一条，其他规则照常命中、照常计分 |

被抑制的命中：

- **不参与风险聚合**，不影响等级与得分；
- 仍完整写入报告 `suppressedFindings[]`，带 `suppressed: true`、`suppressReason`、`suppressedBy`、原始 `score`；
- HTML 报告里单独一节「白名单抑制记录」，方便定期复核白名单是否被滥用。

## C. 风险聚合规则（强/中/弱 + 组合升级）

信号分级：`severity 4-5` = 强，`3` = 中，`1-2` = 弱。

```
1) 同一目标（PID / 文件 / 设备）累加得分，封顶 100
2) 强信号直判：任一条 severity>=4 且单条 score>=70 → 高度可疑
3) 组合升级（分数还不够，但信号够多）：
     弱信号 >= 3                → 可疑
     弱 >= 2 且 中 >= 1         → 可疑
     中 >= 2                    → 可疑
4) 最终等级 = max(分值等级, 组合升级等级)
```

证据链按目标输出，包含每条规则的 id / 分类 / severity / confidence / 得分 / 原始证据，以及"等级是怎么升上去的"。

## D. 报告输出

```powershell
EagleEye.exe scan                                  # 默认同时出 JSON + HTML
EagleEye.exe scan --format json --out D:\r.json
EagleEye.exe scan --format html --html D:\r.html
EagleEye.exe scan --log D:\audit.jsonl
```

HTML 特点：

- 单文件、内联 CSS、**零外部引用**（无 CDN、无脚本、无字体文件），断网双击可看
- 章节：主机信息 → KPI 卡片 → 风险评定与证据链（可展开）→ 白名单抑制记录 → 未检查项 → DMA 设备 → 审计
- 等级色标：高度可疑红 / 可疑橙 / 信息灰

## E. 审计日志（本地 JSONL）

每行一个事件，UTF-8：

```jsonl
{"ts":"...","event":"scan.start","data":{"rulesVersion":"1.3.0","options":{...},"note":"本工具不发起任何网络请求，不上传任何数据"}}
{"ts":"...","event":"process.open","data":{"pid":28800,"name":"msedge.exe","accessMask":"0x1000","accessMeaning":"PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION（只读查询）","result":"granted","win32Error":0,"imagePath":"C:\\...\\msedge.exe"}}
{"ts":"...","event":"process.open","data":{"pid":7272,"name":"svchost.exe","accessMask":"0x1000","result":"denied","win32Error":5}}
{"ts":"...","event":"signature.verify","data":{"path":"...","state":"unsigned"}}
{"ts":"...","event":"driver.load","data":{"service":"PcieCfg","result":"failed","detail":"..."}}
{"ts":"...","event":"scan.end","data":{"riskLevel":"可疑","score":54,"activeFindings":86,"suppressed":1,"outputs":[...]}}
{"ts":"...","event":"audit.closed","data":{"processOpenAttempts":295,"processOpenDenied":166,"signatureChecks":129,"driverLoadAttempts":0}}
```

要点：

- **每个进程**都记一条：目标 PID、名字、申请的访问掩码、成功/被拒、Win32 错误码；
- 访问掩码恒为 `0x1000`（`PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION`），**从不申请** `PROCESS_VM_READ/WRITE`；
- 驱动加载/卸载各记一条，失败也记；
- 报告里的 `audit` 段是这份日志的摘要。

## F. 无网络

代码中没有任何 HTTP / socket / 上传逻辑；`audit.networkUsed` 恒为 `false`。
报告页脚与审计日志都会写明"本工具不发起任何网络请求，不上传任何数据"。

## G. 实测

```
规则集   : EagleEye 默认规则集 v1.3.0（更新 2026-11-10，schema 2.0）
规则数量 : 通用 18（启用 17），DMA 5，分类 8，白名单 17 条
命中/抑制 : 4 条活跃 / 0 条被白名单抑制
目标分布  : 信息 4  可疑 0  高度可疑 0
审计      : 进程打开 296 次（拒绝 168），权限仅 0x1000，验签 128 次，驱动的未加载，网络访问：无
```

组合升级实测（3 条弱信号，累计仅 36 分）：

```
[可疑]   36.0  WidgetService.exe (PID 22600)
           ↑ 组合升级：3 个弱信号叠加（规则：弱信号≥3 视为可疑）
```

定向抑制实测（`suppressRules: ["test.sig.unsigned"]`）：

```
suppressed: test.sig.unsigned@ArmouryCrate.exe
ArmouryCrate 仍活跃的证据: test.strong.module, test.path.windowsapps
```

# 附：合规红线（可执行自检）

红线不写在文档里靠自觉，而是由脚本逐条校验：

```powershell
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\audit-policy.ps1
# 退出码 0 = 全部通过；1 = 存在违规
```

当前 13 项全部 PASS：

| 检查项 | 结果 |
|---|---|
| C# 无注入/内存读写/Hook 实现（21 个文件） | PASS |
| C# 无反反作弊/绕过/提权/关 HVCI 关键词 | PASS |
| 驱动无写配置空间/内存读写/回调/文件操作 | PASS |
| 驱动只发 `IRP_MN_READ_CONFIG` | PASS |
| 驱动服务 `StartType=3`（按需启动，不自启） | PASS |
| `CreateService` 仅存在于 Kernel 装载模块 | PASS |
| 规则均有 description（可解释） | PASS |
| 规则均有 category（可分类） | PASS |
| 规则集有版本号与更新时间 | PASS |
| 白名单段存在 | PASS |
| 支持定向抑制 `suppressRules` | PASS |
| 报告命中均带 ruleId + evidence | PASS |
| 报告含审计段且 `networkUsed=false` | PASS |

脚本会**先剥离 C/C# 注释再匹配关键词**，避免"注释里写了绝不使用 XXX"造成误报；也会校验最新报告里每条命中是否都带证据。

## 被禁止且本工具从未实现的行为

进程注入、`ReadProcessMemory`/`WriteProcessMemory`、`VirtualAllocEx`、`CreateRemoteThread`、
`SetWindowsHookEx`、驱动隐藏、`ObRegisterCallbacks`、反反作弊、绕过 EAC/BattlEye/Vanguard/ACE、
伪造签名、提权（`SeDebugPrivilege`/`AdjustTokenPrivileges`）、关闭 HVCI/VBS、修改游戏文件。

## 若需求触碰红线，只用只读替代

| 需求 | 不做 | 只读替代 |
|---|---|---|
| 判断某 DLL 是否被注入进游戏 | 读游戏内存、扫内存特征 | 枚举已加载模块名 + 签名 + 路径（受保护进程跳过并注明"未检查"） |
| 判断是否有人读写游戏 | Hook / 句柄拦截 | 枚举全系统句柄持有者，看谁以 `PROCESS_VM_READ` 打开游戏（只读枚举，不拦截） |
| 判断 DMA 板卡 | 写配置空间、映射 BAR | `IRP_MN_READ_CONFIG` 读 256 字节 + 用户态资源报告近似 BAR 大小 |
| 判断驱动是否恶意 | 内核回调拦载/隐藏检测 | 枚举已加载驱动 + Authenticode + 哈希黑名单 |

# 附：AI 视觉外挂线索（阶段 5）

## A. 这个模块能做什么、不能做什么

| 层 | 数据来源 | 能证明 |
|---|---|---|
| `capture` | 可见窗口（`EnumWindows`）+ 已加载模块（Toolhelp） | 某进程**同时**拥有可见窗口并加载了采集/推理模块 |
| `hardware` | 复用 SetupAPI 设备枚举 | 本机存在采集卡 / HID 开发板 / 输入映射驱动 |
| `behavior` | `GetCursorPos` 轮询 | 输入事件的宏观统计（信息级） |

**不能证明**：双机链路、外接采集卡、KMBox 转发、模型内容、是否在对局中生效。
报告固定声明：*双机+外部采集+硬件注入链路在本机用户态无法独立确证*。

判定上限：单层 → 信息；≥2 层 → 可疑；**永远不超过「可疑」**，`maxLevel` 写高了规则校验会直接报错。

## B. 行为提示为什么**不用**低级钩子

需求允许「风险可控时使用 WH_MOUSE_LL」，但本实现明确**不采用**，理由：

1. `SetWindowsHookEx(WH_MOUSE_LL)` 是系统级全局钩子，属于本项目红线（不 Hook 任何 API），`tools\audit-policy.ps1` 会直接判 FAIL；
2. 全局钩子对反作弊高度可见，且在游戏运行期安装有被判定为可疑行为的风险；
3. Raw Input 的 `RIDEV_INPUTSINK` 需要后台收包，等价于全局输入捕获，同样踩线；
4. Windows 没有暴露"鼠标移动平滑度"这类性能计数器。

因此改为 `GetCursorPos` 定时轮询（默认 16ms / 3s）：

- 无钩子、无注册、无消息循环，对目标进程零侵入
- 采样点只存在于本地 List，函数返回即释放，**不落盘、不记录坐标轨迹**
- 只输出聚合值：直线化比例、匀速比例、静止占比、平均速度、速度标准差
- `severity` 恒为 1，`confidence ≤ 0.15`，输出注明「信息级，不构成判定」

## C. 规则文件（`vision` 段）

```jsonc
"vision": {
  "enabled": true,

  "capture": {
    "enabled": true,
    "moduleRules": [
      { "id": "vision.capture.onnxruntime", "tier": "strong", "pattern": "onnxruntime*.dll",
        "match": "glob", "category": "inference_runtime",
        "severity": 2, "confidence": 0.35,
        "description": "进程拥有可见窗口且加载了 ONNX Runtime（AI 推理运行时）" },
      { "id": "vision.capture.graphics_hook", "tier": "strong", "pattern": "graphics-hook*.dll",
        "match": "glob", "category": "capture_hook", "severity": 3, "confidence": 0.5,
        "description": "进程加载了 OBS Game Capture 的注入模块（画面采集中）" },
      { "id": "vision.context.gdi32", "tier": "context", "pattern": "gdi32*.dll",
        "match": "glob", "category": "capture_context", "severity": 1, "confidence": 0.05,
        "description": "加载了 GDI（BitBlt 等画面捕获能力的载体）" }
    ]
  },

  "hardware": {
    "enabled": true,
    "rules": [
      { "id": "vision.hw.elgato", "category": "video_capture", "vendorId": "0FD9",
        "severity": 2, "confidence": 0.4, "description": "Elgato 视频采集设备" },
      { "id": "vision.hw.arduino_hid", "category": "hid_injector", "vendorId": "2341",
        "deviceClass": "HIDClass", "severity": 3, "confidence": 0.5,
        "description": "Arduino 类开发板以 HID 输入设备形式枚举" },
      { "id": "vision.hw.vigem", "category": "input_mapper", "service": "ViGEmBus",
        "severity": 3, "confidence": 0.5, "description": "ViGEm 虚拟手柄总线" }
    ]
  },

  "behavior": { "enabled": false, "durationMs": 3000, "sampleMs": 16, "minSamples": 50,
                "straightLineRatio": 0.85, "constantVelocityRatio": 0.8, "zeroDeltaRatio": 0.6 },

  "whitelist": [
    { "processPattern": "nvcontainer.exe", "match": "exact", "reason": "NVIDIA 容器进程" },
    { "vendorId": "046D", "reason": "罗技外设" }
  ],

  "aggregation": { "maxLevel": "可疑", "requireIndependentLayers": 2 }
}
```

**tier 语义**：
- `strong`：特异性高（推理运行时 / CV 库 / 采集 hook），单独出现即可成条；
- `context`：通用图形模块（gdi32/dxgi/d3d11），几乎所有 GUI 程序都加载，**只加权不单独成条**——否则一台机器会出几百条噪音。

## D. 报告字段

```jsonc

  "enabled": true,
  "note": "双机+外部采集+硬件注入链路在本机用户态无法独立确证，以下为可观测线索，不代表最终判定。",
  "softwareCapture": [{
    "id": "vision.capture.onnxruntime", "layer": "capture", "category": "inference_runtime",

## E. 运行

```powershell

```

           模块: onnxruntime.dll, gdi32.dll
           签名: valid   窗口: 1295x823
[可疑]   16.0  vision.hw.elgato  Elgato 4K Capture
           VID:PID 0FD9:0061  Class Camera  Service usbvideo
声明: 双机+外部采集+硬件注入链路在本机用户态无法独立确证，以上为可观测线索，不代表最终判定。

干净机器（默认规则）：`0 条软件 / 0 条硬件 / 信息` —— 说明默认规则没有噪音。
