`src/EagleEye/Reporting/RuleTuner.cs`:

```cs
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Serialization;
using EagleEye.Models;
using EagleEye.Rules;

namespace EagleEye.Reporting;

/// <summary>
/// 规则调参：从一批带标签的扫描报告里统计每条规则的真实误报/命中表现，
/// 产出可直接放进 rules\ 的调参包（rules\tuning.suggested.json）。
///
/// 判定策略（刻意保守）：
///   · 只在干净机器命中、作弊样本 0 次 → 噪音
///       - 目标数 ≤ whitelistThreshold → 只加白，不动规则
///       - 受保护类型（句柄/驱动/哈希/特征码）→ 只降权，不停用
///       - 普通类型且干净报告 ≥ disableAt → 停用
///   · 干净/作弊都命中 → 保留规则，只把"干净出现、作弊从未出现"的目标加白
/// </summary>
public static class RuleTuner
{
    public sealed class Options
    {
        public string ReportsDir { get; set; } = "";
        public string? OutputPath { get; set; }
        public string Label { get; set; } = "clean";
        public string? RulesDir { get; set; }
        public double ConfidenceFactor { get; set; } = 0.6;
        public int DisableAt { get; set; } = 3;
        public int WhitelistThreshold { get; set; } = 20;
        public int MinRuleCount { get; set; } = 10;
        public bool NoWhitelist { get; set; }
    }

    private static readonly HashSet<string> ProtectedTypes = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
    {
        "handle_access", "kernel_driver", "file_hash", "file_signature"
    };

    private sealed class Stat
    {
        public string RuleId { get; init; } = "";
        public HashSet<string> Clean { get; } = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        public HashSet<string> Cheat { get; } = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        public List<string> Types { get; } = new();
        public double ScoreSum { get; set; }
        public int Hits { get; set; }
    }

    public static int Run(Options options, bool quiet)
    {
        if (!Directory.Exists(options.ReportsDir))
        {
            Console.Error.WriteLine($"报告目录不存在: {options.ReportsDir}");
            return 1;
        }

        var reports = Directory.GetFiles(options.ReportsDir, "*.json", SearchOption.TopDirectoryOnly)
            .Where(f => !Path.GetFileName(f).StartsWith("aggregate", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            .OrderBy(f => f)
            .ToList();

        var stats = new Dictionary<string, Stat>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        int cleanReports = 0, cheatReports = 0;

        foreach (string file in reports)
        {
            JsonDocument doc;
            try { doc = JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(file)); }
            catch { continue; }

            using (doc)
            {
                var root = doc.RootElement;
                if (!root.TryGetProperty("tool", out var tool) || tool.GetString() != "EagleEye")
                {
                    continue;
                }

                // 跳过验证用的小规则包
                if (root.TryGetProperty("ruleset", out var rs) &&
                    rs.TryGetProperty("ruleCount", out var rc) && rc.GetInt32() < options.MinRuleCount)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                bool isCheat = Path.GetFileName(file).StartsWith("cheat-", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                if (isCheat) cheatReports++; else cleanReports++;

                if (!root.TryGetProperty("findings", out var findings) || findings.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (var f in findings.EnumerateArray())
                {
                    string ruleId = f.TryGetProperty("ruleId", out var id) ? id.GetString() ?? "" : "";
                    if (ruleId.Length == 0) continue;

                    string target = f.TryGetProperty("target", out var tg) ? tg.GetString() ?? "" : "";
                    string type = f.TryGetProperty("ruleType", out var rt) ? rt.GetString() ?? "" : "";
                    double score = f.TryGetProperty("score", out var sc) ? sc.GetDouble() : 0;

                    if (!stats.TryGetValue(ruleId, out var stat))
                    {
                        stat = new Stat { RuleId = ruleId };
                        stats[ruleId] = stat;
                    }

                    if (isCheat) stat.Cheat.Add(target); else stat.Clean.Add(target);
                    stat.ScoreSum += score;
                    stat.Hits++;
                    if (type.Length > 0 && !stat.Types.Contains(type)) stat.Types.Add(type);
                }
            }
        }

        if (cleanReports == 0 && cheatReports == 0)
        {
            Console.Error.WriteLine($"目录里没有可用的扫描报告: {options.ReportsDir}");
            return 1;
        }

        // 主规则集（用于复制完整定义做覆盖）
        string rulesDir = options.RulesDir ?? Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "rules");
        var ruleSet = RuleLoader.LoadDirectory(rulesDir).RuleSet;

        // 可被覆盖的规则（只有通用 rules[] 能写回 schema）
        var ruleById = ruleSet.Rules.ToDictionary(r => r.Id, r => r, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        // 全部已知规则 id（含 DMA / 视觉 / 深度段）—— 用于判断"这条规则是不是真的存在"
        var knownIds = new HashSet<string>(ruleSet.Rules.Select(r => r.Id), StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        foreach (var r in ruleSet.Dma.Rules) knownIds.Add(r.Id);
        foreach (var r in ruleSet.Vision.Capture.ModuleRules) knownIds.Add(r.Id);
        foreach (var r in ruleSet.Vision.Hardware.Rules) knownIds.Add(r.Id);
        foreach (var r in ruleSet.Deep.Handles.Rules) knownIds.Add(r.Id);
        foreach (var r in ruleSet.Deep.Drivers.Rules) knownIds.Add(r.Id);
        foreach (var r in ruleSet.Deep.FileSignatures.Rules) knownIds.Add(r.Id);

        var rows = new List<(string RuleId, string Types, int Clean, int Cheat, double AvgScore, string Action)>();
        var tuningRules = new List<Rule>();
        var whitelist = new List<WhitelistEntry>();

        foreach (var stat in stats.Values)
        {
            var cleanOnly = stat.Clean.Where(t => !stat.Cheat.Contains(t)).ToList();
            bool isRealRule = knownIds.Contains(stat.RuleId);
            bool noise = isRealRule && stat.Clean.Count > 0 && stat.Cheat.Count == 0;
            bool isProtected = stat.Types.Any(t => ProtectedTypes.Contains(t));

            string action = "保留";
            if (noise)
            {
                if (stat.Clean.Count <= options.WhitelistThreshold) action = "仅加白";
                else if (isProtected) action = "建议降权（受保护类型）";
                else if (stat.Clean.Count >= options.DisableAt) action = "建议停用";
                else action = "建议降权";
            }

            double avg = stat.Hits > 0 ? Math.Round(stat.ScoreSum / stat.Hits, 1) : 0;
            rows.Add((stat.RuleId, string.Join("/", stat.Types), stat.Clean.Count, stat.Cheat.Count, avg, action));

            // 需要动规则的：复制完整定义后调整
            if (noise && action != "仅加白" && ruleById.TryGetValue(stat.RuleId, out var baseRule))
            {
                var copy = new Rule
                {
                    Id = baseRule.Id,
                    Type = baseRule.Type,
                    Category = baseRule.Category,
                    Pattern = baseRule.Pattern,
                    Match = baseRule.Match,
                    Hash = baseRule.Hash,
                    Size = baseRule.Size,
                    Severity = baseRule.Severity,
                    Confidence = Math.Round(baseRule.Confidence * options.ConfidenceFactor, 3),
                    Enabled = action != "建议停用",
                    Description = $"调参：干净基线命中 {stat.Clean.Count} 次、作弊样本 0 次 —— {baseRule.Description}",
                    Advice = baseRule.Advice,
                    Tags = baseRule.Tags
                };

                tuningRules.Add(copy);
            }

            // 目标级精准加白
            if (isRealRule && !options.NoWhitelist && cleanOnly.Count > 0 && cleanOnly.Count <= 50)
            {
                foreach (string target in cleanOnly)
                {
                    if (target.Length == 0 || target.StartsWith("pid", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;
                    if (!target.EndsWith(".exe", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                        !target.EndsWith(".dll", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                        !target.EndsWith(".sys", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;

                    whitelist.Add(new WhitelistEntry
                    {
                        Pattern = target,
                        Match = "exact",
                        Reason = $"调参基线：{stat.RuleId} 在干净机器命中、作弊样本从未出现",
                        SuppressRules = new[] { stat.RuleId }
                    });
                }
            }
        }

        // 输出
        string outPath = options.OutputPath ?? Path.Combine(rulesDir, "tuning.suggested.json");
        var pack = new
        {
            meta = new
            {
                name = "tuning-suggested",
                version = DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyy.MM.dd.HHmm"),
                updated = DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd"),
                schemaVersion = "2.0",
                description = $"由 tune 命令生成：基于 {cleanReports} 份干净报告 / {cheatReports} 份作弊报告"
            },
            rules = tuningRules,
            whitelist = new
            {
                processes = whitelist,
                paths = Array.Empty<object>(),
                files = Array.Empty<object>(),
                signers = Array.Empty<object>()
            }
        };

        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(Path.GetFullPath(outPath))!);
        File.WriteAllText(outPath, JsonSerializer.Serialize(pack, new JsonSerializerOptions
        {
            WriteIndented = true,
            PropertyNamingPolicy = JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase,
            DefaultIgnoreCondition = JsonIgnoreCondition.WhenWritingNull,
            Encoder = System.Text.Encodings.Web.JavaScriptEncoder.UnsafeRelaxedJsonEscaping
        }), new UTF8Encoding(false));

        if (!quiet)
        {
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine($"调参分析：{cleanReports} 份干净 / {cheatReports} 份作弊");
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine($"{"规则",-34}{"类型",-16}{"干净",6}{"作弊",6}{"均分",8}  判定");
            foreach (var row in rows.OrderByDescending(r => r.Clean).Take(25))
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"{Trim(row.RuleId, 34),-34}{Trim(row.Types, 16),-16}{row.Clean,6}{row.Cheat,6}{row.AvgScore,8}  {row.Action}");
            }

            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine($"调参包: {Path.GetFullPath(outPath)}（规则调整 {tuningRules.Count} 条，白名单 {whitelist.Count} 条）");
            Console.WriteLine("应用: 把该文件放进 rules\\ 目录，下次扫描自动生效");
        }

        return 0;
    }

    private static string Trim(string text, int max)
        => text.Length <= max ? text : text[..(max - 1)] + "…";
}

```

`src/EagleEye/Rules/RuleLoader.cs`:

```cs
using System.Text.Json;
using EagleEye.Models;

namespace EagleEye.Rules;

public sealed class RuleLoadResult
{
    public RuleSet RuleSet { get; set; } = new();
    public List<string> Errors { get; } = new();
    public List<string> Warnings { get; } = new();
    public string? SourcePath { get; set; }
    public bool Success => Errors.Count == 0;
}

public static class RuleLoader
{
    private static readonly HashSet<string> KnownTypes = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
    {
        RuleTypes.ProcessName, RuleTypes.ProcessPath, RuleTypes.ProcessCommandLine,
        RuleTypes.ModuleName, RuleTypes.FileName, RuleTypes.FileHash, RuleTypes.Signature
    };

    private static readonly HashSet<string> KnownSignaturePatterns = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
    {
        SignaturePatterns.Unsigned, SignaturePatterns.BadDigest, SignaturePatterns.UntrustedRoot,
        SignaturePatterns.Expired, SignaturePatterns.Revoked, SignaturePatterns.CatalogOnly,
        SignaturePatterns.AnyInvalid
    };

    private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOptions = new()
    {
        PropertyNameCaseInsensitive = true,
        ReadCommentHandling = JsonCommentHandling.Skip,
        AllowTrailingCommas = true
    };

    // ───────────────────────── 单文件加载 ─────────────────────────
    public static RuleLoadResult Load(string path)
    {
        var result = new RuleLoadResult { SourcePath = path };

        if (!File.Exists(path))
        {
            result.Errors.Add($"规则文件不存在: {path}");
            return result;
        }

        RuleSet? ruleSet;
        try
        {
            ruleSet = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<RuleSet>(File.ReadAllText(path), JsonOptions);
        }
        catch (JsonException ex)
        {
            result.Errors.Add($"规则文件 JSON 解析失败: {ex.Message}");
            return result;
        }

        if (ruleSet is null)
        {
            result.Errors.Add("规则文件解析结果为空");
            return result;
        }

        result.RuleSet = ruleSet;
        Validate(ruleSet, result);
        return result;
    }

    // ───────────────────────── 目录合并加载 ─────────────────────────
    /// <summary>
    /// 加载目录下全部 *.json 并按 id 合并（热更新）。
    /// meta 逐字段覆盖；rules 按 id 后者覆盖前者；whitelist 追加去重；
    /// options/dma/vision/deep/telemetry 出现即整段替换。
    /// </summary>
    public static RuleLoadResult LoadDirectory(string directory)
    {
        var result = new RuleLoadResult { SourcePath = directory };

        if (!Directory.Exists(directory))
        {
            result.Errors.Add($"规则目录不存在: {directory}");
            return result;
        }

        // rules.json 作为基础规则集优先（决定 meta/options/各段默认值），
        // 其余补丁包按文件名顺序后合并
        var files = Directory.GetFiles(directory, "*.json", SearchOption.TopDirectoryOnly)
            .OrderBy(f => Path.GetFileName(f).Equals("rules.json", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? 0 : 1)
            .ThenBy(f => Path.GetFileName(f), StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            .ToList();

        if (files.Count == 0)
        {
            result.Errors.Add($"规则目录里没有 json 文件: {directory}");
            return result;
        }

        var merged = new RuleSet();
        var ruleIndex = new Dictionary<string, int>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        int overrides = 0;
        bool firstFile = true;

        foreach (string file in files)
        {
            string name = Path.GetFileName(file);

            RuleSet? part;
            HashSet<string> keys;
            try
            {
                string text = File.ReadAllText(file);
                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(text, new JsonDocumentOptions
                {
                    AllowTrailingCommas = true,
                    CommentHandling = JsonCommentHandling.Skip
                });

                keys = doc.RootElement.EnumerateObject().Select(p => p.Name)
                    .ToHashSet(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

                part = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<RuleSet>(text, JsonOptions);
            }
            catch (JsonException ex)
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"{name} JSON 解析失败: {ex.Message}");
                continue;
            }

            if (part is null)
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"{name} 解析结果为空");
                continue;
            }

            result.Warnings.Add($"已加载: {name}（规则 {part.Rules.Count} 条，白名单 {CountWhitelistEntries(part.Whitelist)} 条）");

            if (firstFile)
            {
                merged.Meta = part.Meta;
                merged.Options = part.Options;
                merged.Dma = part.Dma;
                merged.Vision = part.Vision;
                merged.Deep = part.Deep;
                merged.Telemetry = part.Telemetry;
                firstFile = false;
            }

            if (keys.Contains("meta"))
            {
                // 名称/版本/更新时间只认第一份（基础规则集），否则会被后加载的补丁包改名
                // 例如 tuning.suggested.json 会把整个规则集显示成 "tuning-suggested"
                foreach (var category in part.Meta.Categories)
                {
                    if (!merged.Meta.Categories.Any(c => string.Equals(c.Id, category.Id, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                    {
                        merged.Meta.Categories.Add(category);
                    }
                }

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(merged.Meta.Description) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(part.Meta.Description))
                {
                    merged.Meta.Description = part.Meta.Description;
                }

            }


            if (keys.Contains("options")) merged.Options = part.Options;
            if (keys.Contains("dma")) merged.Dma = part.Dma;
            if (keys.Contains("vision")) merged.Vision = part.Vision;
            if (keys.Contains("deep")) merged.Deep = part.Deep;
            if (keys.Contains("telemetry")) merged.Telemetry = part.Telemetry;

            foreach (var rule in part.Rules)
            {
                if (ruleIndex.TryGetValue(rule.Id, out int index))
                {
                    merged.Rules[index] = rule;
                    overrides++;
                }
                else
                {
                    ruleIndex[rule.Id] = merged.Rules.Count;
                    merged.Rules.Add(rule);
                }
            }

            if (keys.Contains("whitelist"))
            {
                AppendWhitelist(merged.Whitelist, part.Whitelist);
            }
        }

        if (overrides > 0)
        {
            result.Warnings.Add($"规则 id 冲突 {overrides} 条，已按文件名顺序后者覆盖前者");
        }

        result.RuleSet = merged;
        Validate(merged, result);
        return result;
    }

    private static void AppendWhitelist(Whitelist target, Whitelist source)
    {
        void Merge(List<WhitelistEntry> dst, List<WhitelistEntry> src)
        {
            foreach (var entry in src)
            {
                bool exists = dst.Any(d =>
                    string.Equals(d.Pattern, entry.Pattern, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                    string.Equals(d.Hash, entry.Hash, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                    string.Equals(d.SubjectContains, entry.SubjectContains, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

                if (!exists) dst.Add(entry);
            }
        }

        Merge(target.Processes, source.Processes);
        Merge(target.Paths, source.Paths);
        Merge(target.Files, source.Files);
        Merge(target.Signers, source.Signers);
    }

    // ───────────────────────── 校验 ─────────────────────────
    private static void Validate(RuleSet ruleSet, RuleLoadResult result)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ruleSet.Meta.Name))
        {
            result.Warnings.Add("meta.name 为空");
        }

        var idSeen = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        for (int i = 0; i < ruleSet.Rules.Count; i++)
        {
            var rule = ruleSet.Rules[i];
            string where = $"rules[{i}]";

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Id))
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"{where}.id 不能为空");
            }
            else if (!idSeen.Add(rule.Id))
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"{where}.id 重复: {rule.Id}");
            }
            else
            {
                where = $"rules[{i}]({rule.Id})";
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Type))
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"{where}.type 不能为空");
            }
            else if (!KnownTypes.Contains(rule.Type))
            {
                result.Warnings.Add($"{where}.type 未知类型，将被忽略: {rule.Type}");
            }

            if (rule.Severity is < 1 or > 5)
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"{where}.severity 必须在 1-5 之间，当前 {rule.Severity}");
            }

            if (rule.Confidence is < 0 or > 1)
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"{where}.confidence 必须在 0-1 之间，当前 {rule.Confidence}");
            }

            switch (rule.Type.ToLowerInvariant())
            {
                case RuleTypes.FileHash:
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Hash) || rule.Hash.Length != 64)
                    {
                        result.Errors.Add($"{where}.hash 必须是 64 位 SHA256 十六进制");
                    }
                    break;

                case RuleTypes.Signature:
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Pattern) || !KnownSignaturePatterns.Contains(rule.Pattern))
                    {
                        result.Errors.Add($"{where}.pattern 必须是 unsigned|bad_digest|untrusted_root|expired|revoked|catalog_only|any_invalid");
                    }
                    break;

                default:
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Pattern))
                    {
                        result.Errors.Add($"{where}.pattern 不能为空");
                    }
                    break;
            }

            if (rule.Enabled && rule.Severity >= 4 && rule.Confidence < 0.5)
            {
                result.Warnings.Add($"{where} 高危规则但置信度偏低（{rule.Confidence}），可能放大误报");
            }
        }

        ValidateWhitelist(ruleSet.Whitelist, result);
        ValidateDma(ruleSet.Dma, result);
        ValidateVision(ruleSet.Vision, result);
        ValidateDeep(ruleSet.Deep, result);
        ValidateTelemetry(ruleSet.Telemetry, result);
    }

    private static void ValidateWhitelist(Whitelist whitelist, RuleLoadResult result)
    {
        void Check(List<WhitelistEntry> entries, string section)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < entries.Count; i++)
            {
                var e = entries[i];
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(e.Pattern) && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(e.Hash) && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(e.SubjectContains))
                {
                    result.Errors.Add($"whitelist.{section}[{i}] 至少要给 pattern / hash / subjectContains 之一");
                }

                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(e.Hash) && e.Hash.Length != 64)
                {
                    result.Errors.Add($"whitelist.{section}[{i}].hash 必须是 64 位 SHA256 十六进制");
                }

                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(e.Expires) && !DateTime.TryParse(e.Expires, out _))
                {
                    result.Warnings.Add($"whitelist.{section}[{i}].expires 格式不合法（应为 yyyy-MM-dd）: {e.Expires}");
                }
            }
        }

        Check(whitelist.Processes, "processes");
        Check(whitelist.Paths, "paths");
        Check(whitelist.Files, "files");
        Check(whitelist.Signers, "signers");
    }

    private static void ValidateDma(DmaSection dma, RuleLoadResult result)
    {
        var ids = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        for (int i = 0; i < dma.Rules.Count; i++)
        {
            var rule = dma.Rules[i];
            string where = $"dma.rules[{i}]";

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Id))
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"{where}.id 不能为空");
            }
            else if (!ids.Add(rule.Id))
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"{where}.id 重复: {rule.Id}");
            }
            else
            {
                where = $"dma.rules[{i}]({rule.Id})";
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Vid) && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.ClassCode))
            {
                result.Warnings.Add($"{where} 既没有 vid 也没有 classCode，会匹配全部 PCI 设备");
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Vid) && rule.Vid!.TrimStart('0', 'x', 'X').Length is < 1 or > 4)
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"{where}.vid 必须是 4 位十六进制，如 10EE");
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Pid) && rule.Pid!.TrimStart('0', 'x', 'X').Length is < 1 or > 4)
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"{where}.pid 必须是 4 位十六进制，如 7024");
            }

            if (rule.Severity is < 1 or > 5)
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"{where}.severity 必须在 1-5 之间，当前 {rule.Severity}");
            }

            if (rule.Confidence is < 0 or > 1)
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"{where}.confidence 必须在 0-1 之间，当前 {rule.Confidence}");
            }

            if (rule.Severity >= 4 && rule.Confidence > 0.8)
            {
                result.Warnings.Add($"{where} 用户态无法验证配置空间，建议 confidence 不超过 0.8");
            }
        }

        foreach (var w in dma.Whitelist)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(w.Vid) && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(w.Pid))
            {
                result.Errors.Add("dma.whitelist 条目至少要给 vid 或 pid");
            }
        }
    }

    private static void ValidateVision(VisionSection vision, RuleLoadResult result)
    {
        if (!vision.Enabled) return;

        var ids = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        foreach (var rule in vision.Capture.ModuleRules.Concat(vision.Hardware.Rules))
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Id))
            {
                result.Errors.Add("vision 规则 id 不能为空");
                continue;
            }

            if (!ids.Add(rule.Id))
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"vision 规则 id 重复: {rule.Id}");
            }

            if (rule.Severity is < 1 or > 4)
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"vision[{rule.Id}].severity 必须在 1-4 之间（视觉线索不应给高危）");
            }

            if (rule.Confidence is < 0 or > 0.7)
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"vision[{rule.Id}].confidence 必须在 0-0.7 之间（本地线索置信度上限 0.7）");
            }

            bool isHardware = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.VendorId) || !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Service);
            if (!isHardware && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Pattern))
            {
                result.Errors.Add($"vision[{rule.Id}] 既没有 pattern 也没有 vid/service");
            }
        }

        if (ScoringRank(vision.Aggregation.MaxLevel) > ScoringRank(RiskLevels.Suspicious))
        {
            result.Errors.Add("vision.aggregation.maxLevel 不得高于「可疑」：视觉线索不能单独定为高度可疑");
        }

        if (vision.Aggregation.RequireIndependentLayers is < 1 or > 3)
        {
            result.Errors.Add("vision.aggregation.requireIndependentLayers 必须在 1-3 之间");
        }

        foreach (var w in vision.Whitelist)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(w.ProcessPattern) && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(w.VendorId))
            {
                result.Errors.Add("vision.whitelist 条目至少要给 processPattern 或 vid");
            }
        }
    }

    private static int ScoringRank(string level) => level switch
    {
        RiskLevels.High => 2,
        RiskLevels.Suspicious => 1,
        _ => 0
    };

    private static void ValidateDeep(DeepSection deep, RuleLoadResult result)
    {
        var ids = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        foreach (var rule in deep.Handles.Rules.Where(r => r.Enabled))
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Id)) { result.Errors.Add("deep.handles 规则 id 不能为空"); continue; }
            if (!ids.Add(rule.Id)) result.Errors.Add($"deep 规则 id 重复: {rule.Id}");
            if (rule.Severity is < 1 or > 5) result.Errors.Add($"deep.handles[{rule.Id}].severity 必须在 1-5");
            if (rule.Confidence is < 0 or > 1) result.Errors.Add($"deep.handles[{rule.Id}].confidence 必须在 0-1");
        }

        foreach (var rule in deep.Drivers.Rules.Where(r => r.Enabled))
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Id)) { result.Errors.Add("deep.drivers 规则 id 不能为空"); continue; }
            if (!ids.Add(rule.Id)) result.Errors.Add($"deep 规则 id 重复: {rule.Id}");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Pattern) && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Sha256))
                result.Errors.Add($"deep.drivers[{rule.Id}] 需要 pattern 或 sha256");
            if (rule.Severity is < 1 or > 5) result.Errors.Add($"deep.drivers[{rule.Id}].severity 必须在 1-5");
        }

        foreach (var rule in deep.FileSignatures.Rules.Where(r => r.Enabled))
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Id)) { result.Errors.Add("deep.fileSignatures 规则 id 不能为空"); continue; }
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Pattern)) result.Errors.Add($"deep.fileSignatures[{rule.Id}] 需要 pattern");
            string type = (rule.PatternType ?? "ascii").ToLowerInvariant();
            if (type is not ("ascii" or "hex"))
                result.Errors.Add($"deep.fileSignatures[{rule.Id}].patternType 只能是 ascii 或 hex");
        }

        if (deep.Overlay.MinAreaRatio is <= 0 or > 1)
            result.Errors.Add("deep.overlay.minAreaRatio 必须在 (0,1] 区间");
    }

    private static void ValidateTelemetry(TelemetrySection telemetry, RuleLoadResult result)
    {
        if (!telemetry.Enabled) return;

        var ids = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        foreach (var rule in telemetry.Signals.Where(r => r.Enabled))
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Id)) { result.Errors.Add("telemetry 规则 id 不能为空"); continue; }
            if (!ids.Add(rule.Id)) result.Errors.Add($"telemetry 规则 id 重复: {rule.Id}");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Metric)) result.Errors.Add($"telemetry[{rule.Id}].metric 不能为空");
            if ((rule.Comparator ?? "gte").ToLowerInvariant() is not ("gte" or "lte"))
                result.Errors.Add($"telemetry[{rule.Id}].comparator 只能是 gte 或 lte");
            if (rule.Severity is < 1 or > 5) result.Errors.Add($"telemetry[{rule.Id}].severity 必须在 1-5");
            if (rule.Confidence is < 0 or > 1) result.Errors.Add($"telemetry[{rule.Id}].confidence 必须在 0-1");
        }

        foreach (var rule in telemetry.Signals.Where(r => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(r.Metric)))
        {
            if (!telemetry.Baseline.ContainsKey(rule.Metric))
                result.Warnings.Add($"telemetry[{rule.Id}] 的 metric '{rule.Metric}' 没有基线值，报告里无法给出人群对比");
        }
    }

    public static int CountWhitelistEntries(Whitelist w)
        => w.Processes.Count + w.Paths.Count + w.Files.Count + w.Signers.Count;

    public static bool IsExpired(WhitelistEntry entry)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.Expires)) return false;
        return DateTime.TryParse(entry.Expires, out var d) && d.Date < DateTime.Today;
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Scanning/AuditLogger.cs`:

```cs
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Encodings.Web;
using System.Text.Json;

namespace EagleEye.Scanning;

/// <summary>
/// 扫描器自身行为日志（JSONL，一行一个事件）。
/// 记录：扫描起止与参数、每个进程的打开请求与申请的权限、验签次数、驱动加载/卸载、报告输出路径。
/// 全程只写本地文件，不联网、不上传。
/// </summary>
public sealed class AuditLogger : IDisposable
{
    private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOptions = new()
    {
        Encoder = JavaScriptEncoder.UnsafeRelaxedJsonEscaping
    };

    private readonly object _lock = new();
    private readonly StreamWriter? _writer;

    private int _openAttempts;
    private int _openDenied;
    private int _signatureChecks;
    private int _driverAttempts;
    private int _driverLoadedFlag;
    private int _driverUnloadedFlag;
    private int _disposed;

    public string? LogPath { get; }

    public int ProcessOpenAttempts => Volatile.Read(ref _openAttempts);
    public int ProcessOpenDenied => Volatile.Read(ref _openDenied);
    public int SignatureChecks => Volatile.Read(ref _signatureChecks);
    public int DriverLoadAttempts => Volatile.Read(ref _driverAttempts);
    public bool DriverLoaded => Volatile.Read(ref _driverLoadedFlag) == 1;
    public bool DriverUnloaded => Volatile.Read(ref _driverUnloadedFlag) == 1;

    public AuditLogger(string? path)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path))
        {
            return;
        }

        LogPath = Path.GetFullPath(path!);
        string? dir = Path.GetDirectoryName(LogPath);
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dir))
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
        }

        _writer = new StreamWriter(
            new FileStream(LogPath, FileMode.Append, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.Read),
            new UTF8Encoding(false))
        {
            AutoFlush = true
        };
    }

    public void Write(string eventName, object? payload)
    {
        if (_writer is null || Volatile.Read(ref _disposed) == 1)
        {
            return;
        }

        var record = new Dictionary<string, object?>
        {
            ["ts"] = DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("o"),
            ["event"] = eventName,
            ["data"] = payload
        };

        string line = JsonSerializer.Serialize(record, JsonOptions);
        lock (_lock)
        {
            _writer.WriteLine(line);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>记录一次进程打开：目标、申请的访问掩码、结果。</summary>
    public void ProcessOpen(int pid, string name, uint accessMask, bool success, int win32Error, string? path)
    {
        Interlocked.Increment(ref _openAttempts);
        if (!success)
        {
            Interlocked.Increment(ref _openDenied);
        }

        Write("process.open", new
        {
            pid,
            name,
            accessMask = $"0x{accessMask:X}",
            accessMeaning = accessMask == 0x1000 ? "PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION（只读查询）" : "unknown",
            result = success ? "granted" : "denied",
            win32Error = success ? 0 : win32Error,
            imagePath = path,
            note = "不申请 PROCESS_VM_READ / PROCESS_VM_WRITE，不读写进程内存"
        });
    }

    public void SignatureChecked(string path, string state)
    {
        Interlocked.Increment(ref _signatureChecks);
        if (SignatureChecks <= 400)
        {
            Write("signature.verify", new { path, state });
        }
    }

    public void DriverEvent(string phase, string serviceName, string? driverPath, bool success, string? detail)
    {
        if (phase == "load")
        {
            Interlocked.Increment(ref _driverAttempts);
            if (success)
            {
                Interlocked.Exchange(ref _driverLoadedFlag, 1);
            }
        }
        else if (phase == "unload" && success)
        {
            Interlocked.Exchange(ref _driverUnloadedFlag, 1);
        }

        Write("driver." + phase, new
        {
            service = serviceName,
            driverPath,
            result = success ? "ok" : "failed",
            detail
        });
    }

    public void Dispose()
    {
        if (Interlocked.Exchange(ref _disposed, 1) == 1)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (_writer is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        var summary = new Dictionary<string, object?>
        {
            ["ts"] = DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("o"),
            ["event"] = "audit.closed",
            ["data"] = new
            {
                processOpenAttempts = ProcessOpenAttempts,
                processOpenDenied = ProcessOpenDenied,
                signatureChecks = SignatureChecks,
                driverLoadAttempts = DriverLoadAttempts,
                driverLoaded = DriverLoaded,
                driverUnloaded = DriverUnloaded
            }
        };

        lock (_lock)
        {
            _writer.WriteLine(JsonSerializer.Serialize(summary, JsonOptions));
            _writer.Flush();
            _writer.Dispose();
        }
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Scanning/DmaScanner.cs`:

```cs
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using EagleEye.Core;
using EagleEye.Models;
using EagleEye.Native;

namespace EagleEye.Scanning;

public sealed class DmaScanResult
{
    public bool Enabled { get; set; }
    public int DevicesEnumerated { get; set; }
    public List<DmaDeviceInfo> Devices { get; } = new();
    public List<DmaFinding> Findings { get; } = new();
    public List<NotChecked> NotChecked { get; } = new();
    public ConfigSpaceReadResult? Kernel { get; set; }
}

/// <summary>
/// DMA 外设检测。
///
/// 两阶段：
///   1) Enumerate —— 用户态 SetupAPI/CfgMgr32 枚举 PCI 设备（VID/PID/REV/设备类/服务/位置/PDO 名）；
///   2) Match     —— 可选先让 configReader 用一次性只读驱动补齐真实配置空间，再做规则比对。
///
/// 能做的：拿到 VID/PID/REV/近似或真实 Class Code、Capability 链、BAR 原始值、MSI 能力。
/// 不能做的：确定 BAR 大小需要写 0xFFFFFFFF 回读，本工具禁止写配置空间，故 barSizeDeterminable 恒为 false。
/// 不做的事：不加载常驻驱动、不改设备状态、不访问 IOMMU、不读写进程内存。
/// </summary>
public sealed class DmaScanner
{
    private static readonly Regex VidRegex = new(@"VEN_([0-9A-Fa-f]{4})", RegexOptions.Compiled);
    private static readonly Regex DidRegex = new(@"DEV_([0-9A-Fa-f]{4})", RegexOptions.Compiled);
    private static readonly Regex SubsysRegex = new(@"SUBSYS_([0-9A-Fa-f]{8})", RegexOptions.Compiled);
    private static readonly Regex RevRegex = new(@"REV_([0-9A-Fa-f]{2})", RegexOptions.Compiled);

    /// <summary>设备类名 → 近似 PCI 基础类（1 字节）。仅为启发式，非配置空间真实值。</summary>
    private static readonly Dictionary<string, string> ClassGuessMap = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
    {
        ["Display"] = "03", ["Net"] = "02", ["Media"] = "04", ["System"] = "06",
        ["USB"] = "0C", ["Bluetooth"] = "0D", ["HDC"] = "01", ["SCSIAdapter"] = "01",
        ["DiskDrive"] = "01", ["CDROM"] = "01", ["Volume"] = "01", ["Mouse"] = "09",
        ["Keyboard"] = "09", ["HIDClass"] = "03", ["Ports"] = "07", ["Modem"] = "07",
        ["Processor"] = "0B", ["Memory"] = "05", ["SmartCardReader"] = "0C",
        ["SecurityDevices"] = "10", ["SoftwareDevice"] = "00", ["Unknown"] = "FF"
    };

    public DmaScanResult Scan(
        DmaSection section,
        CancellationToken ct,
        RuleSet ruleSet,
        Func<List<DmaDeviceInfo>, ConfigSpaceReadResult>? configReader = null)
    {
        var result = new DmaScanResult { Enabled = section.Enabled };
        if (!section.Enabled)
        {
            return result;
        }

        var raw = SetupApiInterop.EnumeratePresentDevices(out string? enumError);
        result.DevicesEnumerated = raw.Count;

        if (enumError is not null)
        {
            result.NotChecked.Add(new NotChecked { Target = "PCI/PCIe 设备", Reason = enumError });
        }

        // ---- 阶段 1：用户态枚举 ----
        foreach (var device in raw)
        {
            ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();

            if (!device.InstanceId.StartsWith("PCI\\", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                continue;
            }

            result.Devices.Add(new DmaDeviceInfo
            {
                Name = device.FriendlyName ?? device.DeviceDesc ?? "未知设备",
                InstanceId = device.InstanceId,
                VendorId = FirstGroup(VidRegex, device.InstanceId),
                DeviceId = FirstGroup(DidRegex, device.InstanceId),
                SubsystemId = FirstGroup(SubsysRegex, device.InstanceId),
                Revision = FirstGroup(RevRegex, device.InstanceId),
                DeviceClass = device.ClassName,
                ClassCodeGuess = GuessClassCode(device.ClassName),
                Service = device.Service,
                Location = device.Location,
                Manufacturer = device.Manufacturer,
                ProblemCode = device.ProblemCode,
                PdoName = device.PdoName
            });
        }

        // ---- 阶段 2（可选）：一次性只读内核枚举，补齐真实配置空间 ----
        if (configReader is not null && result.Devices.Count > 0)
        {
            result.Kernel = configReader(result.Devices);
        }

        // ---- 阶段 3：规则比对 ----
        var activeRules = section.Rules.Where(r => r.Enabled).ToList();

        foreach (var device in result.Devices)
        {
            ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();

            var (whitelisted, wlReason) = CheckWhitelist(section, device.VendorId, device.DeviceId);
            var config = device.ConfigSpace;

            foreach (var rule in activeRules)
            {
                if (!Matches(rule, device, config)) continue;
                if (!ConditionsSatisfied(rule, config)) continue;

                double score = Scoring.ComputeScore(rule.Severity, rule.Confidence, Scoring.WeightOf(ruleSet, rule.Category));

                result.Findings.Add(new DmaFinding
                {
                    RuleId = rule.Id,
                    Target = device.Name,
                    InstanceId = device.InstanceId,
                    VendorId = device.VendorId,
                    DeviceId = device.DeviceId,
                    Revision = config is not null ? $"{config.RevisionId:X2}" : device.Revision,
                    ClassCodeGuess = config is not null ? config.ClassCodeHex : device.ClassCodeGuess,
                    Severity = rule.Severity,
                    Confidence = rule.Confidence,
                    Score = score,
                    RiskLevel = Scoring.LevelOf(score),
                    Description = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Description) ? "DMA 外设规则命中" : rule.Description!,
                    Advice = rule.Advice,
                    Category = rule.Category,
                    Suppressed = whitelisted,
                    SuppressReason = wlReason,
                    SuppressedBy = whitelisted ? "whitelist" : null,
                    UserModeOnly = config is null,
                    ConfigSpaceVerified = config is not null,
                    Evidence = BuildEvidence(device, config)
                });
            }
        }

        result.Findings.Sort((a, b) => b.Score.CompareTo(a.Score));
        result.Devices.Sort((a, b) => string.Compare(a.Name, b.Name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
        return result;
    }

    private static Dictionary<string, object?> BuildEvidence(DmaDeviceInfo device, ConfigSpaceInfo? config)
    {
        var evidence = new Dictionary<string, object?>
        {
            ["instanceId"] = device.InstanceId,
            ["pdoName"] = device.PdoName,
            ["deviceClass"] = device.DeviceClass,
            ["classCodeSource"] = config is not null ? "pci-config-space" : "setupapi-derived-guess",
            ["service"] = device.Service,
            ["location"] = device.Location,
            ["manufacturer"] = device.Manufacturer,
            ["problemCode"] = device.ProblemCode,
            ["configSpaceAvailable"] = config is not null
        };

        if (config is not null)
        {
            evidence["configSpace"] = new
            {
                vendorId = $"0x{config.VendorId:X4}",
                deviceId = $"0x{config.DeviceId:X4}",
                revision = config.RevisionId,
                classCode = config.ClassCodeHex,
                subsystem = $"0x{config.SubsystemVendorId:X4}:0x{config.SubsystemId:X4}",
                capabilityIds = config.CapabilityIds.Select(c => $"0x{c:X2}").ToArray(),
                hasMsi = config.HasMsi,
                hasMsiX = config.HasMsiX,
                bars = config.Bars.Select(b => $"0x{b:X8}").ToArray(),
                barKinds = config.BarKinds,
                barSizeDeterminable = config.BarSizeDeterminable,
                raw64Bytes = config.RawHex
            };
        }

        return evidence;
    }

    private static bool Matches(DmaRule rule, DmaDeviceInfo device, ConfigSpaceInfo? config)
    {
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Vid) &&
            !HexEquals(rule.Vid, config is not null ? $"{config.VendorId:X4}" : device.VendorId))
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Pid) &&
            !HexEquals(rule.Pid, config is not null ? $"{config.DeviceId:X4}" : device.DeviceId))
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Revision) &&
            !HexEquals(rule.Revision, config is not null ? $"{config.RevisionId:X2}" : device.Revision))
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.ClassCode) &&
            !HexEquals(rule.ClassCode, config is not null ? config.ClassCodeHex[..2] : device.ClassCodeGuess))
        {
            return false;
        }

        return true;
    }

    /// <summary>conditions 只在拿到真实配置空间时生效；没有配置空间数据时该规则不触发。</summary>
    private static bool ConditionsSatisfied(DmaRule rule, ConfigSpaceInfo? config)
    {
        if (rule.Conditions is null || rule.Conditions.Length == 0)
        {
            return true;
        }

        if (config is null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        foreach (string condition in rule.Conditions)
        {
            bool ok = condition.Trim().ToLowerInvariant() switch
            {
                "msi_missing" => !config.HasMsi,
                "msix_missing" => !config.HasMsiX,
                "no_capability_list" => config.CapabilityIds.Length == 0,
                "bar0_zero" => config.Bars.Length > 0 && config.Bars[0] == 0,
                "class_ff" => config.BaseClass == 0xFF,
                "revision_zero" => config.RevisionId == 0,
                _ => true
            };

            if (!ok)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    private static (bool, string?) CheckWhitelist(DmaSection section, string? vid, string? did)
    {
        foreach (var entry in section.Whitelist)
        {
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.Expires) &&
                DateTime.TryParse(entry.Expires, out DateTime expires) && expires.Date < DateTime.Today)
            {
                continue;
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.Vid) && !HexEquals(entry.Vid, vid)) continue;
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.Pid) && !HexEquals(entry.Pid, did)) continue;

            return (true, entry.Reason ?? $"DMA 白名单: VID {entry.Vid}");
        }

        return (false, null);
    }

    public static string NormalizeHex(string? value)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value)) return "";
        string s = value.Trim();
        if (s.StartsWith("0x", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) s = s[2..];
        return s.ToUpperInvariant();
    }

    private static bool HexEquals(string? ruleValue, string? deviceValue)
        => string.Equals(NormalizeHex(ruleValue), NormalizeHex(deviceValue), StringComparison.Ordinal);

    private static string FirstGroup(Regex regex, string input)
    {
        Match m = regex.Match(input);
        return m.Success ? m.Groups[1].Value.ToUpperInvariant() : "";
    }

    private static string GuessClassCode(string? className)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(className)) return "FF";
        return ClassGuessMap.TryGetValue(className!, out string? code) ? code : "FF";
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Scanning/DriverScanner.cs`:

```cs
using EagleEye.Core;
using EagleEye.Models;
using EagleEye.Native;

namespace EagleEye.Scanning;

/// <summary>
/// 已加载驱动线索：枚举内核模块 → 比对规则（文件名 / 哈希）→ 验签。
/// 只读：SystemModuleInformation 查询 + 文件只读哈希与验签，不加载、不卸载、不打开设备。
/// </summary>
public sealed class DriverScanner
{
    public (DriverScanReport Report, List<Finding> Findings) Scan(
        DriverScanOptions options,
        SignatureChecker signatures,
        CancellationToken ct,
        AuditLogger? audit = null)
    {
        var report = new DriverScanReport { Enabled = options.Enabled, Attempted = options.Enabled };
        var findings = new List<Finding>();

        if (!options.Enabled)
        {
            return (report, findings);
        }

        var modules = DriverInterop.Enumerate(out string? error);
        if (error is not null)
        {
            report.Error = error;
            audit?.Write("driver.scan", new { result = "failed", error });
            return (report, findings);
        }

        report.DriversEnumerated = modules.Count;
        audit?.Write("driver.scan", new { modules = modules.Count, note = "只枚举已加载模块，不加载/卸载任何驱动" });

        var imagePaths = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        var matchedThisRun = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        foreach (var module in modules)
        {
            ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();

            string win32Path = DriverInterop.ToWin32Path(module.FullPath);
            imagePaths[module.Name] = win32Path;

            foreach (var rule in options.Rules.Where(r => r.Enabled))
            {
                bool nameHit = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Pattern) &&
                               PatternMatcher.IsMatch(module.Name, rule.Pattern, null);

                string? hash = null;
                bool hashHit = false;

                if (!nameHit && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Sha256) && File.Exists(win32Path))
                {
                    hash = Hashing.TrySha256(win32Path, out _);
                    hashHit = string.Equals(hash, rule.Sha256, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                }

                if (!nameHit && !hashHit) continue;

                matchedThisRun.Add(module.Name);
                var signature = File.Exists(win32Path) ? signatures.Check(win32Path) : null;
                if (signature is { State: SignatureStates.Unsigned }) report.UnsignedDrivers++;

                double score = Scoring.ComputeScore(rule.Severity, rule.Confidence);
                findings.Add(new Finding
                {
                    RuleId = rule.Id,
                    RuleType = "kernel_driver",
                    Category = rule.Category ?? "driver",
                    Target = module.Name,
                    TargetPath = win32Path,
                    Pid = null,
                    Severity = rule.Severity,
                    Confidence = rule.Confidence,
                    Score = score,
                    RiskLevel = Scoring.LevelOf(score),
                    Description = rule.Description ?? $"已加载驱动命中规则：{module.Name}",
                    Advice = rule.Advice,
                    Evidence = new Dictionary<string, object?>
                    {
                        ["moduleName"] = module.Name,
                        ["ntPath"] = module.FullPath,
                        ["win32Path"] = win32Path,
                        ["imageBase"] = $"0x{module.ImageBase:X}",
                        ["imageSize"] = module.ImageSize,
                        ["signatureState"] = signature?.State,
                        ["signer"] = signature?.SignerSubject,
                        ["sha256"] = hash,
                        ["loaded"] = true
                    }
                });

                report.Suspicious.Add(new LoadedDriverInfo
                {
                    Name = module.Name,
                    Path = win32Path,
                    ImageBase = module.ImageBase,
                    ImageSize = module.ImageSize,
                    SignatureState = signature?.State,
                    Signer = signature?.SignerSubject,
                    MatchedRuleId = rule.Id
                });
            }
        }

        // 白名单模式：不用样本也能查出"不该出现的驱动"
        if (options.WhitelistMode)
        {
            foreach (var module in modules)
            {
                ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
                if (matchedThisRun.Contains(module.Name)) continue;

                string win32Path2 = DriverInterop.ToWin32Path(module.FullPath);
                if (!File.Exists(win32Path2)) continue;

                var signature = signatures.Check(win32Path2);
                bool validSignature = signature.State is SignatureStates.Valid or SignatureStates.CatalogValid;

                // 目录签名（catalog_valid）走的是 Windows 目录信任链，SignerSubject 常为空，直接放行
                if (signature.State == SignatureStates.CatalogValid) continue;

                bool trustedSigner = signature.SignerSubject is not null &&
                    options.TrustedSigners.Any(s => signature.SignerSubject!.Contains(s, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

                if (validSignature && (trustedSigner || options.TrustedSigners.Count == 0)) continue;

                string reason = validSignature
                    ? "驱动签名主体不在受信任列表：" + signature.SignerSubject
                    : "驱动无有效签名（" + signature.State + "）";

                double score = Scoring.ComputeScore(options.UntrustedSeverity, options.UntrustedConfidence);
                findings.Add(new Finding
                {
                    RuleId = "driver.untrusted_signature",
                    RuleType = "kernel_driver",
                    Category = "driver",
                    Target = module.Name,
                    TargetPath = win32Path2,
                    Severity = options.UntrustedSeverity,
                    Confidence = options.UntrustedConfidence,
                    Score = score,
                    RiskLevel = Scoring.LevelOf(score),
                    Description = reason,
                    Advice = "第三方驱动不一定是外挂，需核对厂商与用途；外挂驱动几乎都落在这里",
                    Evidence = new Dictionary<string, object?>
                    {
                        ["moduleName"] = module.Name,
                        ["win32Path"] = win32Path2,
                        ["signatureState"] = signature.State,
                        ["signer"] = signature.SignerSubject,
                        ["mode"] = "driver-whitelist",
                        ["note"] = "无样本哈希，仅依据签名与受信任主体列表"
                    }
                });

                report.Suspicious.Add(new LoadedDriverInfo
                {
                    Name = module.Name,
                    Path = win32Path2,
                    ImageBase = module.ImageBase,
                    ImageSize = module.ImageSize,
                    SignatureState = signature.State,
                    Signer = signature.SignerSubject,
                    MatchedRuleId = "driver.untrusted_signature"
                });
            }
        }

        return (report, findings);
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Scanning/ExtendedScans.cs`:

```cs
using EagleEye.Models;
using EagleEye.Native;

namespace EagleEye.Scanning;

public sealed class DeepScanRequest
{
    public bool Handles { get; set; } = true;
    public bool Drivers { get; set; } = true;
    public bool Overlay { get; set; } = true;
    public bool FileSignatures { get; set; }
    public List<string> FileRoots { get; set; } = new();
}

/// <summary>
/// 深度扫描编排：句柄线索 + 已加载驱动线索 + overlay 窗口 + 文件特征码。
/// 全部只读；不新增任何内核组件；不使用任何 Hook。
/// </summary>
public static class ExtendedScans
{
    public static DeepScanResult Run(
        RuleSet rules,
        ProcessScanResult processes,
        DeepScanRequest request,
        SignatureChecker signatures,
        CancellationToken ct,
        AuditLogger? audit = null)
    {
        var result = new DeepScanResult();

        try
        {
            if (request.Handles && rules.Deep.Handles.Enabled)
            {
                var (handleReport, handleFindings) = new HandleScanner()
                    .Scan(rules.Deep.Handles, processes, ct, audit);
                result.Handles = handleReport;
                result.Findings.AddRange(handleFindings);

                if (handleReport.Error is not null)
                {
                    result.NotChecked.Add(new NotChecked
                    {
                        Target = "句柄表",
                        Reason = $"句柄枚举未完成：{handleReport.Error}（通常需要管理员权限）"
                    });
                }
            }

            if (request.Drivers && rules.Deep.Drivers.Enabled)
            {
                var (driverReport, driverFindings) = new DriverScanner()
                    .Scan(rules.Deep.Drivers, signatures, ct, audit);
                result.Drivers = driverReport;
                result.Findings.AddRange(driverFindings);

                if (driverReport.Error is not null)
                {
                    result.NotChecked.Add(new NotChecked
                    {
                        Target = "已加载驱动",
                        Reason = $"驱动枚举未完成：{driverReport.Error}"
                    });
                }
            }

            if (rules.Deep.Injection.Enabled)
            {
                var (injFindings, injTraces) = new InjectionTraceScanner()
                    .Scan(rules.Deep.Injection, processes, signatures, ct, audit);
                result.Findings.AddRange(injFindings);
                result.InjectionTraces.AddRange(injTraces);
            }

            if (request.Overlay && rules.Deep.Overlay.Enabled && rules.Vision.Enabled)
            {
                result.Overlays.AddRange(new OverlayScanner().Scan(rules.Deep.Overlay, processes, ct));
            }

            if (request.FileSignatures && rules.Deep.FileSignatures.Enabled)
            {
                // 显式指定的 --file-root 优先，避免被默认目录先占满 maxFiles
                var roots = request.FileRoots.Concat(rules.Options.ScanRoots);

                var (sigReport, sigFindings) = new FileSignatureScanner()
                    .Scan(rules.Deep.FileSignatures, roots, ct, audit);
                result.FileSignatures = sigReport;
                result.Findings.AddRange(sigFindings);
            }

            if (result.Findings.Count == 0 && result.Overlays.Count == 0)
            {
                result.Notes.Add("深度扫描未发现句柄/驱动/overlay 相关线索");
            }
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException)
        {
            throw;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            result.NotChecked.Add(new NotChecked { Target = "deep-scan", Reason = ex.Message });
        }

        return result;
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Scanning/FileScanner.cs`:

```cs
using EagleEye.Core;
using EagleEye.Models;
using EagleEye.Rules;

namespace EagleEye.Scanning;

public sealed class FileScanResult
{
    public int FilesScanned { get; set; }
    public int FilesHashed { get; set; }
    public bool Truncated { get; set; }
    public List<Finding> Findings { get; } = new();
    public List<NotChecked> NotChecked { get; } = new();
}

/// <summary>
/// 文件扫描：只读遍历配置目录，按文件名规则 + SHA256 名单比对。
/// 不写文件、不改属性、不跟随符号链接。
/// </summary>
public sealed class FileScanner
{
    public FileScanResult Scan(RuleSet rules, IEnumerable<string> extraRoots, CancellationToken ct, AuditLogger? audit = null)
    {
        var result = new FileScanResult();
        var config = rules.Options;

        var nameRules = rules.Rules.Where(r => r.Enabled && IsType(r.Type, RuleTypes.FileName)).ToList();
        var hashRules = rules.Rules.Where(r => r.Enabled && IsType(r.Type, RuleTypes.FileHash)).ToList();

        bool needHash = hashRules.Count > 0 || rules.Whitelist.Files.Any(w => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(w.Hash));

        var hashRuleMap = hashRules
            .Where(r => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(r.Hash))
            .GroupBy(r => r.Hash!.ToLowerInvariant())
            .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.ToList());

        var sizePrefilter = hashRules
            .Where(r => r.Size.HasValue)
            .Select(r => r.Size!.Value)
            .ToHashSet();

        var extensions = new HashSet<string>(
            config.IncludeExtensions.Select(e => e.StartsWith('.') ? e : "." + e),
            StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        var roots = new List<string>();
        foreach (string raw in config.ScanRoots.Concat(extraRoots))
        {
            string expanded = Environment.ExpandEnvironmentVariables(raw);
            if (Directory.Exists(expanded))
            {
                roots.Add(expanded);
            }
            else
            {
                result.NotChecked.Add(new NotChecked { Target = raw, Reason = "扫描目录不存在或不可访问，已跳过" });
            }
        }

        if (roots.Count == 0)
        {
            return result;
        }

        foreach (string file in EnumerateFiles(roots, config.MaxDepth, result, ct))
        {
            ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();

            if (result.FilesScanned >= config.MaxFiles)
            {
                result.Truncated = true;
                result.NotChecked.Add(new NotChecked
                {
                    Target = string.Join("; ", roots),
                    Reason = $"已达到 maxFiles={config.MaxFiles} 上限，扫描不完整"
                });
                break;
            }

            string fileName = Path.GetFileName(file);
            if (!extensions.Contains(Path.GetExtension(file)))
            {
                continue;
            }

            long size = 0;
            try
            {
                size = new FileInfo(file).Length;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                result.NotChecked.Add(new NotChecked { Target = fileName, Path = file, Reason = $"读取文件大小失败: {ex.Message}" });
                continue;
            }

            if (size > config.MaxFileSizeBytes)
            {
                result.NotChecked.Add(new NotChecked
                {
                    Target = fileName,
                    Path = file,
                    Reason = $"文件过大（{size} 字节），超过 maxFileSizeBytes，未计算哈希"
                });
                continue;
            }

            result.FilesScanned++;

            string? sha256 = null;
            bool sizeMatchesRule = sizePrefilter.Count == 0 || sizePrefilter.Contains(size);

            if (needHash && sizeMatchesRule && result.FilesHashed < config.MaxHashFiles && size > 0)
            {
                sha256 = Hashing.TrySha256(file, out string? hashError);
                if (sha256 is null)
                {
                    result.NotChecked.Add(new NotChecked { Target = fileName, Path = file, Reason = $"计算 SHA256 失败: {hashError}" });
                }
                else
                {
                    result.FilesHashed++;
                }
            }

            var (whitelisted, wlReason, suppressRules) = EvaluateWhitelist(rules, file, fileName, sha256);

            foreach (var rule in nameRules)
            {
                if (!PatternMatcher.IsMatch(fileName, rule.Pattern, rule.Match)) continue;
                result.Findings.Add(Build(rule, fileName, file, size, sha256, whitelisted, wlReason, suppressRules, Scoring.WeightOf(rules, rule.Category), "文件名命中", "fileName", fileName));
            }

            if (sha256 is not null && hashRuleMap.TryGetValue(sha256, out var matchedHashRules))
            {
                foreach (var rule in matchedHashRules)
                {
                    result.Findings.Add(Build(rule, fileName, file, size, sha256, whitelisted, wlReason, suppressRules, Scoring.WeightOf(rules, rule.Category), "SHA256 命中已知名单", "sha256", sha256));
                }
            }
        }

        result.Findings.Sort((a, b) => b.Score.CompareTo(a.Score));
        return result;
    }

    private static bool IsType(string a, string b) => string.Equals(a, b, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    private static bool IsSuppressed(bool whitelisted, string[]? suppressRules, string ruleId)
    {
        if (!whitelisted) return false;
        if (suppressRules is null || suppressRules.Length == 0) return true;
        return suppressRules.Any(r => string.Equals(r, ruleId, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
    }

    private static Finding Build(
        Rule rule,
        string fileName,
        string fullPath,
        long size,
        string? sha256,
        bool whitelisted,
        string? whitelistReason,
        string[]? suppressRules,
        double categoryWeight,
        string fallbackDescription,
        string matchedField,
        string matchedValue)
    {
        bool suppressed = IsSuppressed(whitelisted, suppressRules, rule.Id);
        double score = Scoring.ComputeScore(rule.Severity, rule.Confidence, categoryWeight);
        return new Finding
        {
            RuleId = rule.Id,
            RuleType = rule.Type,
            Target = fileName,
            TargetPath = fullPath,
            Pid = null,
            Severity = rule.Severity,
            Confidence = rule.Confidence,
            Score = score,
            RiskLevel = Scoring.LevelOf(score),
            Description = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Description) ? fallbackDescription : rule.Description!,
            Advice = rule.Advice,
            Category = rule.Category,
            Suppressed = suppressed,
            SuppressReason = suppressed ? whitelistReason : null,
            SuppressedBy = suppressed ? "whitelist" : null,
            Evidence = new Dictionary<string, object?>
            {
                ["matchedField"] = matchedField,
                ["matchedValue"] = matchedValue,
                ["fileName"] = fileName,
                ["fullPath"] = fullPath,
                ["sizeBytes"] = size,
                ["sha256"] = sha256
            }
        };
    }

    private static (bool Matched, string? Reason, string[]? SuppressRules) EvaluateWhitelist(RuleSet rules, string fullPath, string fileName, string? sha256)
    {
        foreach (var entry in rules.Whitelist.Files)
        {
            if (RuleLoader.IsExpired(entry)) continue;

            bool hit = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.Pattern) && PatternMatcher.IsMatch(fileName, entry.Pattern, entry.Match);
            if (!hit && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.Hash) && sha256 is not null)
            {
                hit = string.Equals(entry.Hash, sha256, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            }

            if (!hit) continue;
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.Hash) &&
                !string.Equals(entry.Hash, sha256, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;

            return (true, entry.Reason ?? $"文件白名单: {entry.Pattern ?? entry.Hash}", entry.SuppressRules);
        }

        foreach (var entry in rules.Whitelist.Paths)
        {
            if (RuleLoader.IsExpired(entry)) continue;
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.Pattern)) continue;
            if (!PatternMatcher.IsMatch(fullPath, entry.Pattern, entry.Match)) continue;

            return (true, entry.Reason ?? $"路径白名单: {entry.Pattern}", entry.SuppressRules);
        }

        return (false, null, null);
    }

    private static IEnumerable<string> EnumerateFiles(List<string> roots, int maxDepth, FileScanResult result, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var stack = new Stack<(string Dir, int Depth)>();
        foreach (string root in roots)
        {
            stack.Push((root, 0));
        }

        while (stack.Count > 0)
        {
            ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
            var (dir, depth) = stack.Pop();

            string[] files;
            try
            {
                files = Directory.GetFiles(dir);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                result.NotChecked.Add(new NotChecked { Target = dir, Reason = $"目录不可读，已跳过: {ex.Message}" });
                continue;
            }

            foreach (string f in files)
            {
                yield return f;
            }

            if (depth >= maxDepth)
            {
                continue;
            }

            string[] subDirs;
            try
            {
                subDirs = Directory.GetDirectories(dir);
            }
            catch
            {
                continue;
            }

            foreach (string sub in subDirs)
            {
                try
                {
                    var info = new DirectoryInfo(sub);
                    if (info.Attributes.HasFlag(FileAttributes.ReparsePoint))
                    {
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                catch
                {
                    continue;
                }

                stack.Push((sub, depth + 1));
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Scanning/FileSignatureScanner.cs`:

```cs
using System.Text;
using EagleEye.Core;
using EagleEye.Models;

namespace EagleEye.Scanning;

/// <summary>
/// 文件特征码扫描：只读文件前 N 字节，做 ASCII / 十六进制字节匹配。
/// 用途：识别没有哈希记录、但二进制里有已知特征的样本（如注入器字符串、驱动名）。
/// 只读：FileStream(Read) + 只读首部缓冲，不执行、不解包、不写回。
/// </summary>
public sealed class FileSignatureScanner
{
    public (FileSignatureReport Report, List<Finding> Findings) Scan(
        FileSignatureOptions options,
        IEnumerable<string> roots,
        CancellationToken ct,
        AuditLogger? audit = null)
    {
        var report = new FileSignatureReport { Enabled = options.Enabled };
        var findings = new List<Finding>();
        if (!options.Enabled || options.Rules.Count == 0)
        {
            return (report, findings);
        }

        var patterns = new List<(FileSignatureRule Rule, byte[] Bytes)>();
        foreach (var rule in options.Rules.Where(r => r.Enabled))
        {
            byte[]? bytes = rule.PatternType.Equals("hex", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                ? ParseHex(rule.Pattern)
                : Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes(rule.Pattern);

            if (bytes is null || bytes.Length == 0)
            {
                report.Error = $"规则 {rule.Id} 的特征码格式不正确";
                continue;
            }

            patterns.Add((rule, bytes));
        }

        if (patterns.Count == 0) return (report, findings);

        var extensions = new HashSet<string>(
            options.IncludeExtensions.Select(e => e.StartsWith('.') ? e : "." + e),
            StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        var buffer = new byte[options.MaxBytesPerFile];
        int scanned = 0;

        foreach (string file in Enumerate(roots, ct))
        {
            if (scanned >= options.MaxFiles)
            {
                report.Error = $"达到 maxFiles={options.MaxFiles} 上限，扫描不完整";
                break;
            }

            if (!extensions.Contains(Path.GetExtension(file))) continue;

            long size;
            try { size = new FileInfo(file).Length; }
            catch { continue; }

            if (size == 0 || size > options.MaxBytesPerFile) continue;

            int read;
            try
            {
                using var fs = new FileStream(file, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.ReadWrite | FileShare.Delete);
                read = fs.Read(buffer, 0, (int)Math.Min(size, buffer.Length));
            }
            catch
            {
                continue;
            }

            scanned++;
            report.FilesScanned = scanned;

            var span = new ReadOnlySpan<byte>(buffer, 0, read);

            foreach (var (rule, bytes) in patterns)
            {
                ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
                if (span.IndexOf(bytes) < 0) continue;

                string? sha = Hashing.TrySha256(file, out _);
                report.FilesMatched++;
                report.Hits.Add(new FileSignatureHit
                {
                    RuleId = rule.Id,
                    FileName = Path.GetFileName(file),
                    Path = file,
                    Size = size,
                    Sha256 = sha,
                    Pattern = rule.Pattern
                });

                double score = Scoring.ComputeScore(rule.Severity, rule.Confidence);
                findings.Add(new Finding
                {
                    RuleId = rule.Id,
                    RuleType = "file_signature",
                    Category = "file_signature",
                    Target = Path.GetFileName(file),
                    TargetPath = file,
                    Severity = rule.Severity,
                    Confidence = rule.Confidence,
                    Score = score,
                    RiskLevel = Scoring.LevelOf(score),
                    Description = rule.Description ?? $"文件命中特征码：{rule.Pattern}",
                    Evidence = new Dictionary<string, object?>
                    {
                        ["path"] = file,
                        ["sizeBytes"] = size,
                        ["sha256"] = sha,
                        ["patternType"] = rule.PatternType,
                        ["pattern"] = rule.Pattern,
                        ["note"] = "只读文件首部字节做匹配，未执行、未解包"
                    }
                });

                break;   // 一个文件只记一条，避免刷屏
            }
        }

        audit?.Write("file.signature", new { scanned = report.FilesScanned, matched = report.FilesMatched });
        return (report, findings);
    }

    private static byte[]? ParseHex(string? hex)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(hex)) return null;

        string clean = hex.Replace(" ", "").Replace("-", "");
        if (clean.Contains('?')) return null;   // 不支持通配符，避免误报

        if (clean.Length % 2 != 0) return null;

        var bytes = new byte[clean.Length / 2];
        for (int i = 0; i < bytes.Length; i++)
        {
            if (!byte.TryParse(clean.AsSpan(i * 2, 2), System.Globalization.NumberStyles.HexNumber, null, out bytes[i]))
            {
                return null;
            }
        }

        return bytes;
    }

    private static IEnumerable<string> Enumerate(IEnumerable<string> roots, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        foreach (string root in roots)
        {
            string expanded = Environment.ExpandEnvironmentVariables(root);
            if (!Directory.Exists(expanded)) continue;

            var stack = new Stack<string>();
            stack.Push(expanded);
            int guard = 0;

            while (stack.Count > 0 && guard++ < 20000)
            {
                ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
                string dir = stack.Pop();

                string[] files;
                try { files = Directory.GetFiles(dir); }
                catch { continue; }

                foreach (string f in files) yield return f;

                string[] subs;
                try { subs = Directory.GetDirectories(dir); }
                catch { continue; }

                foreach (string s in subs)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        if (new DirectoryInfo(s).Attributes.HasFlag(FileAttributes.ReparsePoint)) continue;
                    }
                    catch { continue; }

                    stack.Push(s);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Scanning/HandleScanner.cs`:

```cs
using EagleEye.Core;
using EagleEye.Models;
using EagleEye.Native;

namespace EagleEye.Scanning;

/// <summary>
/// 句柄线索：找出"谁以 VM_READ / VM_WRITE / CREATE_THREAD 打开了某个进程"。
/// 这是软件外挂（读内存、远程线程）在本机最直接的痕迹。
///
/// 只读：枚举系统句柄表 + DuplicateHandle 读目标 PID；不读任何进程内存、不拦截、不修改对象。
/// 需要管理员权限（PROCESS_DUP_HANDLE）；失败时降级并在报告中注明。
/// </summary>
public sealed class HandleScanner
{
    public (HandleScanReport Report, List<Finding> Findings) Scan(
        HandleScanOptions options,
        ProcessScanResult processes,
        CancellationToken ct,
        AuditLogger? audit = null)
    {
        var report = new HandleScanReport { Enabled = options.Enabled, Attempted = options.Enabled };
        var findings = new List<Finding>();

        if (!options.Enabled)
        {
            return (report, findings);
        }

        var byPid = processes.Processes
            .GroupBy(p => p.Pid)
            .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.First());

        // 目标 PID → 父 PID：CreateProcess 会给父进程一个 ALL_ACCESS 句柄，属正常，必须排除
        var parentOf = processes.Processes
            .GroupBy(p => p.Pid)
            .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.First().ParentPid);

        var refs = HandleInterop.FindProcessHandles(out int total, out int highAccess, out string? error);
        report.HandlesEnumerated = total;
        report.HighAccessHandles = highAccess;

        if (error is not null)
        {
            report.Error = error;
            audit?.Write("handle.scan", new { result = "failed", error, note = "需要管理员权限（PROCESS_DUP_HANDLE）" });
            return (report, findings);
        }

        audit?.Write("handle.scan", new
        {
            handles = total,
            highAccess,
            pairs = refs.Count,
            note = "只读枚举：DuplicateHandle 读目标 PID，未读取任何进程内存"
        });

        var seen = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        foreach (var reference in refs)
        {
            ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();

            byPid.TryGetValue(reference.HolderPid, out ProcessRecord? holder);
            byPid.TryGetValue(reference.TargetPid, out ProcessRecord? target);

            // 目标进程不在快照里（已退出 / ACE 拉起的独立进程）→ 无法判断价值，只记录不告警
            if (target is null)
            {
                report.UnresolvedTargets++;
                continue;
            }

            string holderName = holder?.Name ?? $"pid{reference.HolderPid}";
            string targetName = target?.Name ?? $"pid{reference.TargetPid}";

            // 祖先关系：CreateProcess 会给父进程（乃至更上层）ALL_ACCESS 句柄，属正常
            // 只比直接父进程不够 —— 游戏 → UnrealCEFSubProcess → browser.exe 是隔代的
            if (IsAncestor(parentOf, reference.HolderPid, reference.TargetPid))
            {
                continue;
            }

            // 同映像（同一程序的多进程/子进程）也跳过
            if (holder is not null && target is not null &&
                !string.IsNullOrEmpty(holder.ImagePath) &&
                string.Equals(holder.ImagePath, target.ImagePath, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                continue;
            }

            // 同名进程互持（受保护进程读不到路径时的兜底）：同一款程序内部句柄，不构成外部读取
            if (holder is not null && target is not null &&
                string.Equals(holder.Name, target.Name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                continue;
            }

            if (options.HolderWhitelist.Any(p => PatternMatcher.IsMatch(holderName, p, null))) continue;
            if (options.TargetWhitelist.Any(p => PatternMatcher.IsMatch(targetName, p, null))) continue;

            // 微软签名的持有者（杀软、系统服务）大量以 VM_READ 打开进程，默认放过
            if (holder?.Signature.SignerSubject is { } signer &&
                signer.Contains("Microsoft", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                continue;
            }

            report.HoldersChecked++;

            var holderInfo = new HandleHolder
            {
                HolderPid = reference.HolderPid,
                HolderName = holderName,
                HolderPath = holder?.ImagePath,
                HolderSignature = holder?.Signature.State,
                TargetPid = reference.TargetPid,
                TargetName = targetName,
                AccessMask = $"0x{reference.GrantedAccess:X}",
                AccessRights = HandleInterop.DescribeAccess(reference.GrantedAccess).Split(' ')[0].Split('|').ToList()
            };

            report.Holders.Add(holderInfo);

            foreach (var rule in options.Rules.Where(r => r.Enabled).OrderByDescending(r => r.Severity))
            {
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.TargetProcessPattern) &&
                    !PatternMatcher.IsMatch(targetName, rule.TargetProcessPattern, null))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                bool accessHit = rule.AccessMaskAny is null || rule.AccessMaskAny.Length == 0;
                if (!accessHit)
                {
                    foreach (string mask in rule.AccessMaskAny!)
                    {
                        string hex = mask.StartsWith("0x", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? mask[2..] : mask;
                        if (!uint.TryParse(hex, System.Globalization.NumberStyles.HexNumber, null, out uint m)) continue;
                        if ((reference.GrantedAccess & m) == m) { accessHit = true; break; }
                    }
                }

                if (!accessHit) continue;

                // 同一对 (持有者, 目标) 只记一条，避免一个 ALL_ACCESS 句柄命中 3 条规则被重复累加
                string key = $"{reference.HolderPid}|{reference.TargetPid}";
                if (!seen.Add(key)) break;

                double confidence = rule.Confidence;
                if (reference.GrantedAccess == 0x1FFFFF)
                {
                    // ALL_ACCESS 常见于进程创建/宿主附加，置信度大幅打折
                    confidence = Math.Round(confidence * 0.5, 2);
                }

                double score = Scoring.ComputeScore(rule.Severity, confidence);
                findings.Add(new Finding
                {
                    RuleId = rule.Id,
                    RuleType = "handle_access",
                    Category = rule.Category ?? "handle",
                    Target = holderName,
```
