```cs
                    TargetPath = holder?.ImagePath,
                    Pid = reference.HolderPid,
                    Severity = rule.Severity,
                    Confidence = confidence,
                    Score = score,
                    RiskLevel = Scoring.LevelOf(score),
                    Description = rule.Description ?? $"{holderName} 以 {HandleInterop.DescribeAccess(reference.GrantedAccess)} 打开了 {targetName}",
                    Advice = rule.Advice,
                    Evidence = new Dictionary<string, object?>
                    {
                        ["holderPid"] = reference.HolderPid,
                        ["holderPath"] = holder?.ImagePath,
                        ["holderSignature"] = holder?.Signature.State,
                        ["targetPid"] = reference.TargetPid,
                        ["targetName"] = targetName,
                        ["grantedAccess"] = $"0x{reference.GrantedAccess:X}",
                        ["accessMeaning"] = HandleInterop.DescribeAccess(reference.GrantedAccess),
                        ["memoryRead"] = false,
                        ["note"] = "仅枚举句柄表并复制句柄读取目标 PID，未读取目标进程内存"
                    }
                });

                break;
            }
        }

        return (report, findings);
    }
    /// <summary>holder 是否为 target 的祖先（最多向上追 6 层）。</summary>
    private static bool IsAncestor(Dictionary<int, int> parentOf, int holder, int target, int maxDepth = 6)
    {
        int current = target;
        for (int i = 0; i < maxDepth; i++)
        {
            if (!parentOf.TryGetValue(current, out int parent) || parent <= 4) return false;
            if (parent == holder) return true;
            current = parent;
        }

        return false;
    }
}

```

`src/EagleEye/Scanning/InjectionTraceScanner.cs`:

```cs
using EagleEye.Core;
using EagleEye.Models;
using EagleEye.Native;

namespace EagleEye.Scanning;

/// <summary>
/// 注入痕迹检测（**不需要样本哈希**）。
///
/// 原理：外挂 DLL 必须出现在目标进程的模块列表里，而它通常满足至少一条：
///   1. 模块文件在磁盘上不存在（内存加载 / 反射注入 / manual mapping）
///   2. 模块路径在临时目录、下载目录、用户公共目录
///   3. 模块没有有效数字签名（且不在 Windows 目录下）
/// 这三条都是**结构性痕迹**，与具体是哪个外挂无关，所以没有样本也能查。
///
/// 只读：复用已枚举的模块列表 + 只读文件属性与 Authenticode 校验，
///       不读进程内存、不注入、不 Hook。
/// </summary>
public sealed class InjectionTraceScanner
{
    private static readonly string[] SuspiciousPathMarkers =
    {
        "\\Temp\\", "\\Downloads\\", "\\Users\\Public\\", "\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\",
        "\\Desktop\\", "\\Documents\\", "\\ProgramData\\Temp\\"
    };

    public (List<Finding> Findings, List<InjectionFindingInfo> Traces) Scan(
        InjectionScanOptions options,
        ProcessScanResult processes,
        SignatureChecker signatures,
        CancellationToken ct,
        AuditLogger? audit = null)
    {
        var findings = new List<Finding>();
        var traces = new List<InjectionFindingInfo>();

        if (!options.Enabled || options.TargetProcessPatterns.Count == 0)
        {
            return (findings, traces);
        }

        string windowsDir = Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.Windows);

        foreach (var process in processes.Processes)
        {
            ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();

            bool isTarget = options.TargetProcessPatterns.Any(p => PatternMatcher.IsMatch(process.Name, p, null));
            if (!isTarget) continue;
            if (process.Modules.Count == 0) continue;

            foreach (var module in process.Modules)
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(module.Name)) continue;
                if (options.ModuleWhitelist.Any(w => PatternMatcher.IsMatch(module.Name, w, null))) continue;

                string? path = module.Path;

                // 1) 磁盘上不存在 —— manual mapping / 反射注入的典型特征
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path) || !File.Exists(path))
                {
                    Add(findings, traces, options.MissingFileSeverity, 0.75,
                        "inj.module_missing_file", process, module,
                        $"模块「{module.Name}」在磁盘上不存在（内存加载/反射注入特征）",
                        "模块没有对应磁盘文件，这是手动映射注入最直接的本机证据", null);
                    continue;
                }

                // 2) 路径异常
                string marker = SuspiciousPathMarkers.FirstOrDefault(m =>
                    path.Contains(m, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) ?? string.Empty;

                if (marker.Length > 0)
                {
                    Add(findings, traces, options.SuspiciousPathSeverity, 0.55,
                        "inj.module_suspicious_path", process, module,
                        $"模块「{module.Name}」从可疑目录加载：{path}",
                        "正常系统/游戏模块不会放在临时目录或下载目录", null);
                    continue;
                }

                // 3) 非 Windows 目录且无有效签名（Windows 目录下的系统模块跳过，省 IO）
                if (path.StartsWith(windowsDir, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;

                var signature = signatures.Check(path);
                if (signature.State is SignatureStates.Unsigned or SignatureStates.BadDigest
                    or SignatureStates.UntrustedRoot or SignatureStates.InvalidChain or SignatureStates.Distrusted)
                {
                    Add(findings, traces, options.UnsignedSeverity, 0.35,
                        "inj.module_unsigned", process, module,
                        $"模块「{module.Name}」无有效签名（{signature.State}）：{path}",
                        "非系统目录 + 无签名，需人工确认来源", signature.State);
                }
            }
        }

        audit?.Write("injection.scan", new
        {
            traces = traces.Count,
            note = "只读模块枚举 + 文件存在性 + 验签；未读进程内存"
        });

        return (findings, traces);
    }

    private static void Add(
        List<Finding> findings,
        List<InjectionFindingInfo> traces,
        int severity,
        double confidence,
        string ruleId,
        ProcessRecord process,
        ModuleInfo module,
        string description,
        string advice,
        string? signatureState)
    {
        double score = Scoring.ComputeScore(severity, confidence);
        findings.Add(new Finding
        {
            RuleId = ruleId,
            RuleType = "module_injection",
            Category = "injection",
            Target = process.Name,
            TargetPath = process.ImagePath,
            Pid = process.Pid,
            Severity = severity,
            Confidence = confidence,
            Score = score,
            RiskLevel = Scoring.LevelOf(score),
            Description = description,
            Advice = advice,
            Evidence = new Dictionary<string, object?>
            {
                ["pid"] = process.Pid,
                ["processPath"] = process.ImagePath,
                ["module"] = module.Name,
                ["modulePath"] = module.Path,
                ["moduleSize"] = module.Size,
                ["signatureState"] = signatureState,
                ["memoryRead"] = false,
                ["note"] = "仅比对模块列表与磁盘文件，未读取进程内存"
            }
        });

        traces.Add(new InjectionFindingInfo
        {
            Target = process.Name,
            Pid = process.Pid,
            Module = module.Name,
            ModulePath = module.Path,
            Reason = description,
            SignatureState = signatureState
        });
    }
}

```

`src/EagleEye/Scanning/InputBehaviorSampler.cs`:

```cs
using EagleEye.Models;
using EagleEye.Native;

namespace EagleEye.Scanning;

/// <summary>
/// 输入行为统计（信息级）。
///
/// 实现选择与理由：
///   1. **不使用 WH_MOUSE_LL 低级钩子**。SetWindowsHookEx 是系统级全局钩子，
///      属于本项目红线（不 Hook 任何 API），且会被反作弊视为高风险行为。
///   2. **不使用 Raw Input**。RIDEV_INPUTSINK 需要后台收包，等价于全局输入捕获，
///      同样踩线且需要窗口与消息循环。
///   3. **不使用系统性能计数器**。Windows 没有暴露"鼠标移动平滑度"这类计数器。
///   4. 因此只能用 GetCursorPos 定时轮询：无钩子、无注册、无消息循环，
///      对目标进程零侵入，反作弊侧完全不可见。
///
/// 只计算聚合指标，采样缓冲区只在本地栈上，函数返回即丢弃，
/// 不写盘、不关联窗口、不记录任何坐标轨迹。
/// </summary>
public static class InputBehaviorSampler
{
    private const double StraightAngleDegrees = 8.0;
    private const double ConstantVelocityTolerance = 0.15;

    public static VisionBehavioralHint Sample(VisionBehaviorOptions options, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var hint = new VisionBehavioralHint
        {
            DurationMs = Math.Clamp(options.DurationMs, 500, 60000)
        };

        int interval = Math.Clamp(options.SampleMs, 8, 200);
        int totalSamples = hint.DurationMs / interval;

        if (totalSamples < options.MinSamples)
        {
            hint.SkipReason = $"采样窗口太短（{totalSamples} 个采样点 < MinSamples {options.MinSamples}），已跳过";
            return hint;
        }

        var points = new List<(int X, int Y)>(totalSamples + 1);

        try
        {
            for (int i = 0; i <= totalSamples; i++)
            {
                ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();

                if (!WindowApi.GetCursorPos(out WindowApi.POINT p))
                {
                    hint.SkipReason = "GetCursorPos 调用失败，已跳过行为统计";
                    return hint;
                }

                points.Add((p.X, p.Y));
                Thread.Sleep(interval);
            }
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException)
        {
            hint.SkipReason = "扫描被取消，行为统计未完成";
            return hint;
        }

        return Analyze(points, hint, options);
    }

    private static VisionBehavioralHint Analyze(List<(int X, int Y)> points, VisionBehavioralHint hint, VisionBehaviorOptions options)
    {
        hint.Collected = true;
        hint.SampleCount = points.Count;

        var deltas = new List<(double Dx, double Dy, double Speed)>(points.Count);

        for (int i = 1; i < points.Count; i++)
        {
            double dx = points[i].X - points[i - 1].X;
            double dy = points[i].Y - points[i - 1].Y;
            deltas.Add((dx, dy, Math.Sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)));
        }

        if (deltas.Count == 0)
        {
            hint.Collected = false;
            hint.SkipReason = "没有采集到移动样本";
            return hint;
        }

        int zeroDelta = deltas.Count(d => d.Speed < 0.5);
        hint.ZeroDeltaRatio = Math.Round((double)zeroDelta / deltas.Count, 3);

        // 只对"有移动"的相邻采样做角度/速度统计
        var moving = deltas.Where(d => d.Speed >= 0.5).ToList();
        if (moving.Count < 3)
        {
            hint.Description = "本段时间内几乎没有鼠标移动，统计无意义";
            hint.Confidence = 0.05;
            return hint;
        }

        int straightPairs = 0;
        int constantPairs = 0;
        int pairs = 0;

        for (int i = 1; i < moving.Count; i++)
        {
            var a = moving[i - 1];
            var b = moving[i];

            double dot = a.Dx * b.Dx + a.Dy * b.Dy;
            double norm = a.Speed * b.Speed;
            if (norm <= 0) continue;

            double cos = Math.Clamp(dot / norm, -1.0, 1.0);
            double angle = Math.Acos(cos) * 180.0 / Math.PI;

            if (angle <= StraightAngleDegrees) straightPairs++;

            double mean = (a.Speed + b.Speed) / 2.0;
            if (mean > 0 && Math.Abs(a.Speed - b.Speed) / mean <= ConstantVelocityTolerance) constantPairs++;

            pairs++;
        }

        if (pairs == 0)
        {
            hint.Description = "样本不足以计算方向一致性";
            hint.Confidence = 0.05;
            return hint;
        }

        hint.StraightLineRatio = Math.Round((double)straightPairs / pairs, 3);
        hint.ConstantVelocityRatio = Math.Round((double)constantPairs / pairs, 3);

        double avgSpeed = moving.Average(d => d.Speed);
        double variance = moving.Average(d => (d.Speed - avgSpeed) * (d.Speed - avgSpeed));

        hint.AverageSpeedPxPerSec = Math.Round(avgSpeed * (1000.0 / Math.Max(1, options.SampleMs)), 1);
        hint.SpeedStdDev = Math.Round(Math.Sqrt(variance), 2);

        bool suspicious =
            hint.StraightLineRatio >= options.StraightLineRatio ||
            hint.ConstantVelocityRatio >= options.ConstantVelocityRatio ||
            hint.ZeroDeltaRatio >= options.ZeroDeltaRatio;

        hint.Severity = suspicious ? 1 : 1;   // 永远是 1：信息级
        hint.Confidence = suspicious ? 0.15 : 0.08;
        hint.Description = suspicious
            ? $"输入轨迹呈现较高机械性（直线化 {hint.StraightLineRatio:P0}，匀速 {hint.ConstantVelocityRatio:P0}，静止占比 {hint.ZeroDeltaRatio:P0}）"
            : $"输入轨迹未见明显机械特征（直线化 {hint.StraightLineRatio:P0}，匀速 {hint.ConstantVelocityRatio:P0}）";

        return hint;
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Scanning/OverlayScanner.cs`:

```cs
using EagleEye.Core;
using EagleEye.Models;
using EagleEye.Native;

namespace EagleEye.Scanning;

/// <summary>
/// 叠加层（overlay）检测：找出"透明/置顶、覆盖在别的进程窗口之上的窗口"。
/// 这是 ESP / AI 框最常见的显示方式（画在游戏画面之上，不改游戏本身）。
///
/// 只读：EnumWindows + 读扩展样式/矩形，不截屏、不点击、不发消息。
/// </summary>
public sealed class OverlayScanner
{
    public List<VisionIndicator> Scan(
        OverlayScanOptions options,
        ProcessScanResult processes,
        CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var results = new List<VisionIndicator>();
        if (!options.Enabled)
        {
            return results;
        }

        var windows = WindowApi.EnumerateVisibleWindows()
            .Where(w => w.Width >= 64 && w.Height >= 64)
            .ToList();

        var byPid = processes.Processes
            .GroupBy(p => p.Pid)
            .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.First());

        foreach (var candidate in windows)
        {
            ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();

            bool layered = (candidate.ExStyle & WindowApi.WS_EX_LAYERED) != 0;
            bool transparent = (candidate.ExStyle & WindowApi.WS_EX_TRANSPARENT) != 0;
            bool topmost = (candidate.ExStyle & WindowApi.WS_EX_TOPMOST) != 0;
            bool noActivate = (candidate.ExStyle & WindowApi.WS_EX_NOACTIVATE) != 0;
            bool toolWindow = (candidate.ExStyle & WindowApi.WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW) != 0;

            if (!layered) continue;
            if (!(transparent || topmost || noActivate)) continue;
            if (candidate.Width < options.MinWidth || candidate.Height < options.MinHeight) continue;

            // 找被它覆盖的、属于其它进程的窗口
            WindowApi.WindowInfo? covered = null;
            double bestRatio = 0;

            foreach (var other in windows)
            {
                if (other.ProcessId == candidate.ProcessId) continue;
                if (other.Handle == candidate.Handle) continue;
                if (other.Area <= 0) continue;

                double ratio = IntersectionArea(candidate, other) / (double)other.Area;
                if (ratio > bestRatio)
                {
                    bestRatio = ratio;
                    covered = other;
                }
            }

            if (covered is null || bestRatio < options.MinAreaRatio) continue;

            byPid.TryGetValue(candidate.ProcessId, out ProcessRecord? owner);
            byPid.TryGetValue(covered.Value.ProcessId, out ProcessRecord? target);

            double confidence = options.Confidence;
            if (owner?.Signature.State is SignatureStates.Unsigned) confidence += 0.1;
            confidence = Math.Round(Math.Clamp(confidence, 0, 0.7), 2);

            double score = Scoring.ComputeScore(options.Severity, confidence);

            results.Add(new VisionIndicator
            {
                Id = "vision.overlay.window",
                Layer = "capture",
                Category = "overlay",
                Target = owner?.Name ?? $"pid{candidate.ProcessId}",
                Pid = candidate.ProcessId,
                Path = owner?.ImagePath,
                SignatureState = owner?.Signature.State,
                Severity = options.Severity,
                Confidence = confidence,
                Score = score,
                Description = $"检测到叠加窗口覆盖在 {target?.Name ?? $"pid{covered.Value.ProcessId}"} 之上" +
                              $"（覆盖 {bestRatio:P0}，Layered/Transparent/Topmost 样式）",
                Advice = "覆盖层也可能是 Discord/Steam/录屏软件的合法覆盖，需结合进程签名与场景复核",
                Evidence = new Dictionary<string, object?>
                {
                    ["overlayHandle"] = $"0x{candidate.Handle.ToInt64():X}",
                    ["overlayTitle"] = candidate.Title,
                    ["overlaySize"] = $"{candidate.Width}x{candidate.Height}",
                    ["exStyle"] = $"0x{candidate.ExStyle:X}",
                    ["layered"] = layered,
                    ["transparent"] = transparent,
                    ["topmost"] = topmost,
                    ["noActivate"] = noActivate,
                    ["toolWindow"] = toolWindow,
                    ["coveredPid"] = covered.Value.ProcessId,
                    ["coveredName"] = target?.Name,
                    ["coveredRatio"] = Math.Round(bestRatio, 3),
                    ["memoryRead"] = false,
                    ["screenshotTaken"] = false
                }
            });
        }

        return results
            .GroupBy(v => v.Pid)
            .Select(g => g.OrderByDescending(v => v.Score).First())
            .ToList();
    }

    private static double IntersectionArea(WindowApi.WindowInfo a, WindowApi.WindowInfo b)
    {
        int left = Math.Max(a.Left, b.Left);
        int top = Math.Max(a.Top, b.Top);
        int right = Math.Min(a.Left + a.Width, b.Left + b.Width);
        int bottom = Math.Min(a.Top + a.Height, b.Top + b.Height);

        if (right <= left || bottom <= top) return 0;
        return (right - left) * (bottom - top);
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Scanning/ProcessScanner.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using EagleEye.Core;
using EagleEye.Models;
using EagleEye.Native;
using EagleEye.Rules;

namespace EagleEye.Scanning;

public sealed class ProcessScanResult
{
    public List<ProcessRecord> Processes { get; } = new();
    public List<Finding> Findings { get; } = new();
    public List<NotChecked> NotChecked { get; } = new();
    public int SelfSkipped { get; set; }
}

/// <summary>
/// 进程扫描：Toolhelp 枚举 + 最小权限打开 + 名称/路径/命令行/模块/签名规则匹配。
/// 全程只读：只 OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION)，不做任何内存读取或写入。
/// </summary>
public sealed class ProcessScanner
{
    private readonly SignatureChecker _signatures;

    public ProcessScanner(SignatureChecker signatures)
    {
        _signatures = signatures;
    }

    private readonly record struct ProcEntry(int Pid, int ParentPid, int Threads, string Name);

    public ProcessScanResult Scan(RuleSet rules, CancellationToken ct, AuditLogger? audit = null, bool forceModules = false)
    {
        var result = new ProcessScanResult();

        var activeRules = rules.Rules.Where(r => r.Enabled).ToList();
        var nameRules = activeRules.Where(r => Eq(r.Type, RuleTypes.ProcessName)).ToList();
        var pathRules = activeRules.Where(r => Eq(r.Type, RuleTypes.ProcessPath)).ToList();
        var cmdRules = activeRules.Where(r => Eq(r.Type, RuleTypes.ProcessCommandLine)).ToList();
        var moduleRules = activeRules.Where(r => Eq(r.Type, RuleTypes.ModuleName)).ToList();
        var signatureRules = activeRules.Where(r => Eq(r.Type, RuleTypes.Signature)).ToList();

        bool needModules = moduleRules.Count > 0 || forceModules;
        bool needSignature = signatureRules.Count > 0 || forceModules;   // 视觉线索需要签名状态
        bool needHashForWhitelist = rules.Whitelist.Processes.Any(w => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(w.Hash))
                                    || rules.Whitelist.Paths.Any(w => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(w.Hash));

        int selfPid = Environment.ProcessId;
        var entries = EnumerateProcessEntries(ct);

        var findings = new ConcurrentBag<Finding>();
        var records = new ConcurrentBag<ProcessRecord>();
        var skipped = new ConcurrentBag<NotChecked>();
        int selfSkipped = 0;

        var parallelOptions = new ParallelOptions
        {
            MaxDegreeOfParallelism = Math.Clamp(Environment.ProcessorCount, 2, 8),
            CancellationToken = ct
        };

        Parallel.ForEach(entries, parallelOptions, entry =>
        {
            ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();

            // 跳过自身，避免把本工具的模块名当成可疑模块（自我误报）
            if (entry.Pid == selfPid)
            {
                Interlocked.Increment(ref selfSkipped);
                return;
            }

            var rec = new ProcessRecord
            {
                Pid = entry.Pid,
                ParentPid = entry.ParentPid,
                ThreadCount = entry.Threads,
                Name = entry.Name
            };

            var reasons = new List<string>();
            IntPtr handle = IntPtr.Zero;

            try
            {
                handle = NativeMethods.OpenProcess(NativeMethods.ProcessQueryLimitedInformation, false, (uint)entry.Pid);
                if (handle == IntPtr.Zero)
                {
                    int err = Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
                    audit?.ProcessOpen(entry.Pid, entry.Name, NativeMethods.ProcessQueryLimitedInformation, false, err, null);
                    rec.AccessError = $"OpenProcess 失败（Win32={err} {new Win32Exception(err).Message}）";
                    reasons.Add("受保护进程或权限不足：仅完成名称级检查，路径/命令行/模块/签名未采集");
                }
                else
                {
                    rec.ImagePath = NativeMethods.TryGetImagePath(handle);
                    rec.CommandLine = NativeMethods.TryGetCommandLine(handle);
                    audit?.ProcessOpen(entry.Pid, entry.Name, NativeMethods.ProcessQueryLimitedInformation, true, 0, rec.ImagePath);
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                rec.AccessError = ex.Message;
                reasons.Add($"打开进程异常：{ex.Message}");
            }
            finally
            {
                if (handle != IntPtr.Zero)
                {
                    NativeMethods.CloseHandle(handle);
                }
            }

            if (needModules && entry.Pid > 4)
            {
                var modules = TryGetModules(entry.Pid, out string? moduleError);
                if (modules is null)
                {
                    rec.ModuleError = moduleError;
                    reasons.Add($"模块列表不可枚举：{moduleError}");
                }
                else
                {
                    rec.Modules = modules;
                }
            }

            if (needSignature && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rec.ImagePath))
            {
                rec.Signature = _signatures.Check(rec.ImagePath);
                audit?.SignatureChecked(rec.ImagePath!, rec.Signature.State);
            }

            string? processHash = null;
            if (needHashForWhitelist && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rec.ImagePath))
            {
                processHash = Hashing.TrySha256(rec.ImagePath!, out _);
            }

            var (whitelisted, wlReason, suppressRules) = EvaluateWhitelist(rules, rec, processHash);

            foreach (var rule in nameRules)
            {
                if (!PatternMatcher.IsMatch(rec.Name, rule.Pattern, rule.Match)) continue;
                findings.Add(Build(rule, rec, rec.Name, whitelisted, wlReason, suppressRules, Scoring.WeightOf(rules, rule.Category), "进程名命中", new()
                {
                    ["matchedField"] = "processName",
                    ["matchedValue"] = rec.Name,
                    ["pid"] = rec.Pid,
                    ["parentPid"] = rec.ParentPid,
                    ["imagePath"] = rec.ImagePath
                }));
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rec.ImagePath))
            {
                foreach (var rule in pathRules)
                {
                    if (!PatternMatcher.IsMatch(rec.ImagePath, rule.Pattern, rule.Match)) continue;
                    findings.Add(Build(rule, rec, rec.Name, whitelisted, wlReason, suppressRules, Scoring.WeightOf(rules, rule.Category), "映像路径命中", new()
                    {
                        ["matchedField"] = "imagePath",
                        ["matchedValue"] = rec.ImagePath,
                        ["pid"] = rec.Pid,
                        ["signatureState"] = rec.Signature.State,
                        ["signer"] = rec.Signature.SignerSubject
                    }));
                }
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rec.CommandLine))
            {
                foreach (var rule in cmdRules)
                {
                    if (!PatternMatcher.IsMatch(rec.CommandLine, rule.Pattern, rule.Match)) continue;
                    findings.Add(Build(rule, rec, rec.Name, whitelisted, wlReason, suppressRules, Scoring.WeightOf(rules, rule.Category), "命令行命中", new()
                    {
                        ["matchedField"] = "commandLine",
                        ["matchedValue"] = rec.CommandLine,
                        ["pid"] = rec.Pid
                    }));
                }
            }

            if (moduleRules.Count > 0 && rec.Modules.Count > 0)
            {
                foreach (var rule in moduleRules)
                {
                    var hits = rec.Modules
                        .Where(m => PatternMatcher.IsMatch(m.Name, rule.Pattern, rule.Match))
                        .Select(m => new { name = m.Name, path = m.Path })
                        .ToList();

                    if (hits.Count == 0) continue;

                    findings.Add(Build(rule, rec, rec.Name, whitelisted, wlReason, suppressRules, Scoring.WeightOf(rules, rule.Category), "已加载模块命中", new()
                    {
                        ["matchedField"] = "moduleName",
                        ["matchedValue"] = string.Join("; ", hits.Select(h => h.name)),
                        ["matchedModules"] = hits,
                        ["pid"] = rec.Pid,
                        ["imagePath"] = rec.ImagePath
                    }));
                }
            }

            if (needSignature && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rec.ImagePath))
            {
                foreach (var rule in signatureRules)
                {
                    if (!SignatureMatches(rule.Pattern, rec.Signature.State)) continue;
                    findings.Add(Build(rule, rec, rec.Name, whitelisted, wlReason, suppressRules, Scoring.WeightOf(rules, rule.Category), "数字签名异常", new()
                    {
                        ["matchedField"] = "authenticode",
                        ["matchedValue"] = rec.Signature.State,
                        ["winTrustStatus"] = rec.Signature.WinTrustStatus is uint s ? $"0x{s:X8}" : null,
                        ["signer"] = rec.Signature.SignerSubject,
                        ["catalogSigned"] = rec.Signature.CatalogSigned,
                        ["pid"] = rec.Pid,
                        ["imagePath"] = rec.ImagePath
                    }));
                }
            }

            if (reasons.Count > 0)
            {
                skipped.Add(new NotChecked
                {
                    Target = entry.Name,
                    Path = rec.ImagePath,
                    Pid = entry.Pid,
                    Reason = string.Join("；", reasons)
                });
            }

            records.Add(rec);
        });

        result.SelfSkipped = selfSkipped;
        result.Findings.AddRange(findings.OrderByDescending(f => f.Score).ThenBy(f => f.RuleId));
        result.Processes.AddRange(records.OrderBy(r => r.Pid));
        result.NotChecked.AddRange(skipped.OrderBy(n => n.Pid ?? 0));
        return result;
    }

    private static bool Eq(string a, string b) => string.Equals(a, b, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    private static bool IsSuppressed(bool whitelisted, string[]? suppressRules, string ruleId)
    {
        if (!whitelisted) return false;
        if (suppressRules is null || suppressRules.Length == 0) return true;
        return suppressRules.Any(r => string.Equals(r, ruleId, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
    }

    private static Finding Build(
        Rule rule,
        ProcessRecord rec,
        string target,
        bool whitelisted,
        string? whitelistReason,
        string[]? suppressRules,
        double categoryWeight,
        string fallbackDescription,
        Dictionary<string, object?> evidence)
    {
        bool suppressed = IsSuppressed(whitelisted, suppressRules, rule.Id);
        double score = Scoring.ComputeScore(rule.Severity, rule.Confidence, categoryWeight);
        return new Finding
        {
            RuleId = rule.Id,
            RuleType = rule.Type,
            Target = target,
            TargetPath = rec.ImagePath,
            Pid = rec.Pid,
            Severity = rule.Severity,
            Confidence = rule.Confidence,
            Score = score,
            RiskLevel = Scoring.LevelOf(score),
            Description = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Description) ? fallbackDescription : rule.Description!,
            Advice = rule.Advice,
            Evidence = evidence,
            Category = rule.Category,
            Suppressed = suppressed,
            SuppressReason = suppressed ? whitelistReason : null,
            SuppressedBy = suppressed ? "whitelist" : null
        };
    }

    private static (bool Matched, string? Reason, string[]? SuppressRules) EvaluateWhitelist(RuleSet rules, ProcessRecord rec, string? sha256)
    {
        foreach (var entry in rules.Whitelist.Processes)
        {
            if (RuleLoader.IsExpired(entry)) continue;

            bool nameHit = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.Pattern) &&
                           (entry.Pattern!.Contains('\\') || entry.Pattern.Contains('/')
                               ? PatternMatcher.IsMatch(rec.ImagePath, entry.Pattern, entry.Match)
                               : PatternMatcher.IsMatch(rec.Name, entry.Pattern, entry.Match));

            if (!nameHit) continue;
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.Hash) &&
                !string.Equals(entry.Hash, sha256, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;

            return (true, entry.Reason ?? $"进程白名单: {entry.Pattern}", entry.SuppressRules);
        }

        foreach (var entry in rules.Whitelist.Paths)
        {
            if (RuleLoader.IsExpired(entry)) continue;
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rec.ImagePath)) continue;
            if (!PatternMatcher.IsMatch(rec.ImagePath, entry.Pattern, entry.Match)) continue;
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.Hash) &&
                !string.Equals(entry.Hash, sha256, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;

            return (true, entry.Reason ?? $"路径白名单: {entry.Pattern}", entry.SuppressRules);
        }

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rec.Signature.SignerSubject))
        {
            foreach (var entry in rules.Whitelist.Signers)
            {
                if (RuleLoader.IsExpired(entry)) continue;
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.SubjectContains)) continue;
                if (rec.Signature.SignerSubject!.Contains(entry.SubjectContains!, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    return (true, entry.Reason ?? $"签名白名单: {entry.SubjectContains}", entry.SuppressRules);
                }
            }
        }

        return (false, null, null);
    }

    private static bool SignatureMatches(string? pattern, string state)
    {
        return (pattern ?? "").Trim().ToLowerInvariant() switch
        {
            SignaturePatterns.Unsigned => state == SignatureStates.Unsigned,
            SignaturePatterns.BadDigest => state == SignatureStates.BadDigest,
            SignaturePatterns.UntrustedRoot => state is SignatureStates.UntrustedRoot or SignatureStates.InvalidChain,
            SignaturePatterns.Expired => state == SignatureStates.Expired,
            SignaturePatterns.Revoked => state == SignatureStates.Revoked,
            SignaturePatterns.CatalogOnly => state == SignatureStates.CatalogValid,
            SignaturePatterns.AnyInvalid => state is SignatureStates.Unsigned
                or SignatureStates.BadDigest
                or SignatureStates.UntrustedRoot
                or SignatureStates.InvalidChain
                or SignatureStates.Expired
                or SignatureStates.Revoked
                or SignatureStates.Distrusted
                or SignatureStates.Error,
            _ => false
        };
    }

    private static List<ProcEntry> EnumerateProcessEntries(CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var list = new List<ProcEntry>();
        IntPtr snapshot = NativeMethods.CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(NativeMethods.TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0);
        if (snapshot == IntPtr.Zero || snapshot == NativeMethods.InvalidHandleValue)
        {
            throw new Win32Exception(Marshal.GetLastWin32Error(), "CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS) 失败");
        }

        try
        {
            var entry = new NativeMethods.PROCESSENTRY32W { dwSize = (uint)Marshal.SizeOf<NativeMethods.PROCESSENTRY32W>() };
            if (!NativeMethods.Process32First(snapshot, ref entry))
            {
                return list;
            }

            do
            {
                ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
                list.Add(new ProcEntry(
                    (int)entry.th32ProcessID,
                    (int)entry.th32ParentProcessID,
                    (int)entry.cntThreads,
                    entry.szExeFile ?? ""));
            }
            while (NativeMethods.Process32Next(snapshot, ref entry));
        }
        finally
        {
            NativeMethods.CloseHandle(snapshot);
        }

        return list;
    }

    private static List<ModuleInfo>? TryGetModules(int pid, out string? error)
    {
        error = null;
        var list = new List<ModuleInfo>();

        IntPtr snapshot = NativeMethods.CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(
            NativeMethods.TH32CS_SNAPMODULE | NativeMethods.TH32CS_SNAPMODULE32, (uint)pid);

        if (snapshot == IntPtr.Zero || snapshot == NativeMethods.InvalidHandleValue)
        {
            int err = Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
            error = $"Win32={err} {new Win32Exception(err).Message}";
            return null;
        }

        try
        {
            var me = new NativeMethods.MODULEENTRY32W { dwSize = (uint)Marshal.SizeOf<NativeMethods.MODULEENTRY32W>() };
            if (!NativeMethods.Module32First(snapshot, ref me))
            {
                int err = Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
                if (err != 0)
                {
                    error = $"Win32={err} {new Win32Exception(err).Message}";
                    return null;
                }
                return list;
            }

            do
            {
                list.Add(new ModuleInfo
                {
                    Name = me.szModule ?? "",
                    Path = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(me.szExePath) ? null : me.szExePath,
                    BaseAddress = me.modBaseAddr.ToInt64(),
                    Size = me.modBaseSize
                });
            }
            while (NativeMethods.Module32Next(snapshot, ref me));
        }
        finally
        {
            NativeMethods.CloseHandle(snapshot);
        }

        return list;
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Scanning/TelemetryCorrelator.cs`:

```cs
using System.Text.Json;
using EagleEye.Core;
using EagleEye.Models;

namespace EagleEye.Scanning;

/// <summary>
/// 游戏侧遥测与本机线索的联合评估。
/// 遥测来自游戏/运营侧导出的**本地文件**（本工具不联网，不发起任何请求）。
///
/// 联合口径：
///   本机信息 + 遥测信息        → 信息
///   本机信息 + 遥测可疑/高危    → 可疑（本地无痕迹，指向双机/硬件链路）
///   本机可疑 + 遥测可疑/高危    → 高度可疑（建议人工复核 + 取证）
///   本机可疑 + 遥测信息        → 可疑（有痕迹但无行为异常）
/// 任何情况下都只给"复核优先级"，不给"作弊判定"。
/// </summary>
public static class TelemetryCorrelator
{
    private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOptions = new()
    {
        PropertyNameCaseInsensitive = true,
        ReadCommentHandling = JsonCommentHandling.Skip,
        AllowTrailingCommas = true,
        NumberHandling = System.Text.Json.Serialization.JsonNumberHandling.AllowReadingFromString
    };

    public static TelemetryReport Load(string path, TelemetrySection section, string localLevel)
    {
        var report = new TelemetryReport
        {
            Enabled = section.Enabled,
            SourcePath = Path.GetFullPath(path),
            LocalLevel = localLevel
        };

        TelemetryFile? file;
        try
        {
            string json = File.ReadAllText(path);
            file = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<TelemetryFile>(json, JsonOptions);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            report.Error = $"遥测文件读取/解析失败: {ex.Message}";
            report.JointLevel = localLevel;
            return report;
        }

        if (file is null)
        {
            report.Error = "遥测文件解析结果为空";
            report.JointLevel = localLevel;
            return report;
        }

        report.Loaded = true;
        report.SchemaVersion = file.SchemaVersion;
        report.ExportedUtc = file.ExportedUtc;
        report.Sessions = file.Sessions.Count;

        if (file.Sessions.Count == 0)
        {
            report.Error = "遥测文件里没有 sessions";
            report.JointLevel = localLevel;
            return report;
        }

        foreach (var session in file.Sessions)
        {
            report.Assessments.Add(AssessSession(session, section));
        }

        // 取最"热"的一场作为联合依据
        var hottest = report.Assessments
            .OrderByDescending(a => Scoring.Rank(a.TelemetryLevel))
            .ThenByDescending(a => a.TelemetryScore)
            .First();

        var (jointLevel, reasons) = Combine(localLevel, hottest, section);
        report.JointLevel = jointLevel;
        report.JointScore = hottest.TelemetryScore;
        report.JointReasons = reasons;

        return report;
    }

    private static SessionAssessment AssessSession(TelemetrySession session, TelemetrySection section)
    {
        var assessment = new SessionAssessment
        {
            SessionId = session.SessionId,
            MatchId = session.MatchId,
            PlayerId = session.PlayerId,
            AccountName = session.AccountName,
            StartedUtc = session.StartedUtc,
            DurationSeconds = session.DurationSeconds
        };

        foreach (var rule in section.Signals.Where(r => r.Enabled))
        {
            if (!session.Metrics.TryGetValue(rule.Metric, out double value)) continue;

            bool hit = rule.Comparator.Equals("lte", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                ? value <= rule.Threshold
                : value >= rule.Threshold;

            if (!hit) continue;

            bool baselineKnown = section.Baseline.TryGetValue(rule.Metric, out double baseline);
            double score = Scoring.ComputeScore(rule.Severity, rule.Confidence);

            assessment.Signals.Add(new TelemetrySignal
            {
                RuleId = rule.Id,
                Metric = rule.Metric,
                Description = rule.Description ?? $"{rule.Metric} {rule.Comparator} {rule.Threshold}",
                Value = value,
                Threshold = rule.Threshold,
                Baseline = baselineKnown ? baseline : 0,
                BaselineKnown = baselineKnown,
                Severity = rule.Severity,
                Confidence = rule.Confidence,
                Score = score,
                RiskLevel = Scoring.LevelOf(score),
                Advice = rule.Advice
            });
        }

        assessment.Signals.Sort((a, b) => b.Score.CompareTo(a.Score));
        assessment.TelemetryScore = Math.Round(Math.Min(100.0, assessment.Signals.Sum(s => s.Score)), 2);
        assessment.TelemetryLevel = Scoring.LevelOf(assessment.TelemetryScore);

        foreach (var signal in assessment.Signals)
        {
            string baselineText = signal.BaselineKnown
                ? $"，人群基线 {signal.Baseline}（本次 {signal.Value}）"
                : string.Empty;
            assessment.Reasons.Add($"{signal.RuleId}: {signal.Metric}={signal.Value}（阈值 {signal.Threshold}{baselineText}）");
        }

        return assessment;
    }

    private static (string Level, List<string> Reasons) Combine(string localLevel, SessionAssessment telemetry, TelemetrySection section)
    {
        var reasons = new List<string>();
        int localRank = Scoring.Rank(localLevel);
        int telemetryRank = Scoring.Rank(telemetry.TelemetryLevel);

        string level;
        if (telemetryRank == 0 && localRank == 0)
        {
            level = RiskLevels.Info;
            reasons.Add("本机无线索，遥测无异常 → 无进一步动作");
        }
        else if (telemetryRank >= 1 && localRank == 0)
        {
            level = RiskLevels.Suspicious;
            reasons.Add("遥测出现行为异常，但本机进程/驱动/句柄全部干净 → 高度指向双机链路、外部采集卡或硬件转发");
            reasons.Add("建议：调取该玩家的对局录像与设备指纹，而不是继续在本机找证据");
        }
        else if (telemetryRank >= 1 && localRank >= 1)
        {
            level = Scoring.MaxLevel(section.BothSidesLevel, RiskLevels.Suspicious);
            reasons.Add("遥测行为异常 + 本机存在痕迹 → 两侧证据互相印证，优先复核");
        }
        else
        {
            level = RiskLevels.Suspicious;
            reasons.Add("本机存在痕迹，但遥测未见行为异常 → 可能是误装、残留工具，或尚未在对局中使用");
            reasons.Add("建议：让玩家清理后复测，而不是直接处罚");
        }

        foreach (string r in telemetry.Reasons.Take(5))
        {
            reasons.Add("遥测：" + r);
        }

        return (level, reasons);
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Scanning/VisionScanner.cs`:

```cs
using EagleEye.Core;
using EagleEye.Models;
using EagleEye.Native;

namespace EagleEye.Scanning;

public sealed class VisionScanResult
{
    public bool Enabled { get; set; }
    public List<VisionIndicator> SoftwareCapture { get; } = new();
    public List<VisionIndicator> HardwareIndicators { get; } = new();
    public VisionBehavioralHint BehavioralHint { get; set; } = new();
    public List<NotChecked> NotChecked { get; } = new();
    public List<string> Notes { get; } = new();
    public int IndependentLayers { get; set; }
    public string HighestLevel { get; set; } = RiskLevels.Info;
}

/// <summary>
/// 视觉外挂线索扫描（用户态、只读）。
///
/// 三层：
///   capture  —— 进程同时具备「可见窗口」+「加载了采集/推理模块」；
///   hardware —— 复用 DmaScanner 已枚举的设备列表，按视觉类规则再匹配一次；
///   behavior —— 可选，GetCursorPos 轮询的聚合统计（信息级）。
///
/// 明确不做：不读进程内存、不注入 DLL、不 Hook API、不截屏、不判断窗口内容、
///           不关联具体游戏、不新增任何内核组件。
///
/// 判定口径：单层命中最高「信息」，≥2 个独立层才升到「可疑」，且封顶为「可疑」。
/// </summary>
public sealed class VisionScanner
{
    private readonly record struct WindowSummary(int Count, int MaxWidth, int MaxHeight, string? Title);

    public VisionScanResult Scan(
        RuleSet ruleSet,
        ProcessScanResult processes,
        List<DmaDeviceInfo>? devices,
        CancellationToken ct,
        AuditLogger? audit = null,
        bool behaviorOverride = false)
    {
        var section = ruleSet.Vision;
        var result = new VisionScanResult { Enabled = section.Enabled };
        if (!section.Enabled)
        {
            return result;
        }

        audit?.Write("vision.start", new
        {
            capture = section.Capture.Enabled,
            hardware = section.Hardware.Enabled,
            behavior = section.Behavior.Enabled || behaviorOverride,
            note = "只读枚举：窗口 + 模块 + 设备；不注入、不 Hook、不截屏"
        });

        if (section.Capture.Enabled)
        {
            ScanCapture(section, processes, result, ct);
        }

        if (section.Hardware.Enabled)
        {
            ScanHardware(section, devices, result);
        }

        if (section.Behavior.Enabled || behaviorOverride)
        {
            result.BehavioralHint = InputBehaviorSampler.Sample(section.Behavior, ct);
            if (result.BehavioralHint.Collected)
            {
                audit?.Write("vision.behavior", new
                {
                    samples = result.BehavioralHint.SampleCount,
                    straight = result.BehavioralHint.StraightLineRatio,
                    constant = result.BehavioralHint.ConstantVelocityRatio,
                    note = "信息级；GetCursorPos 轮询，无 Hook、无轨迹留存"
                });
            }
        }
        else
        {
            result.BehavioralHint.SkipReason = "未启用（默认关闭；需要时用 --vision-behavior 开启）";
        }

        Aggregate(section, result);
        audit?.Write("vision.end", new
        {
            software = result.SoftwareCapture.Count,
            hardware = result.HardwareIndicators.Count,
            layers = result.IndependentLayers,
            level = result.HighestLevel
        });

        return result;
    }

    // ---------------- 采集端 ----------------
    private static void ScanCapture(VisionSection section, ProcessScanResult processes, VisionScanResult result, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var windows = WindowApi.EnumerateVisibleWindows();
        var windowsByPid = windows
            .GroupBy(w => w.ProcessId)
            .ToDictionary(
                g => g.Key,
                g => new WindowSummary(g.Count(), g.Max(w => w.Width), g.Max(w => w.Height),
                                       g.Select(w => w.Title).FirstOrDefault(t => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(t))));

        var strongRules = section.Capture.ModuleRules.Where(r => r.Enabled && IsStrong(r)).ToList();
        var contextRules = section.Capture.ModuleRules.Where(r => r.Enabled && !IsStrong(r)).ToList();

        foreach (var process in processes.Processes)
        {
            ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();

            if (!windowsByPid.TryGetValue(process.Pid, out var window) || window.Count == 0)
            {
                continue;
            }

            if (process.Modules.Count == 0)
            {
                continue;
            }

            var matchedStrong = new List<VisionRule>();
            var matchedContext = new List<VisionRule>();
            var matchedModules = new List<string>();

            foreach (var rule in section.Capture.ModuleRules.Where(r => r.Enabled))
            {
                var hits = process.Modules
                    .Where(m => PatternMatcher.IsMatch(m.Name, rule.Pattern, rule.Match))
                    .Select(m => m.Name)
                    .Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    .ToList();

                if (hits.Count == 0) continue;

                matchedModules.AddRange(hits);
                if (IsStrong(rule)) matchedStrong.Add(rule);
                else matchedContext.Add(rule);
            }

            // 只有上下文模块（gdi32/dxgi 这类）不足以成条
            if (matchedStrong.Count == 0) continue;

            var (suppressed, reason) = CheckProcessWhitelist(section, process);

            double confidence = matchedStrong.Max(r => r.Confidence);
            confidence += Math.Min(0.15, matchedContext.Count * 0.05);
            if (process.Signature.State is SignatureStates.Unsigned or SignatureStates.BadDigest)
            {
                confidence += 0.05;
            }

            confidence = Math.Round(Math.Clamp(confidence, 0, 0.85), 2);
            int severity = Math.Max(1, matchedStrong.Max(r => r.Severity));
            double score = Scoring.ComputeScore(severity, confidence);

            result.SoftwareCapture.Add(new VisionIndicator
            {
                Id = matchedStrong[0].Id,
                Layer = "capture",
                Category = matchedStrong[0].Category,
                Target = process.Name,
                Pid = process.Pid,
                Path = process.ImagePath,
                SignatureState = process.Signature.State,
                CommandLineSummary = Summarize(process.CommandLine),
                MatchedModules = matchedModules.Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToList(),
                Severity = severity,
                Confidence = confidence,
                Score = score,
                RiskLevel = RiskLevels.Info,
                Description = matchedStrong[0].Description ?? "进程同时拥有可见窗口并加载了采集/推理相关模块",
                Advice = matchedStrong[0].Advice ?? "弱信号：需结合游戏侧遥测与人工复核，本地不作为判定依据",
                Suppressed = suppressed,
                SuppressReason = suppressed ? reason : null,
                Evidence = new Dictionary<string, object?>
                {
                    ["pid"] = process.Pid,
                    ["imagePath"] = process.ImagePath,
                    ["signatureState"] = process.Signature.State,
                    ["signer"] = process.Signature.SignerSubject,
                    ["commandLine"] = Summarize(process.CommandLine),
                    ["visibleWindows"] = window.Count,
                    ["windowTitle"] = window.Title,
                    ["largestWindow"] = $"{window.MaxWidth}x{window.MaxHeight}",
                    ["matchedModules"] = matchedModules.Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToArray(),
                    ["strongRules"] = matchedStrong.Select(r => r.Id).ToArray(),
                    ["contextRules"] = matchedContext.Select(r => r.Id).ToArray(),
                    ["memoryRead"] = false,
                    ["screenshotTaken"] = false
                }
            });
        }
    }

    // ---------------- 硬件 ----------------
    private void ScanHardware(VisionSection section, List<DmaDeviceInfo>? devices, VisionScanResult result)
    {
        if (devices is null || devices.Count == 0)
        {
            result.Notes.Add("硬件枚举结果为空（可能未启用 DMA 枚举或本机无 PCI 设备）");
            return;
        }

        foreach (var device in devices)
        {
            foreach (var rule in section.Hardware.Rules.Where(r => r.Enabled))
            {
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.VendorId) && !HexEquals(rule.VendorId, device.VendorId)) continue;
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.DeviceId) && !HexEquals(rule.DeviceId, device.DeviceId)) continue;
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.DeviceClass) &&
                    (device.DeviceClass is null || !device.DeviceClass.Contains(rule.DeviceClass!, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))) continue;
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rule.Service) &&
                    (device.Service is null || !device.Service.Contains(rule.Service!, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))) continue;

                var (suppressed, reason) = CheckDeviceWhitelist(section, device);
                double confidence = Math.Round(Math.Clamp(rule.Confidence, 0, 0.7), 2);
                double score = Scoring.ComputeScore(rule.Severity, confidence);

                result.HardwareIndicators.Add(new VisionIndicator
                {
                    Id = rule.Id,
                    Layer = "hardware",
                    Category = rule.Category,
                    Target = device.Name,
                    Path = device.InstanceId,
                    SignatureState = null,
                    Severity = rule.Severity,
                    Confidence = confidence,
                    Score = score,
                    RiskLevel = RiskLevels.Info,
                    Description = rule.Description ?? "设备特征命中外设类规则",
                    Advice = rule.Advice ?? "需结合使用场景人工确认（直播/双机采集/硬件宏都可能同款设备）",
                    Suppressed = suppressed,
                    SuppressReason = suppressed ? reason : null,
                    Evidence = new Dictionary<string, object?>
                    {
                        ["instanceId"] = device.InstanceId,
                        ["vendorId"] = device.VendorId,
                        ["deviceId"] = device.DeviceId,
                        ["deviceClass"] = device.DeviceClass,
                        ["service"] = device.Service,
                        ["location"] = device.Location,
                        ["manufacturer"] = device.Manufacturer,
                        ["matchedRuleId"] = rule.Id,
                        ["category"] = rule.Category
                    }
                });
            }
        }
    }

    // ---------------- 聚合 ----------------
    /// <summary>重算层级与等级（追加 overlay 线索后调用）。</summary>
    public static void Aggregate(VisionSection section, VisionScanResult result)
    {
        int layers = 0;
        if (result.SoftwareCapture.Any(i => !i.Suppressed)) layers++;
        if (result.HardwareIndicators.Any(i => !i.Suppressed)) layers++;
        if (result.BehavioralHint.Collected && result.BehavioralHint.Confidence >= 0.15) layers++;

        result.IndependentLayers = layers;

        int required = Math.Clamp(section.Aggregation.RequireIndependentLayers, 1, 3);
        string level = layers >= required ? RiskLevels.Suspicious : RiskLevels.Info;

        // 封顶：无论多少层，本地最高只给「可疑」
        int cap = Math.Min(Scoring.Rank(section.Aggregation.MaxLevel), Scoring.Rank(RiskLevels.Suspicious));
        if (Scoring.Rank(level) > cap)
        {
            level = RiskLevels.Suspicious;
        }

        result.HighestLevel = level;

        foreach (var indicator in result.SoftwareCapture.Concat(result.HardwareIndicators))
        {
            indicator.RiskLevel = level;
        }

        if (result.SoftwareCapture.Count == 0 && result.HardwareIndicators.Count == 0 && !result.BehavioralHint.Collected)
        {
            result.Notes.Add("未发现视觉外挂相关线索（注意：双机链路在本机不会有任何痕迹）");
        }

        if (result.SoftwareCapture.Count > 0)
        {
            result.Notes.Add("软件采集线索基于「可见窗口 + 已加载模块」，无法区分正常直播/录屏与作弊采集");
        }

        if (result.HardwareIndicators.Count > 0)
        {
            result.Notes.Add("硬件线索只能说明设备存在，无法证明其被用于作弊（采集卡可用于直播，HID 开发板可用于自动化测试）");
        }
    }

    private static bool IsStrong(VisionRule rule)
        => !string.Equals(rule.Tier, "context", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    private static (bool, string?) CheckProcessWhitelist(VisionSection section, ProcessRecord process)
    {
        foreach (var entry in section.Whitelist)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.ProcessPattern)) continue;
            if (entry.Expires is not null && DateTime.TryParse(entry.Expires, out DateTime expires) && expires.Date < DateTime.Today) continue;
            if (!PatternMatcher.IsMatch(process.Name, entry.ProcessPattern, entry.Match)) continue;

            return (true, entry.Reason ?? $"视觉白名单: {entry.ProcessPattern}");
        }

        return (false, null);
    }

    private static (bool, string?) CheckDeviceWhitelist(VisionSection section, DmaDeviceInfo device)
    {
        foreach (var entry in section.Whitelist)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.VendorId)) continue;
            if (!HexEquals(entry.VendorId, device.VendorId)) continue;
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.DeviceId) && !HexEquals(entry.DeviceId, device.DeviceId)) continue;

            return (true, entry.Reason ?? $"视觉白名单: VID {entry.VendorId}");
        }

        return (false, null);
    }

    private static bool HexEquals(string? a, string? b)
        => string.Equals(DmaScanner.NormalizeHex(a), DmaScanner.NormalizeHex(b), StringComparison.Ordinal);

    private static string? Summarize(string? commandLine)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(commandLine)) return null;
        string trimmed = commandLine.Trim();
        return trimmed.Length <= 200 ? trimmed : trimmed[..200] + "…";
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/SelfTest.cs`:

```cs
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Security.Principal;
using EagleEye.Models;
using EagleEye.Native;
using EagleEye.Rules;
using EagleEye.Scanning;

namespace EagleEye;

/// <summary>
/// 环境自检：现场部署前先跑这个，确认这台机器能不能查。
/// 只做只读探测，不写任何配置。
/// </summary>
public static class SelfTest
{
    private sealed record Item(string Name, bool Ok, string Detail, bool Critical);

    public static int Run(RuleLoadResult load, bool quiet)
    {
        var items = new List<Item>();
        var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();

        // 1. 管理员权限
        bool elevated = IsElevated();
        items.Add(new Item("管理员权限", elevated,
            elevated ? "已提权" : "未提权 —— 游戏/反作弊等受保护进程读不到，覆盖不完整", true));

        // 2. 规则
        bool rulesOk = load.Success && load.RuleSet.Rules.Count > 0;
        items.Add(new Item("规则加载", rulesOk,
            rulesOk
                ? $"{load.RuleSet.Meta.Name} v{load.RuleSet.Meta.Version}，通用 {load.RuleSet.Rules.Count} 条 / DMA {load.RuleSet.Dma.Rules.Count} 条"
                : $"失败：{string.Join("; ", load.Errors.Take(3))}", true));

        // 3. 进程枚举
        int procCount = 0;
        try { procCount = Process.GetProcesses().Length; } catch { }
        items.Add(new Item("进程枚举", procCount > 0, $"{procCount} 个进程", true));

        // 4. 签名校验
        string selfExe = Environment.ProcessPath ?? "";
        bool sigOk = false;
        string sigDetail = "无法定位自身 exe";
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(selfExe) && File.Exists(selfExe))
        {
            var sig = new SignatureChecker(false).Check(selfExe);
            sigOk = sig.State is SignatureStates.Valid or SignatureStates.CatalogValid or SignatureStates.Unsigned;
            sigDetail = $"自身 exe 验签返回 {sig.State}（能返回结果即说明 wintrust 可用）";
        }
        items.Add(new Item("签名校验", sigOk, sigDetail, true));

        // 5. 句柄枚举（需要管理员）
        var handleSw = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        var refs = HandleInterop.FindProcessHandles(out int totalHandles, out int highAccess, out string? handleError);
        handleSw.Stop();
        bool handleOk = handleError is null;
        items.Add(new Item("句柄枚举", handleOk,
            handleOk
                ? $"句柄表 {totalHandles} 个，高权限 {highAccess} 个，可解析配对 {refs.Count} 个（{handleSw.ElapsedMilliseconds} ms）"
                : $"失败：{handleError}（通常是没有管理员权限）", true));

        // 6. 驱动枚举
        var drivers = DriverInterop.Enumerate(out string? driverError);
        items.Add(new Item("驱动枚举", driverError is null, driverError ?? $"{drivers.Count} 个已加载驱动", true));

        // 7. 设备枚举
        var devices = SetupApiInterop.EnumeratePresentDevices(out string? devError);
        int pci = devices.Count(d => d.InstanceId.StartsWith("PCI\\", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
        items.Add(new Item("设备枚举", devError is null, devError ?? $"设备 {devices.Count} 个，其中 PCI/PCIe {pci} 个", false));

        // 8. 目标游戏是否在运行
        var patterns = load.RuleSet.Deep.Injection.TargetProcessPatterns;
        if (patterns.Count == 0) patterns = new List<string> { "*DeltaForce*" };

        var targets = new List<string>();
        try
        {
            foreach (var p in Process.GetProcesses())
            {
                try
                {
                    if (patterns.Any(x => Core.PatternMatcher.IsMatch(p.ProcessName, x, null)))
                    {
                        targets.Add($"{p.ProcessName}(PID {p.Id})");
                    }
                }
                catch { }
            }
        }
        catch { }

        items.Add(new Item("目标游戏进程", targets.Count > 0,
            targets.Count > 0 ? string.Join(", ", targets) : "未检测到（先启动游戏再跑自检）", false));

        // 9. 输出目录可写
        bool canWrite = true;
        try
        {
            string probe = Path.Combine(Directory.GetCurrentDirectory(), $".write-test-{Guid.NewGuid():N}.tmp");
            File.WriteAllText(probe, "x");
            File.Delete(probe);
        }
        catch { canWrite = false; }
        items.Add(new Item("输出目录可写", canWrite, Directory.GetCurrentDirectory(), true));

        sw.Stop();

        // 输出
        if (!quiet)
        {
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine("================ 环境自检 ================");
            foreach (var item in items)
            {
                string mark = item.Ok ? "[PASS]" : "[FAIL]";
                Console.WriteLine($"{mark} {item.Name,-12} {item.Detail}");
            }

            Console.WriteLine();
            var critical = items.Where(i => !i.Ok && i.Critical).ToList();
            if (critical.Count > 0)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"结论：{critical.Count} 项关键检查未通过 —— 建议先解决再扫描：");
                foreach (var c in critical)
                {
                    Console.WriteLine($"  - {c.Name}: {c.Detail}");
                }
            }
            else
            {
                Console.WriteLine("结论：环境就绪，可以扫描。");
            }

            Console.WriteLine($"耗时 {sw.ElapsedMilliseconds} ms");
        }

        return items.Any(i => !i.Ok && i.Critical) ? 1 : 0;
    }

    private static bool IsElevated()
    {
        try
        {
            using WindowsIdentity id = WindowsIdentity.GetCurrent();
            return new WindowsPrincipal(id).IsInRole(WindowsBuiltInRole.Administrator);
        }
        catch { return false; }
    }
}
```

`src/EagleEye/Wizard.cs`:

```cs
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Security.Principal;
using EagleEye.Rules;

namespace EagleEye;

/// <summary>
/// 双击向导：不带参数运行时进入。
/// 面向不熟悉命令行的使用者：自检 → 必要时提权 → 扫描 → 打开报告 → 停留等按键。
/// </summary>
public static class Wizard
{
    public static int Run()
    {
        Console.Title = "Eagle Eye - 本地外挂痕迹扫描";

        Console.WriteLine();
        Console.WriteLine("  ███████╗ █████╗  ██████╗ ██╗     ███████╗    ███████╗██╗   ██╗███████╗");
        Console.WriteLine("  ██╔════╝██╔══██╗██╔════╝ ██║     ██╔════╝    ██╔════╝╚██╗ ██╔╝██╔════╝");
        Console.WriteLine("  █████╗  ███████║██║  ███╗██║     █████╗      █████╗   ╚████╔╝ █████╗  ");
        Console.WriteLine("  ██╔══╝  ██╔══██║██║   ██║██║     ██╔══╝      ██╔══╝    ╚██╔╝  ██╔══╝  ");
        Console.WriteLine("  ███████╗██║  ██║╚██████╔╝███████╗███████╗    ███████╗   ██║   ███████╗");
        Console.WriteLine("  ╚══════╝╚═╝  ╚═╝ ╚═════╝ ╚══════╝╚══════╝    ╚══════╝   ╚═╝   ╚══════╝");
        Console.WriteLine();
        Console.WriteLine("  本地反外挂只读查询工具 · 只读枚举，不注入、不读内存、不 Hook、不联网");
        Console.WriteLine();

        // 1. 权限
        if (!IsElevated())
        {
            Console.WriteLine("  [提示] 当前不是管理员权限。");
            Console.WriteLine("         游戏等受保护进程的模块、句柄读不到，扫描会不完整。");
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.Write("  是否以管理员身份重新运行？(Y/n) ");
            string? answer = Console.ReadLine();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(answer) || answer.Trim().ToLowerInvariant().StartsWith("y"))
            {
                return RelaunchElevated();
            }

            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine("  继续以普通权限扫描（结果会标注「覆盖不完整」）...");
        }

        // 2. 规则
        string rulesDir = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "rules");
        var load = Directory.Exists(rulesDir)
            ? RuleLoader.LoadDirectory(rulesDir)
            : RuleLoader.Load(Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "rules", "rules.json"));

        if (!load.Success)
        {
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine("  [错误] 规则加载失败：");
            foreach (string err in load.Errors.Take(5))
            {
                Console.WriteLine("         " + err);
            }
            Pause();
            return 1;
        }

        Console.WriteLine($"  规则: {load.RuleSet.Meta.Name} v{load.RuleSet.Meta.Version}，" +
                          $"{load.RuleSet.Rules.Count + load.RuleSet.Dma.Rules.Count} 条");
        Console.WriteLine();

        // 3. 扫描
        Console.WriteLine("  开始扫描（约 10-20 秒，请不要关闭窗口）...");
        Console.WriteLine();

        int exitCode;
        try
        {
            exitCode = Program.RunScanFromWizard(rulesDir);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine("  [错误] 扫描中断: " + ex.Message);
            Pause();
            return 1;
        }

        // 4. 打开报告
        string reportDir = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "out", "reports");
        string? html = Directory.Exists(reportDir)
            ? Directory.GetFiles(reportDir, "*.html").OrderByDescending(f => f).FirstOrDefault()
            : null;

        Console.WriteLine();
        if (html is not null)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("  报告已生成：");
            Console.WriteLine("    " + html);
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.Write("  是否立即打开报告？(Y/n) ");
            string? open = Console.ReadLine();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(open) || open.Trim().ToLowerInvariant().StartsWith("y"))
            {
                try { Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo(html) { UseShellExecute = true }); }
                catch { Console.WriteLine("  打开失败，请手动双击上面的路径。"); }
            }
        }
        else
        {
            Console.WriteLine("  报告目录: " + reportDir);
        }

        Console.WriteLine();
        Console.WriteLine("  判定说明：本地最高只给「高度可疑」，需要人工复核。");
        Console.WriteLine("  双机 / 外部采集 / 硬件转发在本机不会留下痕迹，这类只能靠游戏侧遥测。");
        Console.WriteLine();

        Pause();
        return exitCode;
    }

    private static int RelaunchElevated()
    {
        string? exe = Environment.ProcessPath;
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(exe))
        {
            Console.WriteLine("  无法定位自身路径，请手动以管理员身份运行。");
            Pause();
            return 1;
        }

        try
        {
            Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo(exe)
            {
                UseShellExecute = true,
                Verb = "runas",
                WorkingDirectory = AppContext.BaseDirectory
            });
            Console.WriteLine("  已请求管理员权限，请在弹出的窗口中继续。");
            Thread.Sleep(800);
            return 0;
        }
        catch
        {
            Console.WriteLine("  提权被取消，改为普通权限扫描。");
            return Run();
        }
    }

    private static void Pause()
    {
        Console.WriteLine("  按 Enter 键关闭窗口...");
        try { Console.ReadLine(); } catch { }
    }

    private static bool IsElevated()
    {
        try
        {
            using WindowsIdentity id = WindowsIdentity.GetCurrent();
            return new WindowsPrincipal(id).IsInRole(WindowsBuiltInRole.Administrator);
        }
        catch { return false; }
    }
}
```

`tools/audit-policy.ps1`:

```ps1
#requires -Version 5.1
<#
    audit-policy.ps1 —— 合规红线自检
    逐条验证：代码里不存在注入/内存读写/Hook/驱动隐藏/反反作弊/绕过反作弊/
              伪造签名/提权/关闭 HVCI/修改游戏文件 的实现，且规则与报告满足
              「可解释、可审计、可白名单抑制」。

    实现说明：匹配前会剥离 C# 与 C 的注释，避免注释里写"绝不使用 XXX"造成误报。

    退出码：0 = 全部通过；1 = 存在违规
#>

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$root = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot
$srcDir = Join-Path $root 'src\EagleEye'
$drvDir = Join-Path $root 'native\pciecfg'
$rulesFile = Join-Path $root 'rules\rules.json'

$violations = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
$checks = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]

function Add-Check($name, $ok, $detail) {
    $checks.Add([pscustomobject]@{ 检查项 = $name; 结果 = $(if ($ok) { 'PASS' } else { 'FAIL' }); 说明 = $detail })
    if (-not $ok) { $violations.Add("$name => $detail") }
}

function Get-CodeOnly([string]$path) {
    $text = Get-Content -LiteralPath $path -Raw -Encoding UTF8
    $code = [regex]::Replace($text, '/\*[\s\S]*?\*/', '')
    $code = [regex]::Replace($code, '//[^\r\n]*', '')
    return $code
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. C# 代码红线（Kernel 目录是授权的一次性驱动装载，单独评估）
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
$bannedCs = [ordered]@{
    'WriteProcessMemory'  = '进程内存写入'
    'ReadProcessMemory'   = '进程内存读取'
    'VirtualAllocEx'      = '远程内存分配'
    'VirtualProtectEx'    = '远程内存属性修改'
    'CreateRemoteThread'  = '远程线程注入'
    'NtCreateThreadEx'    = '远程线程创建'
    'RtlCreateUserThread' = '远程线程创建'
    'SetWindowsHookEx'    = 'API Hook'
    'NtMapViewOfSection'  = '跨进程映射'
    'QueueUserAPC'        = 'APC 注入'
    'SetWinEventHook'     = 'WinEvent Hook'
}

$csFiles = Get-ChildItem $srcDir -Recurse -Filter *.cs | Where-Object { $_.FullName -notmatch '\\(bin|obj|Kernel)\\' }
$bannedHits = @()
foreach ($f in $csFiles) {
    $code = Get-CodeOnly $f.FullName
    foreach ($name in $bannedCs.Keys) {
        if ($code -match [regex]::Escape($name)) { $bannedHits += "$name($($bannedCs[$name])) in $($f.Name)" }
    }
}
Add-Check 'C# 无注入/内存读写/Hook 实现' ($bannedHits.Count -eq 0) $(if ($bannedHits.Count -eq 0) { "扫描 $($csFiles.Count) 个文件（已剥离注释），0 处命中" } else { $bannedHits -join '; ' })

$bannedThemes = 'BypassAntiCheat|DisableVanguard|BattlEyeBypass|EACBypass|SeDebugPrivilege|AdjustTokenPrivileges|bcdedit|testsigning'
$themeHits = @()
foreach ($f in $csFiles) {
    $code = Get-CodeOnly $f.FullName
    if ($code -match $bannedThemes) { $themeHits += $f.Name }
}
Add-Check 'C# 无反反作弊/绕过/提权/关 HVCI' ($themeHits.Count -eq 0) $(if ($themeHits.Count -eq 0) { '0 处命中' } else { $themeHits -join ', ' })

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. 驱动源码红线
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
$drvFile = Join-Path $drvDir 'PcieCfg.c'
$drvCode = Get-CodeOnly $drvFile

$bannedDrv = [ordered]@{
    'IRP_MN_WRITE_CONFIG'             = '写 PCIe 配置空间'
    'MmCopyVirtualMemory'             = '跨进程内存读写'
    'MmMapIoSpace'                    = '物理内存映射'
    'MmGetPhysicalAddress'            = '物理地址获取'
    'PsLookupProcessByProcessId'      = '进程对象查询'
    'KeStackAttachProcess'            = '进程上下文附加'
    'ObRegisterCallbacks'             = '句柄回调拦截'
    'ZwCreateFile'                    = '文件操作'
    'ZwOpenProcess'                   = '进程句柄操作'
    'PsSetCreateProcessNotifyRoutine' = '进程通知回调'
}
$drvHits = @()
foreach ($name in $bannedDrv.Keys) {
    if ($drvCode -match [regex]::Escape($name)) { $drvHits += "$name($($bannedDrv[$name]))" }
}
Add-Check '驱动无写配置空间/内存读写/回调/文件操作' ($drvHits.Count -eq 0) $(if ($drvHits.Count -eq 0) { '仅使用 IRP_MN_READ_CONFIG' } else { $drvHits -join '; ' })

Add-Check '驱动只发 READ_CONFIG' ($drvCode -match 'IRP_MN_READ_CONFIG') '代码中存在 IRP_MN_READ_CONFIG 的 IRP 构造'

$infText = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $drvDir 'PcieCfg.inf') -Raw -Encoding UTF8
Add-Check '驱动服务为按需启动（StartType=3）' ($infText -match 'StartType=3') 'StartType=3 表示 SERVICE_DEMAND_START，不会自启'

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. 驱动装载代码：只允许在 Kernel 目录出现
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
$createServiceOutside = @()
foreach ($f in $csFiles) {
    if ((Get-CodeOnly $f.FullName) -match 'CreateService') { $createServiceOutside += $f.Name }
}
Add-Check 'CreateService 仅存在于 Kernel 装载模块' ($createServiceOutside.Count -eq 0) $(if ($createServiceOutside.Count -eq 0) { '其他模块不含服务创建代码' } else { $createServiceOutside -join ', ' })

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. 规则可解释性
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
$rules = Get-Content -LiteralPath $rulesFile -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
$noDesc = @($rules.rules | Where-Object { -not $_.description })
$noDmaDesc = @($rules.dma.rules | Where-Object { -not $_.description })
Add-Check '规则均有 description（可解释）' ($noDesc.Count -eq 0 -and $noDmaDesc.Count -eq 0) "通用 $($rules.rules.Count) 条 / DMA $($rules.dma.rules.Count) 条，缺失 $($noDesc.Count + $noDmaDesc.Count) 条"
$noCat = @($rules.rules | Where-Object { -not $_.category })
Add-Check '规则均有 category（可分类统计）' ($noCat.Count -eq 0) "缺失 $($noCat.Count) 条"
Add-Check '规则集有版本号与更新时间' ($rules.meta.version -and $rules.meta.updated) "version=$($rules.meta.version), updated=$($rules.meta.updated)"

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. 白名单可抑制
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
$suppressSupported = (Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $srcDir 'Models\Rule.cs') -Raw -Encoding UTF8) -match 'SuppressRules'
Add-Check '白名单段存在' ($null -ne $rules.whitelist) "processes=$($rules.whitelist.processes.Count) paths=$($rules.whitelist.paths.Count) files=$($rules.whitelist.files.Count) signers=$($rules.whitelist.signers.Count)"
Add-Check '支持定向抑制 suppressRules' $suppressSupported 'WhitelistEntry.SuppressRules 字段存在'

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. 报告可审计性
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
$latest = Get-ChildItem (Join-Path $root 'out\reports') -Filter '*.json' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
          Where-Object { $_.Name -notmatch '^aggregate' -and $_.DirectoryName -notmatch 'aggregates' } |
          Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1
if ($latest) {
    $report = Get-Content -LiteralPath $latest.FullName -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    $badEvidence = @($report.findings | Where-Object { -not $_.ruleId -or -not $_.evidence })
    $auditOk = ($report.audit -and $report.audit.networkUsed -eq $false)
    Add-Check '报告命中均带 ruleId + evidence' ($badEvidence.Count -eq 0) "检查 $($report.findings.Count) 条，缺证据 $($badEvidence.Count) 条（来源 $($latest.Name)）"
    Add-Check '报告含审计段且无网络访问' $auditOk "processOpen=$($report.audit.processOpenAttempts) networkUsed=$($report.audit.networkUsed)"
} else {
    Add-Check '报告可审计性' $false '未找到任何报告 JSON'
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. 视觉模块（AI Vision）：不装钩子、不注册原始输入、不截屏
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
$visionFiles = Get-ChildItem $srcDir -Recurse -Filter *.cs | Where-Object { $_.Name -match 'Vision|InputBehavior|WindowApi' }
$visionBanned = 'SetWindowsHookEx|RegisterRawInputDevices|BitBlt|CreateCompatibleDC|CopyFromScreen|PrintWindow|GraphicsCaptureItem'
$visionHits = @()
foreach ($f in $visionFiles) {
    if ((Get-CodeOnly $f.FullName) -match $visionBanned) { $visionHits += $f.Name }
}
Add-Check '视觉模块无 Hook/RawInput/截屏 API' ($visionHits.Count -eq 0) $(if ($visionHits.Count -eq 0) { "扫描 $($visionFiles.Count) 个文件，0 处命中（行为统计用 GetCursorPos 轮询）" } else { $visionHits -join ', ' })

$visionAll = @($rules.vision.capture.moduleRules) + @($rules.vision.hardware.rules)
$badVision = @($visionAll | Where-Object { $_.confidence -gt 0.7 -or $_.severity -gt 4 })
Add-Check '视觉规则 confidence<=0.7 / severity<=4' ($badVision.Count -eq 0) "共 $($visionAll.Count) 条，越界 $($badVision.Count) 条"
Add-Check '视觉判定上限不高于「可疑」' ($rules.vision.aggregation.maxLevel -ne '高度可疑') "maxLevel=$($rules.vision.aggregation.maxLevel)"

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# 输出
# ------------------------------------------------------------------

# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. 深度扫描模块：句柄/驱动/overlay/特征码，全部只读
# ------------------------------------------------------------------
$deepFiles = Get-ChildItem $srcDir -Recurse -Filter *.cs |
    Where-Object { $_.Name -match 'Handle|Driver|Overlay|FileSignature|ExtendedScans' }
$deepBanned = 'ReadProcessMemory|WriteProcessMemory|VirtualQueryEx|SetWindowLong|SendMessage|PostMessage|BitBlt|CopyFromScreen|SeDebugPrivilege|AdjustTokenPrivileges|NtSetInformation'
$deepHits = @()
foreach ($f in $deepFiles) {
    if ((Get-CodeOnly $f.FullName) -match $deepBanned) { $deepHits += $f.Name }
}
Add-Check '深度扫描无内存读写/窗口改写/提权' ($deepHits.Count -eq 0) $(if ($deepHits.Count -eq 0) { "扫描 $($deepFiles.Count) 个文件，0 处命中" } else { $deepHits -join ', ' })

$handleIoctl = (Get-CodeOnly (Join-Path $srcDir 'Native\HandleInterop.cs'))
Add-Check '句柄线索仅用 DuplicateHandle 读 PID' (($handleIoctl -match 'DuplicateHandle') -and ($handleIoctl -notmatch 'ReadProcessMemory')) '不读取目标进程内存'
$checks | Format-Table -AutoSize -Wrap

if ($violations.Count -gt 0) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "红线自检未通过：" -ForegroundColor Red
    $violations | ForEach-Object { Write-Host "  - $_" -ForegroundColor Red }
    exit 1
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "红线自检全部通过（$($checks.Count) 项）。" -ForegroundColor Green
exit 0
```

`tools/clone_refs.ps1`:
