```ps1
# 浅克隆开源参考项目到 13_开源参考\github\
$dest = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "..\13_开源参考\github"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dest -Force | Out-Null

$repos = @(
  "hasherezade/pe-sieve",
  "hasherezade/hollows_hunter",
  "forrest-orr/moneta",
  "forrest-orr/artifacts-kit",
  "LordNoteworthy/al-khaser",
  "a0rtega/pafish",
  "winsiderss/systeminformer",
  "ufrisk/MemProcFS",
  "ufrisk/pcileech",
  "erocarrera/pefile",
  "horsicq/Detect-It-Easy",
  "microsoft/Windows-driver-samples",
  "microsoft/krabsetw",
  "jdu2600/Windows10EtwEvents",
  "redcanaryco/atomic-red-team",
  "codestage/Actk"
)

foreach ($r in $repos) {
  $name = $r.Split("/")[1]
  $target = Join-Path $dest $name
  if (Test-Path $target) { Write-Host "[skip] $name"; continue }
  Write-Host "[clone] $r"
  git clone --depth 1 "https://github.com/$r.git" $target 2>&1 | Out-String | Write-Host
}
Write-Host "完成，路径: $dest"
```

`tools/collect-game-info.ps1`:

```ps1
<#
.SYNOPSIS
    collect-game-info.ps1 —— 在跑着游戏的机器上采集真实基线

.DESCRIPTION
    网上的外挂名单既不可靠也不及时，最准的名单来自你自己的样本。
    这个脚本在游戏运行时跑一次，输出：
        · 游戏进程名 / 路径 / 命令行 / 签名 / 版本  → 用于填 rules 的 targetProcessPattern
        · 游戏进程已加载的模块（含签名状态）        → 用于发现异常模块
        · 本机现有进程里命中"外挂命名特征"的         → 用于人工确认误报

    它只读：不开进程内存、不注入、不 Hook、不截屏。

.EXAMPLE
    .\collect-game-info.ps1 -Pattern DeltaForce
#>
[CmdletBinding()]
param(
    [string]$Pattern = 'DeltaForce',
    [string]$OutFile,
    [int]$TopModules = 30
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'

if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($OutFile)) {
    $OutFile = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'game-info.json'
}

Write-Host "查找进程: *$Pattern*" -ForegroundColor Cyan

$procs = Get-Process | Where-Object {
    $_.ProcessName -like "*$Pattern*" -or
    ($_.Path -and $_.Path -like "*$Pattern*")
}

if (-not $procs) {
    Write-Warning "没找到匹配的进程。先启动游戏再跑，或用 -Pattern 换关键字（如 DeltaForceClient / Shipping）"
    return
}

$report = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]

foreach ($p in $procs) {
    $info = [ordered]@{
        name          = $p.ProcessName
        pid           = $p.Id
        path          = $null
        commandLine   = $null
        signature     = $null
        signer        = $null
        company       = $null
        version       = $null
        modules       = @()
    }

    try { $info.path = $p.Path } catch { }
    try { $info.company = $p.Company } catch { }
    try { $info.version = $p.ProductVersion } catch { }

    $cim = Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter "ProcessId = $($p.Id)" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($cim) { $info.commandLine = $cim.CommandLine }

    if ($info.path -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $info.path)) {
        try {
            $sig = Get-AuthenticodeSignature -LiteralPath $info.path
            $info.signature = $sig.Status
            if ($sig.SignerCertificate) { $info.signer = $sig.SignerCertificate.Subject }
        } catch { }
    }

    $mods = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
    try {
        foreach ($m in ($p.Modules | Select-Object -First 400)) {
            $modSig = $null
            try {
                if ($m.FileName -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $m.FileName)) {
                    $modSig = (Get-AuthenticodeSignature -LiteralPath $m.FileName).Status
                }
            } catch { }
            $mods.Add([ordered]@{ name = $m.ModuleName; path = $m.FileName; signature = $modSig })
        }
    } catch {
        Write-Warning "模块列表不可读（受保护进程）: $($p.ProcessName)"
    }
    $info.modules = $mods.ToArray()

    $report.Add([pscustomobject]$info)

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "== $($info.name) (PID $($info.pid)) ==" -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host "  路径   : $($info.path)"
    Write-Host "  命令行 : $($info.commandLine)"
    Write-Host "  签名   : $($info.signature)  $($info.signer)"
    Write-Host "  版本   : $($info.company) / $($info.version)"
    Write-Host "  模块   : $($mods.Count) 个"

    $unsigned = @($mods | Where-Object { $_.signature -and $_.signature -ne 'Valid' })
    if ($unsigned.Count -gt 0) {
        Write-Host "  未验签模块（前 10 个）:" -ForegroundColor Yellow
        $unsigned | Select-Object -First 10 | ForEach-Object { Write-Host "    - $($_.name)  [$($_.signature)]  $($_.path)" }
    }
}

# 反向：本机进程里像外挂的
Write-Host ""
Write-Host "== 本机进程里命中外挂命名特征的（可能是误报，用于调白名单）==" -ForegroundColor Cyan
$suspectKeywords = '辅助', '透视', '自瞄', '锁头', '穿墙', '方框', 'kmbox', 'aimbot', 'esp', 'cheat'
Get-Process | ForEach-Object {
    $n = $_.ProcessName
    foreach ($k in $suspectKeywords) {
        if ($n -like "*$k*") {
            $pathInfo = $null
            try { $pathInfo = $_.Path } catch { }
            Write-Host ("  {0,-28} {1}" -f $n, $pathInfo) -ForegroundColor Yellow
            break
        }
    }
}

$json = $report | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6
[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($OutFile, $json, (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)))
Write-Host ""
Write-Host "已写入: $OutFile"
Write-Host ""
Write-Host "下一步：" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "  1. 把上面打印的进程名填进 rules\rules.delta-force.json 的 whitelist（确认是游戏本体/官方组件）"
Write-Host "  2. 把异常模块名加进 df.module.* 规则"
Write-Host "  3. 把确认的外挂样本放进 samples\known-bad\delta-force\，跑 tools\hashgen.ps1 生成哈希包"
```

`tools/hashgen.ps1`:

```ps1
<#
.SYNOPSIS
    hashgen.ps1 —— 从样本目录生成 SHA256 哈希规则包（rules.hash.json）

.DESCRIPTION
    扫描指定目录里的样本文件，计算 SHA256，输出成规则文件片段，可直接放进规则目录由
    --rules-dir 热加载；也可以合并进已有的哈希包（-Merge）。

    产物只包含 meta + rules 两段，按 id 合并进主规则集，不影响其它规则。

.PARAMETER SampleDir
    样本目录（只读遍历，不修改任何文件）

.PARAMETER OutFile
    输出路径，默认 <脚本目录>\rules.hash.json

.PARAMETER Merge
    已存在同名输出文件时按 hash 合并，不重复添加

.EXAMPLE
    .\hashgen.ps1 -SampleDir D:\samples\cheats -OutFile E:\查挂小程序\rules\rules.hash.json

.EXAMPLE
    .\hashgen.ps1 -SampleDir D:\new-samples -Merge -Severity 5 -Confidence 0.95
#>
[CmdletBinding()]
param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)][string]$SampleDir,
    [string]$OutFile,
    [string[]]$Extensions = @('.exe', '.dll', '.sys', '.bat', '.ps1'),
    [int]$Severity = 5,
    [double]$Confidence = 0.95,
    [string]$IdPrefix = 'hash.known',
    [switch]$Merge,
    [long]$MaxSizeMB = 512
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $SampleDir)) { throw "样本目录不存在: $SampleDir" }

if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($OutFile)) {
    $OutFile = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'rules.hash.json'
}

$OutFile = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($OutFile)
$maxBytes = $MaxSizeMB * 1MB

Write-Host "样本目录: $SampleDir"
Write-Host "输出文件: $OutFile"

$existing = @{}
if ($Merge -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $OutFile)) {
    try {
        $old = Get-Content -LiteralPath $OutFile -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
        foreach ($r in $old.rules) {
            if ($r.hash) { $existing[$r.hash.ToLower()] = $r }
        }
        Write-Host "已载入既有规则 $($existing.Count) 条（Merge 模式）"
    }
    catch {
        Write-Warning "既有规则文件解析失败，按新建处理: $($_.Exception.Message)"
    }
}

$rules = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
$skipped = 0
$added = 0

Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SampleDir -Recurse -File | ForEach-Object {
    if ($Extensions -notcontains $_.Extension.ToLower()) { return }
    if ($_.Length -gt $maxBytes) { $skipped++; return }

    $hash = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLower()
    if ($existing.ContainsKey($hash)) { return }

    $shortId = $hash.Substring(0, 12)
    $rules.Add([ordered]@{
        id          = "$IdPrefix.$shortId"
        type        = 'file_hash'
        pattern     = $hash
        hash        = $hash
        size        = $_.Length
        category    = 'file.suspect'
        severity    = $Severity
        confidence  = $Confidence
        enabled     = $true
        description = "已知样本: $($_.Name)（SHA256=$hash）"
        advice      = "命中的是完整文件哈希，仍建议人工确认文件来源与用途"
    })
    $added++
}

# 保留既有条目
foreach ($k in $existing.Keys) { $rules.Add($existing[$k]) }

$payload = [ordered]@{
    meta  = [ordered]@{
        name          = 'hash-pack'
        version       = (Get-Date -Format 'yyyy.MM.dd')
        updated       = (Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd')
        schemaVersion = '2.0'
        description   = "由 hashgen.ps1 生成的哈希规则包（$($rules.Count) 条）"
    }
    rules = @($rules | Sort-Object { $_.id })
}

$dir = Split-Path -Parent $OutFile
if ($dir -and -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dir -Force | Out-Null }

$json = $payload | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8
[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($OutFile, $json, (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)))

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "完成：新增 $added 条，跳过（超大）$skipped 个，总计 $($rules.Count) 条"
Write-Host "用法: EagleEye.exe scan --rules-dir <规则目录>"
```

`tools/intel-to-rules.ps1`:

```ps1
<#
.SYNOPSIS
    intel-to-rules.ps1 —— 把人工整理的情报（关键词 / 网页文本）转成可用规则

.DESCRIPTION
    卡密平台、论坛曝光帖、客服工单里拿到的东西，本质是"字符串"。
    这个脚本把字符串批量转成 process_name / module_name / file_name 三类规则，
    并自动去重、标注置信度、生成复核清单。

    三种输入模式：
        list    —— 每行一个关键词（最常用）
        csv     —— 结构化：type,pattern,severity,confidence,description
        extract —— 给一段网页/聊天文本，自动抽取候选词（只出候选，不直接生成规则）

    输出：
        rules\rules.intel.json   —— 规则包，可 --rules-dir 直接加载
        <Input>\intel-review.md  —— 复核清单（哪些词会变成规则、预期误报面）

.EXAMPLE
    .\intel-to-rules.ps1 -InputFile rules\intel\delta-force-candidates.txt -Mode list

.EXAMPLE
    .\intel-to-rules.ps1 -InputFile page.txt -Mode extract -OutCandidates rules\intel\new-candidates.txt
#>
[CmdletBinding()]
param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)][string]$InputFile,
    [string]$OutFile,
    [ValidateSet('list', 'csv', 'extract')][string]$Mode = 'list',
    [string]$OutCandidates,
    [int]$Severity = 3,
    [double]$Confidence = 0.4,
    [string]$Category = 'intel',
    [string]$IdPrefix = 'intel',
    [int]$MinTokenLength = 2,
    [string]$RulesFile
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$root = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot

if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($OutFile))   { $OutFile   = Join-Path $root 'rules\rules.intel.json' }
if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($RulesFile)) { $RulesFile = Join-Path $root 'src\EagleEye\rules\rules.json' }

if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $InputFile)) { throw "输入文件不存在: $InputFile" }

$text = Get-Content -LiteralPath $InputFile -Raw -Encoding UTF8

# ---------- extract 模式：只抽候选，不生成规则 ----------
if ($Mode -eq 'extract') {
    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($OutCandidates)) {
        $OutCandidates = Join-Path (Split-Path -Parent $InputFile) 'new-candidates.txt'
    }

    $stop = @('the','and','for','with','this','that','from','http','https','www','com','net','org',
              '支持','我们','可以','如何','什么','这个','那个','因为','所以','但是','如果','已经',
              '三角洲行动','游戏','下载','官网','版本','更新','用户','登录','注册')

    $tokens = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]

    foreach ($m in [regex]::Matches($text, '[\u4e00-\u9fa5]{2,8}')) {
        $w = $m.Value
        if ($stop -contains $w) { continue }
        if ($w.Length -lt $MinTokenLength) { continue }
        if (-not $tokens.Contains($w)) { $tokens.Add($w) }
    }
    foreach ($m in [regex]::Matches($text, '[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_\-]{2,20}')) {
        $w = $m.Value
        if ($stop -contains $w.ToLower()) { continue }
        if (-not $tokens.Contains($w)) { $tokens.Add($w) }
    }

    [System.IO.File]::WriteAllLines($OutCandidates, $tokens, (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)))
    Write-Host "抽出候选词 $($tokens.Count) 个 → $OutCandidates"
    Write-Host "人工筛掉通用词后，用 -Mode list -InputFile $OutCandidates 生成规则"
    return
}

# ---------- 读取输入 ----------
$entries = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]

if ($Mode -eq 'csv') {
    $rows = $text -split "`r?`n" | Where-Object { $_.Trim() -ne '' -and -not $_.StartsWith('#') }
    $header = $rows[0] -split ','
    for ($i = 1; $i -lt $rows.Count; $i++) {
        $cells = $rows[$i] -split ','
        if ($cells.Count -lt 2) { continue }
        $entries.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Type        = $cells[0].Trim()
            Pattern     = $cells[1].Trim()
            Severity    = if ($cells.Count -gt 2 -and $cells[2]) { [int]$cells[2] } else { $Severity }
            Confidence  = if ($cells.Count -gt 3 -and $cells[3]) { [double]$cells[3] } else { $Confidence }
            Description = if ($cells.Count -gt 4) { $cells[4].Trim() } else { '' }
        })
    }
}
else {
    foreach ($line in ($text -split "`r?`n")) {
        $w = $line.Trim()
        if ($w -eq '' -or $w.StartsWith('#')) { continue }
        $entries.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Type        = 'auto'
            Pattern     = $w
            Severity    = $Severity
            Confidence  = $Confidence
            Description = "情报导入：$w"
        })
    }
}

if ($entries.Count -eq 0) { throw "输入里没有有效条目: $InputFile" }

# ---------- 已有规则 id（用于去重）----------
$existingIds = @{}
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $RulesFile) {
    $doc = Get-Content -LiteralPath $RulesFile -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    foreach ($r in @($doc.rules)) { $existingIds[$r.id] = $true }
}

# ---------- 生成规则 ----------
$rules = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
$review = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
$added = 0
$skipped = 0

function New-Id([string]$prefix, [string]$type, [string]$pattern) {
    $bytes = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetBytes("$type|$pattern")
    $sha = [System.Security.Cryptography.SHA256]::Create()
    $hash = $sha.ComputeHash($bytes)
    $hex = -join ($hash[0..3] | ForEach-Object { $_.ToString('x2') })
    return "$prefix.$type.$hex"
}

function Add-Rule([string]$type, [string]$pattern, [int]$sev, [double]$conf, [string]$desc) {
    $id = New-Id $IdPrefix $type $pattern
    if ($existingIds.ContainsKey($id)) { $script:skipped++; return }

    $glob = if ($pattern -match '[\*\?]') { $pattern } else { "*$pattern*" }

    $rules.Add([ordered]@{
        id          = $id
        type        = $type
        pattern     = $glob
        match       = 'glob'
        category    = $Category
        severity    = $sev
        confidence  = $conf
        description = $desc
        enabled     = $true
    })

    $existingIds[$id] = $true
    $script:added++
    $review.Add([pscustomobject]@{ 规则ID = $id; 类型 = $type; 模式 = $glob; 等级 = $sev; 置信度 = $conf })
}

foreach ($e in $entries) {
    $p = $e.Pattern.Trim()
    if ($p -eq '') { continue }

    switch ($e.Type.ToLower()) {
        'process' { Add-Rule 'process_name' $p $e.Severity $e.Confidence $e.Description }
        'module'  { Add-Rule 'module_name'  $p $e.Severity $e.Confidence $e.Description }
        'file'    { Add-Rule 'file_name'    $p $e.Severity $e.Confidence $e.Description }
        default {
            Add-Rule 'process_name' $p $e.Severity $e.Confidence $e.Description
            Add-Rule 'module_name'  $p $e.Severity $e.Confidence $e.Description
            Add-Rule 'file_name'    $p $e.Severity $e.Confidence $e.Description
        }
    }
}

# ---------- 输出 ----------
$ruleArray = $rules.ToArray()

$pack = [ordered]@{
    meta = [ordered]@{
        name          = 'intel-pack'
        version       = (Get-Date -Format 'yyyy.MM.dd.HHmm')
        updated       = (Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd')
        schemaVersion = '2.0'
        description   = "由 intel-to-rules.ps1 从 $([System.IO.Path]::GetFileName($InputFile)) 生成（$Mode 模式）"
    }
    rules = $ruleArray
}

$json = $pack | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10
[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($OutFile, $json, (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)))

$reviewPath = Join-Path (Split-Path -Parent $InputFile) 'intel-review.md'
$md = New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder
[void]$md.AppendLine('# 情报转规则 复核清单')
[void]$md.AppendLine('')
[void]$md.AppendLine("- 输入: $InputFile")
[void]$md.AppendLine("- 模式: $Mode")
[void]$md.AppendLine("- 生成: $added 条")
[void]$md.AppendLine('')
[void]$md.AppendLine('| 规则ID | 类型 | 模式 | 等级 | 置信度 |')
[void]$md.AppendLine('|---|---|---|---|---|')
foreach ($r in $review) {
    [void]$md.AppendLine("| $($r.规则ID) | $($r.类型) | ``$($r.模式)`` | $($r.等级) | $($r.置信度) |")
}
[void]$md.AppendLine('')
[void]$md.AppendLine('## 复核要点')
[void]$md.AppendLine('')
[void]$md.AppendLine('1. 这些是**命名类弱信号**，误报率高，severity 默认只给 3')
[void]$md.AppendLine('2. 越短的关键词误报越大（如 `辅助`），建议连品牌名一起写（如 `XX辅助`）')
[void]$md.AppendLine('3. 确认无误后把 `rules.intel.json` 放进规则目录，`--rules-dir` 会自动合并')
[void]$md.AppendLine('4. 命中后跑 `tune-thresholds.ps1` 复测误报')
[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($reviewPath, $md.ToString(), (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)))

Write-Host ""
$review | Format-Table -AutoSize
Write-Host "规则包: $OutFile（新增 $added 条，跳过重复 $skipped 条）"
Write-Host "复核清单: $reviewPath"
```

`tools/package.ps1`:

```ps1
<#
.SYNOPSIS
    package.ps1 —— 打包 Eagle Eye 可分发版本
.DESCRIPTION
    1) 发布自包含单文件（目标机无需 .NET）
    2) 组装 dist：exe + 全部规则 + 工具脚本 + 文档
    3) 打 zip（可直接作为 GitHub Release 附件）
.EXAMPLE
    .\package.ps1
    .\package.ps1 -SkipPublish
#>
[CmdletBinding()]
param(
    [string]$Version = '1.0.0',
    [switch]$SkipPublish,
    [switch]$NoZip
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$repo = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot
$proj = Join-Path $repo 'src\EagleEye\EagleEye.csproj'
$publishDir = Join-Path $repo 'out\publish-portable'
$distName = "EagleEye-v$Version-win-x64"
$dist = Join-Path $repo "dist\$distName"

Write-Host "== 打包 $distName ==" -ForegroundColor Cyan

if (-not $SkipPublish) {
    $dotnet = if (Test-Path 'E:\dotnet8\dotnet.exe') { 'E:\dotnet8\dotnet.exe' } else { 'dotnet' }
    Write-Host "发布中（$dotnet）..."
    & $dotnet publish $proj -c Release -r win-x64 --self-contained true `
        -p:PublishSingleFile=true -p:Version=$Version -o $publishDir -v quiet
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "发布失败" }
}

$exeSrc = Join-Path $publishDir 'EagleEye.exe'
if (-not (Test-Path $exeSrc)) { throw "找不到发布产物: $publishDir" }

if (Test-Path $dist) { Remove-Item $dist -Recurse -Force }
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dist -Force | Out-Null

Copy-Item $exeSrc (Join-Path $dist 'EagleEye.exe') -Force
# 规则：直接从仓库组装（不依赖 publish 的复制行为，避免空目录）
$distRules = Join-Path $dist 'rules'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $distRules -Force | Out-Null

$ruleSources = @(
    (Join-Path $repo 'rules\rules.json'),
    (Join-Path $repo 'rules\rules.delta-force.json'),
    (Join-Path $repo 'rules\rules.intel.json')
)

foreach ($src in $ruleSources) {
    if (Test-Path $src) {
        Copy-Item $src (Join-Path $distRules (Split-Path $src -Leaf)) -Force
    } else {
        Write-Warning "缺少规则文件: $src"
    }
}

$ruleCount = (Get-ChildItem $distRules -Filter *.json).Count
if ($ruleCount -lt 2) { throw "规则组装不完整（只有 $ruleCount 个文件）" }
Write-Host "规则已组装: $ruleCount 个文件"

$toolsDir = Join-Path $dist 'tools'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $toolsDir -Force | Out-Null
foreach ($f in @('audit-policy.ps1', 'hashgen.ps1', 'intel-to-rules.ps1', 'tune-thresholds.ps1', 'collect-game-info.ps1', 'package.ps1')) {
    $src = Join-Path $repo "tools\$f"
    if (Test-Path $src) { Copy-Item $src (Join-Path $toolsDir $f) -Force }
}

# 根目录放 README / LICENSE，docs 放详细文档
foreach ($f in @('README.md', 'README.en.md', 'LICENSE', 'CHANGELOG.md')) {
    $src = Join-Path $repo $f
    if (Test-Path $src) { Copy-Item $src (Join-Path $dist $f) -Force }
}

$docsDir = Join-Path $dist 'docs'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $docsDir -Force | Out-Null
$quick = Join-Path $repo 'docs\快速上手.txt'
if (Test-Path $quick) { Copy-Item $quick (Join-Path $docsDir '快速上手.txt') -Force }

$srcReadme = Join-Path $repo 'src\EagleEye\README.md'
if (Test-Path $srcReadme) { Copy-Item $srcReadme (Join-Path $docsDir '技术文档.md') -Force }

Get-ChildItem (Join-Path $repo 'docs') -Filter *.md -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
    ForEach-Object { Copy-Item $_.FullName (Join-Path $docsDir $_.Name) -Force }

Write-Host "校验打包结果..."
& (Join-Path $dist 'EagleEye.exe') validate 2>&1 | Select-String -Pattern '规则数量' | ForEach-Object { Write-Host $_.Line }

if (-not $NoZip) {
    $zip = Join-Path $repo "dist\$distName.zip"
    if (Test-Path $zip) { Remove-Item $zip -Force }

    # 刚校验过的 exe 可能还被短暂占用，重试打包
    $ok = $false
    for ($i = 1; $i -le 5; $i++) {
        try {
            Compress-Archive -Path (Join-Path $dist '*') -DestinationPath $zip -CompressionLevel Optimal -ErrorAction Stop
            $ok = $true
            break
        }
        catch {
            Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 800
        }
    }

    if ($ok) {
        Write-Host "已打包: $zip（$([math]::Round((Get-Item $zip).Length/1MB,1)) MB）"
    } else {
        Write-Warning "打包失败（文件被占用），稍后重跑：.\tools\package.ps1 -SkipPublish"
    }
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "分发目录: $dist" -ForegroundColor Green
Get-ChildItem $dist | ForEach-Object {
    if ($_.PSIsContainer) { Write-Host ("  {0,-24} <DIR>" -f $_.Name) }
    else { Write-Host ("  {0,-24} {1} MB" -f $_.Name, [math]::Round($_.Length/1MB,2)) }
}
```

`tools/tune-thresholds.ps1`:

```ps1
<#
.SYNOPSIS
    tune-thresholds.ps1 —— 基于真实扫描报告做误报分析，产出可用的调参包

.DESCRIPTION
    读取一批扫描报告（JSON），按文件名前缀区分标签：
        clean-*.json  → 干净机器基线
        cheat-*.json  → 已确认作弊机器（可选；先有 clean 也能跑）

    输出：
        1) rules\tuning.suggested.json  —— 可被 --rules-dir 直接加载的覆盖包
        2) <ReportsDir>\tuning-report.md —— 人读的分析报告

    判定策略：
        · 只在干净机器命中、作弊样本 0 次 → 噪音
            - 命中面窄（≤ -WhitelistThreshold 个不同目标）→ 只加白，不动规则
            - 命中面宽且属受保护类型（句柄/驱动/哈希/特征码）→ 降权，不停用
            - 命中面宽且普通类型 → 停用（干净报告 ≥ -DisableAt）或降权
        · 干净/作弊都命中 → 规则保留；只把"干净出现、作弊从未出现"的目标加白
        · 规则集里不存在的 id（测试规则）→ 忽略

.PARAMETER ReportsDir
    报告目录，默认 <仓库>\out\reports

.EXAMPLE
    .\tune-thresholds.ps1 -ReportsDir ..\out\baseline -Label clean
#>
[CmdletBinding()]
param(
    [string]$ReportsDir,
    [string]$RulesFile,
    [string]$OutFile,
    [string]$Label = 'clean',
    [int]$DisableAt = 3,
    [double]$ConfidenceFactor = 0.6,
    [int]$MinRuleCount = 10,
    [int]$WhitelistThreshold = 20,
    [string[]]$ProtectTypes = @('handle_access', 'kernel_driver', 'file_hash', 'file_signature'),
    [switch]$NoWhitelist
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$root = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot

if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($ReportsDir)) { $ReportsDir = Join-Path $root 'out\reports' }
if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($RulesFile))  { $RulesFile  = Join-Path $root 'src\EagleEye\rules\rules.json' }
if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($OutFile))    { $OutFile    = Join-Path $root 'rules\tuning.suggested.json' }

$reports = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $ReportsDir -Filter *.json -File |
           Where-Object { $_.Name -notmatch 'tuning' } |
           Sort-Object Name

if ($reports.Count -eq 0) { throw "报告目录里没有 json: $ReportsDir" }

Write-Host "分析报告: $($reports.Count) 份（目录 $ReportsDir）"

$stats = @{}
$summary = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]

foreach ($file in $reports) {
    try {
        $r = Get-Content -LiteralPath $file.FullName -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    }
    catch {
        Write-Warning "跳过无法解析的报告: $($file.Name)"
        continue
    }

    $ruleCount = 0
    if ($r.ruleset -and $r.ruleset.ruleCount) { $ruleCount = [int]$r.ruleset.ruleCount }
    if ($ruleCount -lt $MinRuleCount) { continue }   # 跳过验证用的小规则包

    $isCheat = $file.Name -like 'cheat-*'
    # 注意：不能叫 $label —— PowerShell 变量名大小写不敏感，会覆盖参数 $Label
    $reportLabel = if ($isCheat) { 'cheat' } else { $Label }

    $summary.Add([pscustomobject]@{
        File  = $file.Name
        Label = $reportLabel
        Risk  = $r.summary.riskLevel
        Hits  = @($r.findings).Count
    })

    foreach ($f in @($r.findings)) {
        if (-not $f.ruleId) { continue }
        if (-not $stats.ContainsKey($f.ruleId)) {
            $stats[$f.ruleId] = [pscustomobject]@{
                RuleId = $f.ruleId
                Clean  = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
                Cheat  = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
                Scores = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[double]
                Types  = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
            }
        }

        $entry = $stats[$f.ruleId]
        $targetName = if ($f.target) { [string]$f.target } else { '(unknown)' }
        if ($isCheat) { $entry.Cheat.Add($targetName) } else { $entry.Clean.Add($targetName) }
        $entry.Scores.Add([double]$f.score)
        if ($f.ruleType -and -not $entry.Types.Contains($f.ruleType)) { $entry.Types.Add($f.ruleType) }
    }
}

# 主规则集里的全部规则定义（按 id 索引）
$rulesDoc = Get-Content -LiteralPath $RulesFile -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
$ruleById = @{}
foreach ($rule in @($rulesDoc.rules)) { $ruleById[$rule.id] = $rule }
foreach ($rule in @($rulesDoc.dma.rules)) { $ruleById[$rule.id] = $rule }
foreach ($rule in @($rulesDoc.vision.capture.moduleRules)) { if ($rule.id) { $ruleById[$rule.id] = $rule } }
foreach ($rule in @($rulesDoc.vision.hardware.rules)) { if ($rule.id) { $ruleById[$rule.id] = $rule } }
foreach ($rule in @($rulesDoc.deep.handles.rules)) { if ($rule.id) { $ruleById[$rule.id] = $rule } }
foreach ($rule in @($rulesDoc.deep.drivers.rules)) { if ($rule.id) { $ruleById[$rule.id] = $rule } }

$cleanReports = @($summary | Where-Object { $_.Label -ne 'cheat' }).Count
$cheatReports = @($summary | Where-Object { $_.Label -eq 'cheat' }).Count

$rows = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
$tuningRules = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
$whitelistProcesses = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]

foreach ($id in $stats.Keys) {
    $s = $stats[$id]
    $cleanTargets = @($s.Clean | Sort-Object -Unique)
    $cheatTargets = @($s.Cheat | Sort-Object -Unique)
    $cleanCount = $cleanTargets.Count
    $cheatCount = $cheatTargets.Count

    $isRealRule = $ruleById.ContainsKey($id)
    $noise = ($isRealRule -and $cleanCount -gt 0 -and $cheatCount -eq 0)
    $protected = ($s.Types | Where-Object { $ProtectTypes -contains $_ }).Count -gt 0

    $action = '保留'
    if ($noise) {
        if ($cleanCount -le $WhitelistThreshold) { $action = '仅加白' }
        elseif ($protected) { $action = '建议降权（受保护类型）' }
        elseif ($cleanCount -ge $DisableAt) { $action = '建议停用' }
        else { $action = '建议降权' }
    }

    $avgScore = 0
    if ($s.Scores.Count -gt 0) { $avgScore = [math]::Round(($s.Scores | Measure-Object -Average).Average, 1) }

    $rows.Add([pscustomobject]@{
        规则     = $id
        类型     = ($s.Types -join '/')
        干净命中 = $cleanCount
        作弊命中 = $cheatCount
        平均分   = $avgScore
        判定     = $action
    })

    # 需要动规则的：复制完整定义后调整
    if ($noise -and $action -ne '仅加白') {
        $base = $ruleById[$id]
        $copy = [ordered]@{}
        foreach ($prop in $base.PSObject.Properties) { $copy[$prop.Name] = $prop.Value }

        if (-not $copy.Contains('enabled')) { $copy['enabled'] = $true }
        if ($action -eq '建议停用') { $copy['enabled'] = $false }

        if ($copy.Contains('confidence')) {
            $copy['confidence'] = [math]::Round([double]$copy['confidence'] * $ConfidenceFactor, 3)
        }

        $oldDesc = if ($copy.Contains('description')) { $copy['description'] } else { '' }
        $copy['description'] = "调参：干净基线命中 $cleanCount 次、作弊样本 0 次 —— $oldDesc"
        $tuningRules.Add([pscustomobject]$copy)
    }

    # 目标级精准加白：干净出现过、作弊从未出现
    if ($isRealRule -and -not $NoWhitelist -and $cleanCount -gt 0) {
        $cleanOnly = @($cleanTargets | Where-Object { $cheatTargets -notcontains $_ })

        if ($cleanOnly.Count -le 50) {
            foreach ($targetName in $cleanOnly) {
                if ($targetName -match '^pid[0-9]+$') { continue }
                if ($targetName -notmatch '\.(exe|dll|sys)$') { continue }

                $whitelistProcesses.Add([ordered]@{
                    pattern       = $targetName
                    match         = 'exact'
                    reason        = "调参基线：$id 在干净机器命中、作弊样本从未出现"
                    suppressRules = @($id)
                })
            }
        }
    }
}

$ruleArray = $tuningRules.ToArray()
$whitelistArray = $whitelistProcesses.ToArray()

$pack = [ordered]@{
    meta = [ordered]@{
        name          = 'tuning-suggested'
        version       = (Get-Date -Format 'yyyy.MM.dd.HHmm')
        updated       = (Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd')
        schemaVersion = '2.0'
        description   = "由 tune-thresholds.ps1 生成：基于 $cleanReports 份干净报告 / $cheatReports 份作弊报告"
    }
    rules     = $ruleArray
    whitelist = [ordered]@{
        processes = $whitelistArray
        paths     = [object[]]@()
        files     = [object[]]@()
        signers   = [object[]]@()
    }
}

$json = $pack | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 12
[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($OutFile, $json, (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)))

Write-Host ""
$rows | Sort-Object -Property 干净命中 -Descending | Format-Table -AutoSize

$reportPath = Join-Path $ReportsDir 'tuning-report.md'
$md = New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder
[void]$md.AppendLine('# 调参分析报告')
[void]$md.AppendLine('')
[void]$md.AppendLine("- 干净报告: $cleanReports 份")
[void]$md.AppendLine("- 作弊报告: $cheatReports 份")
[void]$md.AppendLine("- 调参包: $OutFile")
[void]$md.AppendLine('')
[void]$md.AppendLine('| 规则 | 类型 | 干净命中 | 作弊命中 | 平均分 | 判定 |')
[void]$md.AppendLine('|---|---|---|---|---|---|')
foreach ($row in ($rows | Sort-Object -Property 干净命中 -Descending)) {
    [void]$md.AppendLine("| $($row.规则) | $($row.类型) | $($row.干净命中) | $($row.作弊命中) | $($row.平均分) | $($row.判定) |")
}
[void]$md.AppendLine('')
[void]$md.AppendLine('## 下一步')
[void]$md.AppendLine('')
[void]$md.AppendLine('1. 把调参包放进规则目录，用 `--rules-dir` 重扫')
[void]$md.AppendLine('2. 对比 `summary.findings` 与 `summary.suppressedFindings`，确认噪音下降且真信号还在')
[void]$md.AppendLine('3. 补充 `cheat-*.json` 样本后再跑一次，防止把真信号降权掉')
[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($reportPath, $md.ToString(), (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)))

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "调参包: $OutFile（规则 $($tuningRules.Count) 条，白名单 $($whitelistProcesses.Count) 条）"
Write-Host "分析报告: $reportPath"
```
